📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期狂犬病病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在 231 个脑组织样本中,有 20 个(8.7%)检测到 狂犬病病毒 抗原。经过质量控制,保留了 868 种血清和 300 种脑脊液化合物。主成分分析和偏最小二乘判别分析都没有将任一矩阵中的组分开。错误发现率 (FDR) 校正后,四种血清特征仍然显着:DFA 阳性犬中分析物 634、甘油酸和分析物 706 较低,苯甲酸较高。分析物 634 给出了最高的曲线下发现集面积 (AUC = 0.9575)。在 CSF 中,经过 FDR 校正后,DFA 阳性狗的一项未注释特征(分析物 369)较低。血清分析物 634 和脑脊液分析物 369 具有相同的保留时间和质谱图,并且可能是相同的化合物。
  • 鼻平面和滤泡窦复杂样本的一致性能支持使用更安全、更容易获得的死后标本,增强了可扩展、现场适应的分子诊断的潜力,以加强流行地区的常规动物 狂犬病 监测。 [10.1371/journal.pone.0362890]。
  • 十八个样本测试呈阳性,其中十六个产生的序列质量足以进行系统发育分析。 [10.1016/j.actatropica.2026.108409]。
  • ROC 曲线分析显示,犬血清的最佳截止值为 42.5%,猫血清的最佳截止值为 54.5%。该试剂盒的临床一致性非常好,犬血清的总体一致性百分比 (OPA) 为 95.67%,猫血清的总体一致性百分比 (OPA) 为 97.17%。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Rabies virus antigen was detected in 20 of 231 brain tissue samples (8.7%). After quality control, 868 serum and 300 CSF compounds were retained.
  • The consistent performance in nasal planum and follicle-sinus complex samples supports using safer, more accessible post-mortem specimens, reinforcing the potential for scalable, field-adapted molecular diagnostics to strengthen routine animal rabies surveillance in endemic regions.
  • Eighteen samples tested positive, of which sixteen yielded sequences of sufficient quality for phylogenetic analysis. [10.1016/j.actatropica.2026.108409].
  • ROC curve analysis revealed optimal cutoff values of 42.5% for canine serum and 54.5% for feline serum.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Metabolomics : Official journal of the Metabolomic Society  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-13  |  DOI: 10.1007/s11306-026-02554-x  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 22, 6, 180
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

尼日利亚表面健康的 狂犬病病毒 阳性狗的非靶向血清和脑脊液代谢组学。

Untargeted serum and cerebrospinal fluid metabolomics of apparently healthy rabies virus-positive dogs in Nigeria.
作者： Ukamaka U. Eze, Monique Opperman, Kenneth Ikejiofor Ogbu, Elmarie Davoren, C. E. Ngoepe, Romanus Chukwuduruo Ezeokonkwo, Boniface Maduka Anene, L. E. Heath, Ilse du Preez, Claude Taurai Sabeta
摘要中文翻译简介:狂犬病 仍然是一种对全球公共卫生具有重要意义的被忽视的人畜共患疾病,因为它几乎导致普遍死亡,可靠的生前诊断选择有限,并且在临床症状出现后缺乏有效的 治疗。然而,狂犬病 阳性但未表现出可观察到的临床症状的狗的代谢变化仍不清楚。因此,本研究旨在鉴定临床上健康的直接荧光抗体 (DFA) 阳性狗与 DFA 阴性狗中代谢物的丰度差异。方法:从 231 只表面上临床健康的狗身上采集包含脑组织、血清和脑脊液 (CSF) 的样本。使用直接荧光抗体(DFA)测试在脑组织中检测到狂犬病病毒抗原,并根据结果将狗分为狂犬病阳性或阴性。使用经过验证的非靶向代谢组学样品制备程序从血清和脑脊液中提取代谢物。在优化的分析条件下,使用综合二维气相色谱结合飞行时间质谱 (GC×GC-TOFMS) 进行代谢组学分析。结果:231 份脑组织样本中有 20 份检测到 狂犬病病毒 抗原(8.7%)。相应的血清和脑脊液样本被归类为狂犬病(DFA)阳性,同时选择20个阴性样本进行比较。经过质量控制,保留了 868 种血清和 300 种脑脊液化合物。主成分分析和偏最小二乘判别分析都没有将任一矩阵中的组分开。错误发现率 (FDR) 校正后,四种血清特征仍然显着:DFA 阳性犬中分析物 634、甘油酸和分析物 706 较低,苯甲酸较高。分析物 634 给出了最高的曲线下发现集面积 (AUC = 0.9575)。在 CSF 中,经过 FDR 校正后,DFA 阳性狗的一项未注释特征(分析物 369)较低。血清分析物 634 和脑脊液分析物 369 具有相同的保留时间和质谱图,并且可能是相同的化合物。具有最低富集 p 值的血清通路主要由单独的甘油绘制,并且在 FDR 校正后没有 CSF 通路仍然显着。在两种基质中,DFA 阳性狗的吲哚乳酸均较高,仅达到标称水平。结论:FDR 校正后,表面健康的 DFA 阳性和 DFA 阴性犬之间的 4 项血清特征和 1 项脑脊液特征存在差异。这些是发现阶段的候选特征,需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行结构确认、有针对性的量化和验证,然后才能做出任何诊断声明。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是一种对全球公共卫生具有重要意义的被忽视的人畜共患疾病,因为它几乎导致普遍死亡,可靠的生前诊断选择有限,并且在临床症状出现后缺乏有效的 治疗。然而,狂犬病 阳性但未表现出可观察到的临床症状的狗的代谢变化仍不清楚。
设计与对象
从 231 只表面上临床健康的狗身上采集了包含脑组织、血清和脑脊液 (CSF) 的样本。相应的血清和脑脊液样本被归类为狂犬病(DFA)阳性,同时选择20个阴性样本进行比较。
核心方法
使用直接荧光抗体(DFA)测试在脑组织中检测到狂犬病病毒抗原,并根据结果将狗分为狂犬病阳性或阴性。使用经过验证的非靶向代谢组学样品制备程序从血清和脑脊液中提取代谢物。在优化的分析条件下,使用综合二维气相色谱结合飞行时间质谱 (GC×GC-TOFMS) 进行代谢组学分析。
主要结果
在 231 个脑组织样本中,有 20 个(8.7%)检测到 狂犬病病毒 抗原。经过质量控制,保留了 868 种血清和 300 种脑脊液化合物。主成分分析和偏最小二乘判别分析都没有将任一矩阵中的组分开。错误发现率 (FDR) 校正后,四种血清特征仍然显着:DFA 阳性犬中分析物 634、甘油酸和分析物 706 较低,苯甲酸较高。分析物 634 给出了最高的曲线下发现集面积 (AUC = 0.9575)。在 CSF 中,经过 FDR 校正后,DFA 阳性狗的一项未注释特征(分析物 369)较低。血清分析物 634 和脑脊液分析物 369 具有相同的保留时间和质谱图,并且可能是相同的化合物。
解释与创新
FDR 校正后,表面健康的 DFA 阳性狗和 DFA 阴性狗之间的四种血清特征和一种脑脊液特征存在差异。这些是发现阶段的候选特征,需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行结构确认、有针对性的量化和验证,然后才能做出任何诊断声明。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为狗 狂犬病病毒 感染的新型诊断标志物的开发提供信息,特别是在没有临床症状的情况下。他们还强调了代谢组学在识别与 狂犬病病毒 感染相关的微妙生化变化方面的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其发现阶段的性质而受到限制,因为已确定的特征需要进一步验证。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于非靶向代谢组学并且不提供明确的诊断声明。

Untargeted serum and cerebrospinal fluid metabolomics of apparently healthy rabies virus-positive dogs in Nigeria.

Authors: Ukamaka U. Eze, Monique Opperman, Kenneth Ikejiofor Ogbu, Elmarie Davoren, C. E. Ngoepe, Romanus Chukwuduruo Ezeokonkwo, Boniface Maduka Anene, L. E. Heath, Ilse du Preez, Claude Taurai Sabeta
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Rabies remains a neglected zoonotic disease of global public health importance because of its near-universal fatality, limited reliable antemortem diagnostic options, and absence of effective treatment following the onset of clinical signs. However, metabolic alterations in dogs that are rabies-positive but show no observable clinical signs remain poorly characterized. Therefore, this study aimed to identify differentially abundant metabolites in apparently clinically healthy, direct fluorescent antibody (DFA)-positive dogs compared with DFA-negative dogs. METHODOLOGY: Samples comprising brain tissue, serum, and cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) were collected from 231 apparently clinically healthy dogs. Rabies virus antigen was detected in brain tissue using the direct fluorescent antibody (DFA) test, and dogs were classified as rabies-positive or negative based on the results. Metabolites were extracted from serum and CSF using a validated sample preparation procedure for untargeted metabolomics. Metabolomic profiling was performed using comprehensive two-dimensional gas chromatography coupled with time-of-flight mass spectrometry (GC×GC-TOFMS) under optimized analytical conditions. RESULTS: Rabies virus antigen was detected in 20 of 231 brain tissue samples (8.7%). Corresponding serum and CSF samples were classified as rabies (DFA)-positive, while 20 negative samples were selected for comparison. After quality control, 868 serum and 300 CSF compounds were retained. Neither principal component analysis nor partial least squares discriminant analysis separated the groups in either matrix. Four serum features remained significant after false discovery rate (FDR) correction: Analyte 634, glyceric acid and Analyte 706 were lower, and benzoic acid was higher, in DFA-positive dogs. Analyte 634 gave the highest discovery-set area under the curve (AUC = 0.9575). In CSF, one unannotated feature (Analyte 369) was lower in DFA-positive dogs after FDR correction. Serum Analyte 634 and CSF Analyte 369 share retention times and mass spectra and may be the same compound. The serum pathways with the lowest enrichment p-values were mapped mainly by glyceric acid alone, and no CSF pathway remained significant after FDR correction. Indolelactic acid was higher in DFA-positive dogs in both matrices at the nominal level only. CONCLUSION: Four serum features and one CSF feature differed between apparently healthy DFA-positive and DFA-negative dogs after FDR correction. These are discovery-stage candidate features that require structural confirmation, targeted quantification and validation in an independent cohort before any diagnostic claim can be made.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies remains a neglected zoonotic disease of global public health importance because of its near-universal fatality, limited reliable antemortem diagnostic options, and absence of effective treatment following the onset of clinical signs. However, metabolic alterations in dogs that are rabies-positive but show no observable clinical signs remain poorly characterized.
Design and population
Samples comprising brain tissue, serum, and cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) were collected from 231 apparently clinically healthy dogs. Corresponding serum and CSF samples were classified as rabies (DFA)-positive, while 20 negative samples were selected for comparison.
Core methods
Rabies virus antigen was detected in brain tissue using the direct fluorescent antibody (DFA) test, and dogs were classified as rabies-positive or negative based on the results. Metabolites were extracted from serum and CSF using a validated sample preparation procedure for untargeted metabolomics. Metabolomic profiling was performed using comprehensive two-dimensional gas chromatography coupled with time-of-flight mass spectrometry (GC×GC-TOFMS) under optimized analytical conditions.
Main results
Rabies virus antigen was detected in 20 of 231 brain tissue samples (8.7%). After quality control, 868 serum and 300 CSF compounds were retained. Neither principal component analysis nor partial least squares discriminant analysis separated the groups in either matrix. Four serum features remained significant after false discovery rate (FDR) correction: Analyte 634, glyceric acid and Analyte 706 were lower, and benzoic acid was higher, in DFA-positive dogs. Analyte 634 gave the highest discovery-set area under the curve (AUC = 0.9575). In CSF, one unannotated feature (Analyte 369) was lower in DFA-positive dogs after FDR correction. Serum Analyte 634 and CSF Analyte 369 share retention times and mass spectra and may be the same compound.
Interpretation and novelty
Four serum features and one CSF feature differed between apparently healthy DFA-positive and DFA-negative dogs after FDR correction. These are discovery-stage candidate features that require structural confirmation, targeted quantification and validation in an independent cohort before any diagnostic claim can be made.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of novel diagnostic markers for rabies virus infection in dogs, particularly in the absence of clinical signs. They also highlight the potential of metabolomics in identifying subtle biochemical changes associated with rabies virus infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its discovery-stage nature, as the identified features require further validation. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on untargeted metabolomics and do not provide definitive diagnostic claims.

Journal: Veterinary research communications  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-10  |  DOI: 10.1007/s11259-026-11588-4  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 51, 1, 45
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

快速竞争性 ELISA,结合塞姆利基森林病毒表达的 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白,用于犬和猫 狂犬病 免疫的血清学 监测。

Rapid competitive ELISA incorporating Semliki Forest virus-expressed rabies virus glycoprotein for serological surveillance of canine and feline rabies immunity.
作者： Xiao-Hu Han, Shu-Yan Sun, Yu-Ling Tian, Lin-Feng Li, Xiao-Xuan Zhu, Jian-Qing Zhao, Yuan Zhang, Ming Zhou, Cheng-Guang Zhang, Ling Zhao
摘要中文翻译尽管狂犬病的病死率接近100%,但通过使用狂犬病诱导的中和抗体是完全可以预防的。因此,快速可靠地检测这些抗体对于有效控制疾病至关重要。使用塞姆利基森林病毒(SFV-RVG)表达的狂犬病病毒糖蛋白作为包被抗原,辣根过氧化物酶标记的7A3(HRP-7A3)作为检测抗体,我们开发了一种竞争性ELISA(cELISA),用于特异性检测狂犬病病毒糖蛋白(RVG)中和抗体。根据内部样本组的测试,接受者操作特征 (ROC) 曲线分析显示,犬血清的最佳截止值为 42.5%,猫血清的最佳截止值为 54.5%。该试剂盒的临床一致性非常好,犬血清的总体一致性百分比 (OPA) 为 95.67%,猫血清的总体一致性百分比 (OPA) 为 97.17%。总体而言,本文报道的 cELISA 为狗和猫的 狂犬病 免疫 监测 提供了一种有前景的工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病病毒 感染是一种致命性疾病,但可以通过 疫苗接种 诱导的中和抗体来预防。因此,快速可靠地检测这些抗体对于有效控制疾病至关重要。
设计与对象
开发了一种竞争性 ELISA (cELISA),用于特异性检测 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白中和抗体,使用塞姆利基森林病毒 (SFV-RVG) 表达的 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白作为包被抗原,辣根过氧化物酶标记的 7A3 (HRP-7A3) 作为检测抗体。该研究测试了犬科动物和猫科动物血清的内部样本组。
核心方法
使用 SFV-RVG 作为包被抗原和 HRP-7A3 作为检测抗体开发 cELISA。进行受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析以确定犬和猫血清中抗体检测的最佳截止值。
主要结果
ROC 曲线分析显示,犬血清的最佳截止值为 42.5%,猫血清的最佳截止值为 54.5%。该试剂盒的临床一致性非常好,犬血清的总体一致性百分比 (OPA) 为 95.67%,猫血清的总体一致性百分比 (OPA) 为 97.17%。
解释与创新
本文报道的 cELISA 为狗和猫的 狂犬病 免疫 监测 提供了一种有前途的工具,为检测中和抗体提供了快速可靠的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为狗和猫的 狂犬病 免疫血清学 监测 提供了一个有价值的工具,这可以帮助公共卫生努力监测和控制 狂犬病 狂犬病 狂犬病。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于内部样本小组,这可能会影响普遍性。证据强度中等,因为结果基于单一研究,没有外部验证。

Rapid competitive ELISA incorporating Semliki Forest virus-expressed rabies virus glycoprotein for serological surveillance of canine and feline rabies immunity.

Authors: Xiao-Hu Han, Shu-Yan Sun, Yu-Ling Tian, Lin-Feng Li, Xiao-Xuan Zhu, Jian-Qing Zhao, Yuan Zhang, Ming Zhou, Cheng-Guang Zhang, Ling Zhao
Original abstract (English)
Although the case fatality rate of rabies approaches 100%, it is entirely preventable through the use of vaccination-induced neutralizing antibodies. Rapid and reliable detection of these antibodies is therefore critical for effective disease control. Using a rabies virus glycoprotein expressed by Semliki Forest virus (SFV-RVG) as a coating antigen and horseradish peroxidase-labeled 7A3 (HRP-7A3) as the detection antibody, we developed a competitive ELISA (cELISA) for the specific detection of rabies virus glycoprotein (RVG)-neutralizing antibodies. On the basis of the testing of the in-house sample panel, receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis revealed optimal cutoff values of 42.5% for canine serum and 54.5% for feline serum. The clinical agreement of the kit was excellent, with an overall percent agreement (OPA) of 95.67% for canine serum and 97.17% for feline serum. Overall, the cELISA reported here provides a promising tool for rabies immune surveillance in dogs and cats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies virus infection is a fatal disease, but it can be prevented through vaccination-induced neutralizing antibodies. Rapid and reliable detection of these antibodies is therefore critical for effective disease control.
Design and population
A competitive ELISA (cELISA) was developed for the specific detection of rabies virus glycoprotein-neutralizing antibodies using a rabies virus glycoprotein expressed by Semliki Forest virus (SFV-RVG) as a coating antigen and horseradish peroxidase-labeled 7A3 (HRP-7A3) as the detection antibody. The study tested an in-house sample panel of canine and feline sera.
Core methods
The cELISA was developed using SFV-RVG as a coating antigen and HRP-7A3 as the detection antibody. Receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis was performed to determine optimal cutoff values for antibody detection in canine and feline sera.
Main results
ROC curve analysis revealed optimal cutoff values of 42.5% for canine serum and 54.5% for feline serum. The clinical agreement of the kit was excellent, with an overall percent agreement (OPA) of 95.67% for canine serum and 97.17% for feline serum.
Interpretation and novelty
The cELISA reported here provides a promising tool for rabies immune surveillance in dogs and cats, offering a rapid and reliable method for detecting neutralizing antibodies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a valuable tool for serological surveillance of rabies immunity in dogs and cats, which can aid in public health efforts to monitor and control rabies outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an in-house sample panel, which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on a single study with no external validation.

开发和验证用于检测死后非脑动物样本中 SEA4 进化枝 狂犬病病毒(菲律宾株)的实时 PCR 测定。

Development and validation of a real-time PCR assay for the detection of SEA4 clade rabies virus (Philippine strain) in post-mortem non-brain animal samples.
作者： Daria Llenaresas MANALO, Jude Karlo G. Bolivar, Jeromir G. Bondoc, Blanca Jarilla Nagataki, Karl Ian T. Ermino, Kazunori Kimitsuki, Leilanie B. Nacion, Mark Joseph M. Espino, Chun-Ho Park, Satoshi Inoue
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 仍然是流行病环境中的一个主要公共卫生问题,有效的控制取决于对动物 狂犬病 感染的及时、可靠的尸检确认。尽管直接荧光抗体测试 (dFAT) 仍然是参考标准,但其对新鲜脑组织的依赖和对样本降解的敏感性限制了其在分散式 监测 系统中的操作使用。目前的研究结果表明,针对非脑组织中核蛋白基因的局部优化 RT-qPCR 检测具有较高的分析灵敏度,并且与从狗死后脑样本中获得的 dFAT 结果具有很强的一致性。鼻平面和滤泡窦复杂样本的一致性能支持使用更安全、更容易获得的死后标本,增强了可扩展、现场适应的分子诊断的潜力,以加强流行地区的常规动物 狂犬病 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是流行病环境中的一个主要公共卫生问题,有效的控制取决于对动物 狂犬病 感染的及时、可靠的尸检确认。
设计与对象
目前的研究结果表明,针对非脑组织中核蛋白基因的局部优化 RT-qPCR 检测具有较高的分析灵敏度,并且与从狗死后脑样本中获得的 dFAT 结果具有很强的一致性。
核心方法
该研究涉及开发和验证一种实时 PCR 检测方法,用于检测非脑动物样本中的 SEA4 进化枝 狂犬病病毒,重点关注尸检样本,例如鼻平面和滤泡窦复合体。
主要结果
鼻平面和滤泡窦复杂样本的一致性能支持使用更安全、更容易获得的死后标本,增强了可扩展、现场适应的分子诊断的潜力,以加强流行地区的常规动物 狂犬病 监测。
解释与创新
该研究为非脑组织中狂犬病病毒检测提供了一种新方法,可能提高动物储存宿主宰后储存宿主的效率和可靠性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,新的 RT-qPCR 检测可以通过使用更容易获得且不易降解的非脑样本来增强 狂犬病 监测 在流行地区的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对特定进化枝和动物物种的关注而受到限制,但证据支持对该检测在非脑组织中靶向 狂犬病病毒 的性能有一定的信心。

Development and validation of a real-time PCR assay for the detection of SEA4 clade rabies virus (Philippine strain) in post-mortem non-brain animal samples.

Authors: Daria Llenaresas MANALO, Jude Karlo G. Bolivar, Jeromir G. Bondoc, Blanca Jarilla Nagataki, Karl Ian T. Ermino, Kazunori Kimitsuki, Leilanie B. Nacion, Mark Joseph M. Espino, Chun-Ho Park, Satoshi Inoue
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains a major public health concern in endemic settings, where effective control depends on timely and reliable post-mortem confirmation of infection in animal reservoirs. Although the direct fluorescent antibody test (dFAT) remains the reference standard, its dependence on fresh brain tissue and susceptibility to specimen degradation limits its operational use in decentralized surveillance systems. The present findings demonstrate that the locally optimized RT-qPCR assay targeting the nucleoprotein gene in non-brain tissues exhibits high analytical sensitivity and strong concordance with dFAT results obtained from post-mortem brain specimens of dogs. The consistent performance in nasal planum and follicle-sinus complex samples supports using safer, more accessible post-mortem specimens, reinforcing the potential for scalable, field-adapted molecular diagnostics to strengthen routine animal rabies surveillance in endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies remains a major public health concern in endemic settings, where effective control depends on timely and reliable post-mortem confirmation of infection in animal reservoirs.
Design and population
The present findings demonstrate that the locally optimized RT-qPCR assay targeting the nucleoprotein gene in non-brain tissues exhibits high analytical sensitivity and strong concordance with dFAT results obtained from post-mortem brain specimens of dogs.
Core methods
The study involved the development and validation of a real-time PCR assay for detecting SEA4 clade rabies virus in non-brain animal samples, with a focus on post-mortem specimens such as nasal planum and follicle-sinus complex.
Main results
The consistent performance in nasal planum and follicle-sinus complex samples supports using safer, more accessible post-mortem specimens, reinforcing the potential for scalable, field-adapted molecular diagnostics to strengthen routine animal rabies surveillance in endemic regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides a novel approach for rabies virus detection in non-brain tissues, which may improve the efficiency and reliability of post-mortem diagnosis in animal reservoirs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the new RT-qPCR assay could enhance rabies surveillance in endemic areas by enabling the use of non-brain specimens, which are more readily available and less prone to degradation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific clade and animal species, but the evidence supports a moderate confidence in the assay's performance for the targeted rabies virus in non-brain tissues.

厄立特里亚 狂犬病病毒 的分子表征揭示了 Africa 1a 谱系的循环。

Molecular characterization of rabies virus in Eritrea reveals circulation of the Africa 1a lineage.
作者： Tesfalem Mokonen Adha, Habtom Habtemaryam Gebremedhin, Arsiema Berhane Adonoy, Yonas Zeru Sahlezghi, Yohane Berhane Tsegay, Gebrehawaryat Tewelde Kahsay, Chalumba Simukoko, Weldu Tesfagaber Mengisteab, Hirofumi Sawa, Walter Muleya
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 在非洲许多地区仍然流行,但缺乏包括厄立特里亚在内的几个国家的分子数据。本研究报告了厄立特里亚使用 2001 年至 2024 年间收集的存档脑样本首次对 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 进行分子表征。使用针对核蛋白 (N) 基因的一步 RT-PCR 对来自家养和野生动物的总共 95 个样本进行了筛选。十八个样本测试呈阳性,其中十六个产生的序列质量足以进行系统发育分析。最大似然分析显示,所有厄立特里亚 RABV 序列都聚集在 Africa 1a 谱系内,并显示出与埃塞俄比亚和索马里毒株的密切遗传相关性,表明区域连通性和可能的​​跨境 传播。来自不同 宿主 物种和区域的序列散布在 系统发育 内,支持家犬作为主要 储存宿主,而频繁的 跨物种溢出 进入牲畜。尽管厄立特里亚定期开展大规模犬疫苗接种运动,但遗传相关RABV毒株的持续传播凸显了监测和控制方面的差距。这些发现为厄立特里亚的 狂犬病 提供了关键的分子基线,并支持需要持续狗 疫苗接种 和加强区域 狂犬病 以推进 狂犬病 消除工作。

问题与背景
狂犬病 在非洲许多地区仍然流行,但缺乏包括厄立特里亚在内的几个国家的分子数据。
设计与对象
这项研究报告了厄立特里亚使用 2001 年至 2024 年间收集的存档脑样本首次对 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 进行分子表征。
核心方法
使用针对核蛋白 (N) 基因的一步 RT-PCR 筛选了来自家养和野生动物的总共 95 个样本。
主要结果
十八个样本测试呈阳性,其中十六个产生的序列质量足以进行系统发育分析。
解释与创新
最大似然分析显示,所有厄立特里亚 RABV 序列都聚集在 Africa 1a 谱系内,并显示出与埃塞俄比亚和索马里毒株的密切遗传相关性,表明区域连通性和可能的​​跨境 传播。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为厄立特里亚的 狂犬病 提供了关键的分子基线,并支持需要持续狗 疫苗接种 和加强区域 狂犬病 以推进 狂犬病 消除工作。

Molecular characterization of rabies virus in Eritrea reveals circulation of the Africa 1a lineage.

Authors: Tesfalem Mokonen Adha, Habtom Habtemaryam Gebremedhin, Arsiema Berhane Adonoy, Yonas Zeru Sahlezghi, Yohane Berhane Tsegay, Gebrehawaryat Tewelde Kahsay, Chalumba Simukoko, Weldu Tesfagaber Mengisteab, Hirofumi Sawa, Walter Muleya
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains endemic in many parts of Africa, yet molecular data from several countries, including Eritrea, are lacking. This study reports the first molecular characterization of rabies virus (RABV) in Eritrea using archived brain samples collected between 2001 and 2024. A total of 95 samples from domestic and wild animals were screened using one-step RT-PCR targeting the nucleoprotein (N) gene. Eighteen samples tested positive, of which sixteen yielded sequences of sufficient quality for phylogenetic analysis. Maximum likelihood analysis revealed that all Eritrean RABV sequences clustered within the Africa 1a lineage and showed close genetic relatedness to strains from Ethiopia and Somalia, indicating regional connectivity and probable cross-border transmission. Sequences from different host species and regions were interspersed within the phylogeny, supporting domestic dogs as the primary reservoir with frequent spillover into livestock. Although Eritrea has implemented periodic mass dog vaccination campaigns, the continued circulation of genetically related RABV strains highlights gaps in surveillance and control. These findings provide a critical molecular baseline for rabies in Eritrea and support the need for sustained dog vaccination and strengthened regional surveillance to advance rabies elimination efforts.

通过荧光抗体测试和快速免疫色谱法检测印度泰米尔纳德邦塞勒姆怀孕山羊中的 狂犬病病毒 抗原

Detection of Rabies Virus Antigen by Fluorescent Antibody Test and Rapid Immunochromatographic Assay in a Pregnant Goat from Salem, Tamil Nadu, India
作者： P. A. Enbavelan, Adithya Sundar, T. Mohanapriya, C. Theophilus Anand Kumar, E. Venkatesakumar, S. Sivaraman, R. Sivachandiran, A. Elango
摘要中文翻译在印度,小反刍动物的 狂犬病 诊断不足,因为通常没有咬伤史,而且临床症状可能类似于妊娠晚期的代谢紊乱。本研究报告了怀孕母鹿中 狂犬病 的病例,经荧光抗体测试证实并得到两种互补技术的支持。一只妊娠约 4.5 个月的母鹿被送往泰米尔纳德邦塞勒姆兽医学院和研究所兽医临床中心,其食欲不振、攻击性强且躺着。生前估计血糖和尿糖。母鹿表现出攻击性、咬人、揉眼睛、连续舔腿、打哈欠、咩咩叫和胸骨躺卧,没有被狗咬伤的报道。母鹿在介绍当天就死了。血糖为103 mg/dL,伴有糖尿。使用荧光抗体测试(FAT)、快速免疫层析侧流测定(LFA)和改良的范吉森染色对通过枕大孔收集的死后脑组织进行检查。FAT 显示苹果绿胞质内荧光,LFA 显示明显的对照带和测试带,并通过改良的 Van Gieson 染色鉴定 Negri 小体。狂犬病被FAT确认;LFA 结果一致,Negri 机构提供了支持性证据。在本例中观察到一只厌食的怀孕母鹿出现高血糖并伴有糖尿。然而,这一发现并不具有特异性,其诊断价值需要在更多病例中进行评估。LFA 提供快速的现场级检测,可以补充 FAT(黄金标准方法)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在印度,小反刍动物的 狂犬病 诊断不足,因为通常没有咬伤史,而且临床症状可能类似于妊娠晚期的代谢紊乱。

核心方法

主要结果
母鹿表现出攻击性、咬人、揉眼睛、连续舔腿、打哈欠、咩咩叫和胸骨躺卧,没有被狗咬伤的报道。母鹿在介绍当天就死了。血糖为103 mg/dL,伴有糖尿。

LFA 提供快速的现场级检测,可以补充 FAT(黄金标准方法)。

在本例中观察到一只厌食的怀孕母鹿出现高血糖并伴有糖尿。然而,这一发现并不具有特异性,其诊断价值需要在更多病例中进行评估。

Detection of Rabies Virus Antigen by Fluorescent Antibody Test and Rapid Immunochromatographic Assay in a Pregnant Goat from Salem, Tamil Nadu, India

Authors: P. A. Enbavelan, Adithya Sundar, T. Mohanapriya, C. Theophilus Anand Kumar, E. Venkatesakumar, S. Sivaraman, R. Sivachandiran, A. Elango
Original abstract (English)
Rabies in small ruminants is underdiagnosed in India because a bite history is often absent and clinical signs may mimic metabolic disorders of late pregnancy. This study reports a case of rabies in a pregnant doe, confirmed by the fluorescent antibody test and supported by two complementary techniques. A doe at approximately 4.5 months’ gestation was presented to the Veterinary Clinical Complex, Veterinary College and Research Institute, Salem, Tamil Nadu, with inappetence, aggression and recumbency. Blood and urine glucose were estimated ante-mortem. The doe exhibited aggression, biting, eye rubbing, continuous leg licking, yawning, bleating and sternal recumbency, with no reported history of a dog bite. The doe died on the day of presentation. Blood glucose was 103 mg/dL, accompanied by glucosuria. Postmortem brain tissue collected through the occipital foramen was examined using the fluorescent antibody test (FAT), a rapid immunochromatographic lateral flow assay (LFA) and modified Van Gieson staining. FAT revealed apple-green intracytoplasmic fluorescence, the LFA showed distinct control and test bands and Negri bodies were identified by modified Van Gieson staining. Rabies was confirmed by FAT; the LFA result was concordant and the Negri bodies provided supportive evidence. Hyperglycaemia with glucosuria in an anorectic pregnant doe was observed in this case; however, this finding is non-specific, and its diagnostic value needs to be evaluated in a larger number of cases. The LFA offers rapid, field-level detection and can complement FAT, the gold standard method.

咬伤后:2014-2024 年加拿大安大略省动物暴露事件的描述性分析,导致人类 狂犬病 暴露后预防 建议

After the bite: A descriptive analysis of animal exposure incidents resulting in a human rabies post-exposure prophylaxis recommendation in Ontario, Canada, 2014–2024
作者： Katherine Paphitis, Alexandra Reid, Mark P. Nelder, Curtis B. Russell, Maureen E. C. Anderson, Richard G. Mather, Heather McClinchey
摘要中文翻译背景:对于加拿大安大略省建议 狂犬病 暴露后预防 (RPEP) 的所有潜在 狂犬病 暴露事件,人口统计和暴露信息将报告给安大略省公共卫生部门,以支持省 狂犬病 处理此类事件,并为省级规划(包括 疫苗 采购)提供信息。目标:描述安大略省本地或当地居民潜在 狂犬病 暴露的人口和物种特定趋势研究结果可以为公共教育和公共卫生风险评估提供信息。方法:从省级 监测 数据库中提取 2014 年至 2024 年建议接受 RPEP 的安大略省居民的人口统计和暴露信息。获得了加拿大食品检验局同期公开的动物 狂犬病 检测数据。对数据进行分析,以确定人口或物种特定趋势。结果:研究期间报告的潜在 狂犬病 暴露事件增加了一倍多。大多数事件涉及狗 (37%)、蝙蝠 (22%) 或猫 (17%),其中蝙蝠暴露在 8 月份持续达到峰值。涉及的动物种类与客户年龄和性别有关。涉及国际旅行的事件中,有 16% 与灵长类动物有关,其中几例与猕猴有关。结论:宠物主人越来越认识到 狂犬病 的重要性。针对家猫和狗的 狂犬病 疫苗接种,以及在发生潜在暴露事件时共享此类信息的需要,可以在某些情况下减轻 RPEP 的需求。对报告涉及灵长类动物的暴露事件的回国旅行者进行风险评估,应考虑其他人畜共患暴露风险,特别是 B 型疱疹病毒。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述安大略省居民潜在 狂犬病 暴露的人口统计和物种特定趋势,从而建议接受 狂犬病 暴露后预防 (RPEP)。这对于为公众教育和公共卫生风险评估提供信息非常重要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了从省 监测 数据库中提取的 2014 年至 2024 年建议接受 RPEP 的安大略省居民的人口统计和暴露信息。同期还获得了加拿大食品检验局公开的动物 狂犬病 检测数据。
核心方法
建议接受 RPEP 的安大略省居民的人口统计和暴露信息是从省 监测 数据库中提取的。来自加拿大食品检验局的公开动物 狂犬病 测试数据是在同一时期获得的,并进行分析以确定人口或物种特定的趋势。
主要结果
研究期间报告的潜在 狂犬病 暴露事件增加了一倍多。大多数事件涉及狗(37%)、蝙蝠(22%)或猫(17%),其中蝙蝠接触率在八月份持续达到峰值。涉及的动物种类与客户的年龄和性别相关。百分之十六涉及国际旅行的事件与灵长类动物有关,其中几起与猕猴有关。
解释与创新
作者建议,提高宠物主人对 狂犬病 疫苗接种 对于家猫和狗的重要性的认识,以及在发生潜在暴露事件时分享此类信息的必要性,可以减轻在某些情况下对 RPEP 的需求。他们还强调,考虑到其他人畜共患病暴露风险,需要对报告涉及灵长类动物的暴露事件的回国旅行者进行风险评估。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为公共教育工作和公共卫生风险评估提供信息,特别是在通过宠物 狂犬病 预防 狂犬病 暴露以及回程旅行者人畜共患病风险管理方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于省级 监测 系统报告的数据,该系统可能存在漏报或数据收集实践存在差异的情况。由于依赖观察数据而不是实验方法,证据的强度中等。

After the bite: A descriptive analysis of animal exposure incidents resulting in a human rabies post-exposure prophylaxis recommendation in Ontario, Canada, 2014–2024

Authors: Katherine Paphitis, Alexandra Reid, Mark P. Nelder, Curtis B. Russell, Maureen E. C. Anderson, Richard G. Mather, Heather McClinchey
Original abstract (English)
Background: For all potential rabies exposure incidents in Ontario, Canada where rabies post-exposure prophylaxis (RPEP) is recommended, demographic and exposure information is reported to Public Health Ontario to support provincial surveillance of such incidents and to inform provincial planning, including vaccine procurement.Objective: To describe demographic and species-specific trends for potential rabies exposures of Ontario residents, either locally or overseas, that resulted in a recommendation to receive RPEP.Findings can inform public education and public health risk assessments.Methods: Demographic and exposure information for Ontario residents who were recommended to receive RPEP from 2014 to 2024 were extracted from the provincial surveillance database.Publicly available animal rabies testing data from the Canadian Food Inspection Agency were obtained for the same period.Data were analyzed to identify demographic or species-specific trends.Results: Reported potential rabies exposure incidents more than doubled over the study period.Most incidents involved a dog (37%), bat (22%) or a cat (17%), with bat exposures consistently peaking during the month of August.Implicated animal species was associated with both client age and gender.Sixteen percent of incidents involving international travel were associated with a primate, several with a macaque.Conclusion: Increasing awareness among pet owners about the importance of rabies vaccination for domestic cats and dogs, and the need to share such information in the event of a potential exposure incident, could mitigate the need for RPEP in some situations.Risk assessment for returning travellers who report an exposure incident involving a primate should consider other zoonotic exposure risks, particularly herpes B virus.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe demographic and species-specific trends for potential rabies exposures of Ontario residents that resulted in a recommendation to receive rabies post-exposure prophylaxis (RPEP). This is important for informing public education and public health risk assessments.
Design and population
The study analyzed demographic and exposure information for Ontario residents recommended to receive RPEP from 2014 to 2024, extracted from the provincial surveillance database. Publicly available animal rabies testing data from the Canadian Food Inspection Agency were also obtained for the same period.
Core methods
Demographic and exposure information for Ontario residents who were recommended to receive RPEP was extracted from the provincial surveillance database. Publicly available animal rabies testing data from the Canadian Food Inspection Agency were obtained for the same period and analyzed to identify demographic or species-specific trends.
Main results
Reported potential rabies exposure incidents more than doubled over the study period. Most incidents involved a dog (37%), bat (22%) or a cat (17%), with bat exposures consistently peaking during the month of August. Implicated animal species was associated with both client age and gender. Sixteen percent of incidents involving international travel were associated with a primate, several with a macaque.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that increasing awareness among pet owners about the importance of rabies vaccination for domestic cats and dogs, and the need to share such information in the event of a potential exposure incident, could mitigate the need for RPEP in some situations. They also highlight the need for risk assessment for returning travellers who report an exposure incident involving a primate, considering other zoonotic exposure risks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public education efforts and public health risk assessments, particularly regarding the prevention of rabies exposure through pet vaccination and the management of zoonotic risks for returning travelers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on reported data from a provincial surveillance system, which may be subject to underreporting or variability in data collection practices. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on observational data rather than experimental methods.

地方公共卫生统一指挥:内布拉斯加州道格拉斯县 狂犬病 和结核病应对案例研究。

Unified Command in Local Public Health: Case Studies of Rabies and Tuberculosis Responses in Douglas County, Nebraska.
作者： Caleb Kuddes, Chad Wetzel, Kari Neemann, Justin Frederick
摘要中文翻译随着突发公共卫生事件变得更加频繁和复杂,地方卫生部门必须越来越多地依靠公私伙伴关系和全社区方法来有效应对。为内布拉斯加州人口最多的县提供服务的道格拉斯县卫生局 (DCHD) 展示了当地机构如何通过与不同合作伙伴实施统一的指挥结构来协调大规模应对措施。本案例研究介绍了 2023 年末同时发生的 2 起突发公共卫生事件:(1) 在内布拉斯加州检测到浣熊 狂犬病病毒 变种 (RRVV),这是一种以前未在内布拉斯加州发现的病原体;(2) 该县历史上调查的最大规模的儿童结核病 (TB) 暴露事件。这两起事件都需要地方、州和联邦机构之间的快速协调,以及医疗系统和社区组织的参与。在每次响应中,DCHD 都启动了统一的指挥结构,以促进行动目标、分配资源并协调风险沟通。狂犬病 应对措施包括增强动物 狂犬病、诱捕-疫苗-释放活动以及手动分发 18,000 个口服 狂犬病 疫苗 诱饵。没有发现其他人类或动物 RRVV 病例。结核病应对措施包括识别 643 名暴露者、与当地医疗合作伙伴一起开展大规模诊所检测,并迅速启动针对弱势群体的预防措施。未发现继发性 传播 病例。这些案例研究展示了地方卫生部门如何通过利用已建立的伙伴关系、符合国家事件管理系统原则以及协调统一指挥行动来领导复杂的应对措施。从道格拉斯县的经验中吸取的教训为其他司法管辖区准备应对人畜共患和空气传播的传染病威胁提供了实用指导,特别是在内部资源有限的情况下。

Unified Command in Local Public Health: Case Studies of Rabies and Tuberculosis Responses in Douglas County, Nebraska.

Authors: Caleb Kuddes, Chad Wetzel, Kari Neemann, Justin Frederick
Original abstract (English)
As public health emergencies become more frequent and complex, local health departments must increasingly rely on public-private partnerships and whole-community approaches to respond effectively. The Douglas County Health Department (DCHD), serving Nebraska's most populous county, demonstrates how a local agency can coordinate large-scale responses by implementing a unified command structure with diverse partners. This case study presents 2 concurrent public health emergencies that occurred in late 2023: (1) the detection of raccoon rabies virus variant (RRVV) in a domestic kitten, a pathogen not previously identified in Nebraska, and (2) the largest pediatric tuberculosis (TB) exposure investigated in the county's history. Both incidents required rapid coordination across local, state, and federal agencies, as well as engagement with healthcare systems and community organizations. In each response, DCHD activated a unified command structure to facilitate operational objectives, allocate resources, and coordinate risk communication. The rabies response included enhanced animal surveillance, a trap-vaccinate-release campaign, and the manual distribution of 18,000 oral rabies vaccine baits. No additional human or animal cases of RRVV were identified. The TB response involved identifying 643 exposed individuals, conducting mass testing clinics with local healthcare partners, and rapidly initiating prophylaxis for vulnerable populations. No cases of secondary transmission were identified. These case studies demonstrate how local health departments can lead complex responses by leveraging established partnerships, aligning with National Incident Management System principles, and coordinating unified command operations. Lessons learned from Douglas County's experience offer practical guidance for other jurisdictions preparing for zoonotic and airborne infectious disease threats, especially when internal resources are limited.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As public health emergencies become more frequent and complex, local health departments must increasingly rely on public-private partnerships and whole-community approaches to respond effectively.
Design and population
The Douglas County Health Department (DCHD), serving Nebraska's most populous county, demonstrates how a local agency can coordinate large-scale responses by implementing a unified command structure with diverse partners.
Core methods
The rabies response included enhanced animal surveillance, a trap-vaccinate-release campaign, and the manual distribution of 18,000 oral rabies vaccine baits. The TB response involved identifying 643 exposed individuals, conducting mass testing clinics with local healthcare partners, and rapidly initiating prophylaxis for vulnerable populations.
Main results
No additional human or animal cases of RRVV were identified. No cases of secondary transmission were identified.
Interpretation and novelty
These case studies demonstrate how local health departments can lead complex responses by leveraging established partnerships, aligning with National Incident Management System principles, and coordinating unified command operations.
Scientific and public-health significance
Lessons learned from Douglas County's experience offer practical guidance for other jurisdictions preparing for zoonotic and airborne infectious disease threats, especially when internal resources are limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

智利消除了以狗为媒介的 狂犬病。

Chile eliminates dog-mediated rabies.
作者： Udani Samarasekera
摘要中文翻译智利是南美洲第一个获得世卫组织确认消除由狗传播的 狂犬病 公共卫生问题的国家,此前已有 50 多年没有出现由狗传播的人类病例。该验证认可了一个程序,该程序在没有人类病例的情况下仍保持了 监测 和响应能力。智利最后一次报道由狗传播人类 狂犬病 病例是在 1972 年。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在消除智利由狗介导的 狂犬病,解决长期存在的公共卫生问题。五十多年来没有出现过由狗传播的人类病例,智利已获得世界卫生组织消除这种疾病的认可。
设计与对象
该研究涉及智利的 监测 和应对能力维护,重点关注自 1972 年以来没有出现由狗传播的 狂犬病 人类病例。
核心方法
尽管没有狗传播的 狂犬病 人类病例,智利仍保持了 监测 和应对能力,最后一次报告病例是在 1972 年。
主要结果
智利最后一次报道由狗传播人类 狂犬病 病例是在 1972 年。
解释与创新
此次验证认可了智利维持 监测 和应对能力的计划,标志着智利成为南美洲第一个消除狗介导的 狂犬病 公共卫生问题的国家。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为未来的 狂犬病 控制策略提供了信息,特别是在 狂犬病 和应对能力维护方面,并强调了长期致力于公共卫生措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度很高,因为它是基于长期 监测 和应对能力,但它没有解决其他 狂犬病 传播 路线的潜在风险。

Chile eliminates dog-mediated rabies.

Authors: Udani Samarasekera
Original abstract (English)
Chile is the first country in South America to gain WHO validation for eliminating dog-mediated rabies as a public health problem, after more than five decades without a human case transmitted by a dog. The validation recognises a programme that has maintained surveillance and response capacity despite the absence of human cases. Chile’s last reported case of human rabies transmitted by dogs was in 1972.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to eliminate dog-mediated rabies in Chile, addressing a long-standing public health issue. After over five decades without a human case transmitted by dogs, Chile has gained WHO validation for eliminating this disease.
Design and population
The study involved surveillance and response capacity maintenance in Chile, focusing on the absence of human cases of rabies transmitted by dogs since 1972.
Core methods
Chile maintained surveillance and response capacity despite the absence of human cases of rabies transmitted by dogs, with the last reported case in 1972.
Main results
Chile’s last reported case of human rabies transmitted by dogs was in 1972.
Interpretation and novelty
The validation recognises Chile’s programme for maintaining surveillance and response capacity, marking it as the first country in South America to eliminate dog-mediated rabies as a public health problem.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform future rabies control strategies, particularly in surveillance and response capacity maintenance, and highlight the importance of long-term commitment to public health measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s evidence strength is high, as it is based on long-term surveillance and response capacity, but it does not address potential risks from other rabies transmission routes.

狂犬病 预防的异质途径:狂犬病 吸收的家庭层面决定因素和狗主人概况

Heterogeneous Pathways to Rabies Prevention: Household-Level Determinants and Dog Owner Profiles of Vaccination Uptake
作者： Richard Nyoni, Tionge Chunda, Gilbert Banda, Marko Chiumia
摘要中文翻译摘要 尽管有有效的 疫苗 且全球承诺通过大规模狗 疫苗 消除该疾病,但狗介导的 狂犬病 仍然是许多低收入和中等收入国家持续存在的公共卫生挑战。由于城市和乡村环境中养狗、管理实践和获得兽医服务的情况存在巨大差异,实现并维持建议的 70% 疫苗接种 覆盖率变得复杂。本研究调查了马拉维姆津巴区养狗家庭中养狗 疫苗接种 的家庭层面决定因素,并探讨了 狂犬病 知识和 狂犬病 行为的异质性。该研究利用 682 个家庭的二次横断面调查数据,结合计数数据回归和潜在类别分析来研究影响每个家庭接种疫苗的狗数量的因素,并确定不同的狗主人类型。负二项回归结果表明,城市居住、较高的教育程度、负责任养狗的培训、较大的养狗规模和先前的 疫苗接种 行为显着增加家庭 疫苗接种 的吸收,而仅靠 狂犬病 知识并不能独立预测 疫苗接种。潜在类别分析揭示了三种不同的主人概况:低意识中等预防主人、以预防为导向的中等意识主人和高意识高风险多犬主人。这些集群在狗的种群结构、知识水平和 疫苗接种 结果方面存在显着差异,凸显了某些群体的意识和实践之间的不匹配。总之,研究结果表明,家庭层面的配置和行为模式在塑造 疫苗接种 结果方面发挥着关键作用。定制的、情境敏感的 狂犬病 控制策略整合了教育、以行为为中心的培训和结构支持,对于提高 疫苗接种 覆盖范围和促进狗介导的 狂犬病 消除至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管存在有效的 疫苗 且全球承诺通过大规模狗 疫苗 消除这种疾病,但本研究解决了低收入和中等收入国家由狗介导的 狂犬病 带来的持续公共卫生挑战。
设计与对象
该研究利用马拉维姆津巴区682户家庭的二次横断面调查数据,调查家庭层面养狗疫苗接种的决定因素,并探讨养狗家庭狂犬病知识和疫苗接种行为的异质性。
核心方法
该研究结合了计数数据回归和潜在类别分析,以检查影响每个家庭接种疫苗的狗数量的因素,并确定不同的狗主人类型。
主要结果
负二项回归结果表明,城市居住、较高的教育程度、负责任养狗的培训、较大的养狗规模以及之前的 疫苗接种 行为显着增加了家庭 疫苗接种 的吸收,而仅靠 狂犬病 知识并不能独立预测 疫苗接种。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,家庭层面的配置和行为模式在塑造 疫苗接种 结果方面发挥着关键作用,根据业主的意识和预防实践确定了不同的业主概况。
科研与公卫意义
这些集群在狗的种群结构、知识水平和 狂犬病 结果方面存在显着差异,强调需要量身定制的、情境敏感的 狂犬病 控制策略,将教育、以行为为中心的培训和结构支持结合起来。
局限与证据强度
由于城市和乡村环境中养狗、管理实践和获得兽医服务的情况存在巨大差异,实现并维持建议的 70% 疫苗接种 覆盖率变得复杂。

Heterogeneous Pathways to Rabies Prevention: Household-Level Determinants and Dog Owner Profiles of Vaccination Uptake

Authors: Richard Nyoni, Tionge Chunda, Gilbert Banda, Marko Chiumia
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dog-mediated rabies remains a persistent public health challenge in many low- and middle-income countries, despite the availability of effective vaccines and global commitments to eliminate the disease through mass dog vaccination. Achieving and sustaining the recommended 70% vaccination coverage is complicated by substantial heterogeneity in dog ownership, management practices, and access to veterinary services, across urban and rural settings. This study investigates household-level determinants of dog vaccination and explores heterogeneity in rabies knowledge and vaccination behaviour among dog-owning households in Mzimba District, Malawi. Using secondary cross-sectional survey data from 682 households, the study combines count data regression and latent class analysis to examine factors influencing the number of dogs vaccinated per household and to identify distinct dog-owner typologies. The Negative Binomial Regression results indicate that urban residence, higher educational attainment, training in responsible dog ownership, larger dog ownership size, and prior vaccination behaviour significantly increase household vaccination uptake, whereas rabies knowledge alone does not independently predict vaccination. Latent Class Analysis revealed three distinct owner profiles: low-awareness moderate-prevention owners, prevention-oriented moderately aware owners , and high-awareness high-risk multidog owners . These clusters differ markedly in dog population structure, knowledge levels, and vaccination outcomes, highlighting mismatches between awareness and practice in some groups. In summary, the findings demonstrate that household-level configurations and behavioural patterns play a critical role in shaping vaccination outcomes. Tailored, context-sensitive rabies control strategies that integrate education, behaviour-focused training, and structural support are essential to improve vaccination coverage and advance dog-mediated rabies elimination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study addresses the persistent public health challenge of dog-mediated rabies in low- and middle-income countries, despite the availability of effective vaccines and global commitments to eliminate the disease through mass dog vaccination.
Design and population
The study uses secondary cross-sectional survey data from 682 households in Mzimba District, Malawi, to investigate household-level determinants of dog vaccination and explore heterogeneity in rabies knowledge and vaccination behaviour among dog-owning households.
Core methods
The study combines count data regression and latent class analysis to examine factors influencing the number of dogs vaccinated per household and to identify distinct dog-owner typologies.
Main results
Negative Binomial Regression results indicate that urban residence, higher educational attainment, training in responsible dog ownership, larger dog ownership size, and prior vaccination behaviour significantly increase household vaccination uptake, whereas rabies knowledge alone does not independently predict vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that household-level configurations and behavioural patterns play a critical role in shaping vaccination outcomes, with distinct owner profiles identified based on their awareness and prevention practices.
Scientific and public-health significance
These clusters differ markedly in dog population structure, knowledge levels, and vaccination outcomes, underscoring the need for tailored, context-sensitive rabies control strategies that integrate education, behaviour-focused training, and structural support.
Limitations and evidence strength
Achieving and sustaining the recommended 70% vaccination coverage is complicated by substantial heterogeneity in dog ownership, management practices, and access to veterinary services, across urban and rural settings.

gp120 与 HIV-1 M 亚型 A 和 B 的差异特性

Differential properties of gp120 from HIV-1 group M subtypes A and B
作者： Yukako C. Tamazawa, Dante I. Suzuki, Yuichiro Suzuki, Nataliia V. Shults
摘要中文翻译HIV-1 M 亚型 A 和 B 的 gp120 序列有很大差异。为了确定这些差异是否导致亚型特异性并发症,特别是肺动脉高压 (PAH),给大鼠注射了 A 或 B 亚型的 gp120。4 周后,使用苏木精和伊红 (H&E) 染色以及 Autona (autona.org) 的血管形态测量分析工具评估肺动脉 (PA) 重塑。A 亚型 gp120 有效增加 PA 壁厚度和右心室肥厚。A 亚型的 CD4 结合区缺少一段 16 个氨基酸(V1 N-糖基化位点),实验表明 B 亚型与 CD4 的结合更强。B 亚型 gp120 包含 RGKV 序列,该序列类似于与烟碱乙酰胆碱受体 (nAChR) 结合的 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 G 的 RGKR 序列。A 亚型 gp120 具有 KDKK 序列。分子动力学模拟表明这两个位点都是表面暴露的。尽管 nAChR 在 PA 细胞中表达,并且可能驱动 A 亚型的显着效应,但 MD 模拟估计 B 亚型 gp120 与 nAChR 的结合更强。我们还注意到 REKR 弗林蛋白酶裂解序列之前的一个氨基酸差异:A 亚型中的谷氨酸与 B 亚型 gp120 中的谷氨酰胺。模拟预测,A 亚型 gp120 与弗林蛋白酶相互作用更有效,并且酶测定表明,A 亚型比 B 亚型更能促进正反馈环。因此,HIV-1 gp120 A 亚型在促进 PAH 方面更有效,其机制可能涉及增强弗林蛋白酶活性以提高 gp120 水平。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 HIV-1 M 亚型 A 和 B 的 gp120 序列的差异是否会导致亚型特异性并发症,特别是肺动脉高压 (PAH)。
设计与对象
该研究涉及给大鼠注射来自 HIV-1 M 亚型 A 或 B 的 gp120,以评估 4 周后的肺动脉 (PA) 重塑。
核心方法
使用苏木精和伊红 (H&E) 染色以及 Autona (autona.org) 的血管形态测量分析工具评估肺动脉 (PA) 重塑。
主要结果
与B亚型gp120相比,A亚型gp120显着增加PA壁厚度和右心室肥厚。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,A 亚型 gp120 可能更有效地促进 PAH,可能是通过增强弗林蛋白酶活性来提高 gp120 水平。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能有助于了解 HIV-1 亚型特异性致病机制及其对疾病进展的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,没有考虑人类特定因素,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。

Differential properties of gp120 from HIV-1 group M subtypes A and B

Authors: Yukako C. Tamazawa, Dante I. Suzuki, Yuichiro Suzuki, Nataliia V. Shults
Original abstract (English)
HIV-1 group M subtypes A and B differ substantially in their gp120 sequence. To determine whether these differences contribute to subtype-specific complications, particularly pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH), rats were injected with gp120 from subtype A or B. Pulmonary artery (PA) remodeling was assessed after four weeks using hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) staining and the Vessel Morphometry Analysis tool by Autona (autona.org). Subtype A gp120 potently increased PA wall thickness and right ventricular hypertrophy. The CD4-binding region of subtype A lacks a stretch of 16 amino acids (V1 N-glycosylation site), and experiments showed stronger binding of subtype B to CD4. Subtype B gp120 contains an RGKV sequence, which resembles the RGKR sequence of the rabies virus glycoprotein G that binds to the nicotinic acetylcholine receptor (nAChR). Subtype A gp120 possesses a KDKK sequence instead. Molecular dynamics simulations indicated that both sites are surface-exposed. Although nAChR is expressed in PA cells and could potentially drive the pronounced effects of subtype A, MD simulations estimated that subtype B gp120 binds more strongly to nAChR. We also noted a single amino acid difference immediately preceding the REKR furin cleavage sequence: glutamate in subtype A versus glutamine in subtype B gp120. Simulations predicted that subtype A gp120 interacts more effectively with furin, and enzyme assays demonstrated that subtype A promotes a positive feedback loop more strongly than subtype B. Thus, HIV-1 gp120 subtype A is more potent in promoting PAH, and the mechanism may involve enhancing furin activity to elevate gp120 levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether differences in the gp120 sequence of HIV-1 group M subtypes A and B contribute to subtype-specific complications, particularly pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH).
Design and population
The study involved injecting rats with gp120 from HIV-1 group M subtypes A or B to assess pulmonary artery (PA) remodeling after four weeks.
Core methods
Pulmonary artery (PA) remodeling was evaluated using hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) staining and the Vessel Morphometry Analysis tool by Autona (autona.org).
Main results
Subtype A gp120 significantly increased PA wall thickness and right ventricular hypertrophy compared to subtype B gp120.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that subtype A gp120 may be more potent in promoting PAH, potentially through enhanced furin activity that elevates gp120 levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform the understanding of subtype-specific pathogenic mechanisms in HIV-1 and their implications for disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model and does not account for human-specific factors, which may affect the generalizability of the findings.

小鼠白质中单个少突胶质细胞的可视化与形态学分析

Visualization and Morphological Analysis of Individual Oligodendrocytes in the Mouse White Matter.
作者： Osanai Y, Looprasertkul S, Battulga B, Yamazaki R, Ohno N
摘要中文翻译少突胶质细胞形成髓鞘对于调节传导速度和维持正常脑功能至关重要。为了确保对如感觉刺激和学习等经验的准确信息处理,少突胶质细胞会调整其数量和形态。此外,少突胶质细胞的形态也会随着衰老和神经退行性疾病的发生而发生变化。因此,对少突胶质细胞进行可视化和形态学分析对于理解我们的大脑在这些条件下的变化至关重要。本文描述了在光学显微镜水平上对小鼠白质中单个少突胶质细胞进行标记和形态学分析的方法。PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP和PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP小鼠使我们能够可视化和分析晚期出生或早期出生的少突胶质细胞的形态。此外,使用减弱的狂犬病病毒表达GFP的稀疏少突胶质细胞标记方法,可以在无需转基因动物的情况下,对各种脑白质区域中的单个少突胶质细胞进行可视化和形态学分析。此外,与厚组织免疫染色相结合,可以识别标记的少突胶质细胞和髓鞘,以及它们与神经元轴突的相互作用。这些方法适用于揭示少突胶质细胞如何根据环境刺激或病理条件调整其形态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解少突胶质细胞如何适应其形态以响应环境刺激或病理条件,因为少突胶质细胞的髓磷脂形成对于调节传导速度和正常的大脑功能至关重要。少突胶质细胞调整其数量和形态,以确保响应感官刺激和学习等体验而进行准确的信息处理。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP 和 PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP 小鼠来可视化和分析晚生或早生的少突胶质细胞形态。用表达 GFP 的减毒 狂犬病病毒 标记稀疏少突胶质细胞,无需转基因动物即可对各个脑白质区域中的单个少突胶质细胞进行可视化和形态学分析。
核心方法
这些方法包括使用表达 GFP 的减毒 狂犬病病毒 标记单个少突胶质细胞,并将其与厚组织中的免疫染色相结合,以识别标记的少突胶质细胞和髓鞘,以及它们与神经元轴突的相互作用。
主要结果
该研究直接观察了在光学显微镜水平上可视化和分析小鼠白质中单个少突胶质细胞形态的能力。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明这些方法适用于揭示少突胶质细胞如何根据环境刺激或病理条件调整其形态。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以帮助理解少突胶质细胞如何适应其形态以响应环境刺激或病理条件,这可能对神经科学研究和神经系统疾病的研究产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅基于抽象信息,这限制了分析的深度。由于缺乏详细的结果和统计分析,研究结果的可信度较低。

Visualization and Morphological Analysis of Individual Oligodendrocytes in the Mouse White Matter.

Authors: Osanai Y, Looprasertkul S, Battulga B, Yamazaki R, Ohno N
Original abstract (English)
Myelin formation by oligodendrocytes is essential for the regulation of the conduction velocity and proper brain function. To ensure accurate information processing in response to experiences such as sensory stimuli and learning, oligodendrocytes adjust their number and morphology. In addition, oligodendrocyte morphology changes with senescence and in the presence of neurodegenerative diseases. Thus, visualizing oligodendrocytes and analyzing their morphology is crucial for understanding how our brains change under such conditions. Herein, we describe the methods for labeling and analyzing the morphologies of individual oligodendrocytes in mouse white matter at the light microscopic level. PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP and PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP mice enable us to visualize and analyze later-born or early-born oligodendrocyte morphology. In addition, sparse oligodendrocyte labeling with attenuated rabies virus expressing GFP enables the visualization and morphological analysis of individual oligodendrocytes in various brain white matter regions without the need for transgenic animals. Furthermore, the combination with immunostaining in thick tissues enables the identification of labeled oligodendrocytes and myelin sheaths, as well as their interactions with neuronal axons. These methods are suitable for revealing how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies depending on environmental stimuli or pathological conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies in response to environmental stimuli or pathological conditions, as myelin formation by oligodendrocytes is essential for the regulation of conduction velocity and proper brain function. Oligodendrocytes adjust their number and morphology to ensure accurate information processing in response to experiences such as sensory stimuli and learning.
Design and population
The study involved the use of PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP and PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP mice to visualize and analyze later-born or early-born oligodendrocyte morphology. Sparse oligodendrocyte labeling with attenuated rabies virus expressing GFP enabled the visualization and morphological analysis of individual oligodendrocytes in various brain white matter regions without the need for transgenic animals.
Core methods
The methods included labeling individual oligodendrocytes using attenuated rabies virus expressing GFP and combining this with immunostaining in thick tissues to identify labeled oligodendrocytes and myelin sheaths, as well as their interactions with neuronal axons.
Main results
The study directly observed the ability to visualize and analyze the morphologies of individual oligodendrocytes in mouse white matter at the light microscopic level.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the suitability of these methods for revealing how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies depending on environmental stimuli or pathological conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the understanding of how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies in response to environmental stimuli or pathological conditions, which may have implications for neuroscience research and the study of neurological disorders.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on abstract-only information, which limits the depth of analysis. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analyses.

纯化鸡胚胎细胞抗狂犬病后的 疫苗 相关吉兰-巴利综合征 疫苗接种:抗 GM1 IgG 抗体的脱髓鞘表型

Vaccine-associated Guillain–Barré Syndrome Following Purified Chick Embryo Cell Antirabies Vaccination: A Demyelinating Phenotype with Anti-GM1 IgG Antibodies
作者： Akash Rawat, Drishtdeum Malik, Chandan Goyal, Keshav Sunita Jindal, Vaidehi Mittal
摘要中文翻译目的 报告纯化鸡胚细胞 (PCEC) 抗狂犬病 疫苗接种 后出现严重脱髓鞘 GBS 变异,并伴有抗 GM1 IgG 抗体,强调需要对 疫苗接种 后神经功能衰退保持警惕。背景 疫苗 相关的 GBS 很少见,但已得到认可,历史上与较旧的神经组织 狂犬病 疫苗 有关。较新的 PCEC 疫苗 被认为更安全;然而,最近的大规模数据表明与 GBS 存在统计学上的显着关联,因此需要重新评估 疫苗接种 后的 监测 阈值。设计/方法 单患者观察报告,包括临床、生化、脑脊液、电诊断、抗体和放射学与 3 个月随访的相关性 结果 一名 25 岁女性在被猴子咬伤后服用第三剂 PCEC 抗狂犬病 疫苗 后三天,出现感觉运动下降无力、面部瘫痪、延髓功能障碍和自主神经受累(包括尿潴留)。未发现既往呼吸道或胃肠道感染。CSF 显示蛋白细胞解离(蛋白质 283 mg/dL,细胞计数正常)。嗜神经病毒组呈阴性。检测到了抗 GM1 IgG 抗体,这种抗体通常与轴突变体相关,但在此发现为脱髓鞘表型。神经传导研究显示正中神经和腓神经有明显的传导阻滞、远端潜伏期延长、传导速度减慢和时间分散,与严重的获得性脱髓鞘性多发性神经病一致。呼吸系统受损需要机械通气。经过 7 次血浆置换(40 mL/kg/次)后,患者在第 7 天拔管。肢体力量改善至 MRC 4/5。4 周时重复 NCS 显示传导阻滞得到缓解,远端潜伏期部分正常化。3 个月随访时,肢体力量全部恢复,仅残留轻微面部无力。结论 该病例表明 PCEC 抗狂犬病 疫苗接种 可能与非典型抗 GM1 IgG 血清阳性的严重脱髓鞘 GBS 相关。无力的下降模式有误诊的风险——需要鉴别肉毒杆菌中毒、白喉和急性间歇性卟啉症。早期电诊断评估和血浆置换启动的低阈值至关重要。随着 狂犬病 疫苗接种 在全球范围内的扩张,后 疫苗接种 神经学 监测 协议需要加强。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在报告纯化鸡胚细胞 (PCEC) 抗狂犬病 疫苗接种 后出现严重脱髓鞘性格林-巴利综合征 (GBS) 变异,并伴有抗 GM1 IgG 抗体,强调需要对 疫苗接种 后神经功能衰退保持警惕。这很重要,因为与 疫苗 相关的 GBS 很少见,但已得到认可,历史上与较旧的神经组织 狂犬病 疫苗 有关。
设计与对象
这是一份单例患者观察报告,涉及一名 25 岁女性,她在被猴子咬伤后服用第三剂 PCEC 抗狂犬病 疫苗 三天后出现神经系统症状。该研究包括临床、生化、脑脊液、电诊断、抗体和放射学评估,并随访 3 个月。
核心方法
该研究涉及临床、生化、脑脊液、电诊断、抗体和放射学评估。3个月后进行随访以监测患者的康复情况和神经系统状态。
主要结果
CSF 显示蛋白细胞解离(蛋白质 283 mg/dL,细胞计数正常)。检测到抗 GM1 IgG 抗体,该抗体通常与轴突变体相关,但在脱髓鞘表型中被鉴定。神经传导研究显示正中神经和腓神经有明显的传导阻滞、远端潜伏期延长、传导速度减慢和时间分散,与严重的获得性脱髓鞘性多发性神经病一致。
解释与创新
作者解释说,该病例表明 PCEC 抗狂犬病 疫苗接种 可能与具有非典型抗 GM1 IgG 血清阳性的严重脱髓鞘 GBS 相关,这是一个新发现,因为抗 GM1 IgG 通常与轴突变体相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,随着 狂犬病 疫苗接种 在全球范围内的扩张,后 疫苗接种 神经学 监测 协议需要加强。早期电诊断评估和血浆去除术对于处理此类病例至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单个患者的观察报告,这限制了研究结果的普遍性。然而,由于提供了详细的临床和诊断评估,证据是强有力的。

Vaccine-associated Guillain–Barré Syndrome Following Purified Chick Embryo Cell Antirabies Vaccination: A Demyelinating Phenotype with Anti-GM1 IgG Antibodies

Authors: Akash Rawat, Drishtdeum Malik, Chandan Goyal, Keshav Sunita Jindal, Vaidehi Mittal
Original abstract (English)
Objective To report severe demyelinating GBS variant with anti-GM1 IgG antibodies following purified chick embryo cell (PCEC) antirabies vaccination, highlighting the need for vigilance in post-vaccination neurological decline. Background Vaccine-associated GBS is rare but recognized, historically linked to older neural-tissue rabies vaccines. Newer PCEC vaccines are considered safer; however, recent large-scale data suggest a statistically significant association with GBS, warranting re-evaluation of post-vaccination surveillance thresholds. Design/Methods Single-patient observational report with clinical, biochemical, cerebrospinal fluid, electrodiagnostic, antibody, and radiologic correlation with follow-up at 3 months Results A 25-year-old woman developed descending sensorimotor weakness, facial diplegia, bulbar dysfunction, and autonomic involvement — including urinary retention — three days after the third dose of PCEC antirabies vaccine administered following a monkey bite. No antecedent respiratory or gastrointestinal infection was identified. CSF demonstrated albuminocytologic dissociation (protein 283 mg/dL, normal cell count). Neurotropic virus panel was negative. Anti-GM1 IgG antibodies were detected — an antibody classically associated with axonal variants but identified here in a demyelinating phenotype. Nerve conduction studies showed prominent conduction block, prolonged distal latencies, slowed conduction velocities, and temporal dispersion in median and peroneal nerves, consistent with severe acquired demyelinating polyneuropathy. Respiratory compromise necessitated mechanical ventilation. Following seven sessions of plasmapheresis (40 mL/kg/session), the patient was extubated at day 7. Limb power improved to MRC 4/5. Repeat NCS at 4 weeks showed resolution of conduction block with partial normalization of distal latencies. At 3-month follow-up, full limb power was restored with only mild residual facial weakness. Conclusions This case demonstrates that PCEC antirabies vaccination can be associated with severe demyelinating GBS with atypical anti-GM1 IgG seropositivity. The descending pattern of weakness risks misdiagnosis — with botulism, diphtheria, and acute intermittent porphyria in the differential. A low threshold for early electrodiagnostic evaluation and plasmapheresis initiation is critical. As rabies vaccination expands globally, post-vaccination neurological surveillance protocols require strengthening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to report a severe demyelinating Guillain–Barré Syndrome (GBS) variant with anti-GM1 IgG antibodies following purified chick embryo cell (PCEC) antirabies vaccination, highlighting the need for vigilance in post-vaccination neurological decline. This is important because vaccine-associated GBS is rare but recognized, historically linked to older neural-tissue rabies vaccines.
Design and population
This is a single-patient observational report involving a 25-year-old woman who developed neurological symptoms three days after the third dose of PCEC antirabies vaccine administered following a monkey bite. The study included clinical, biochemical, cerebrospinal fluid, electrodiagnostic, antibody, and radiologic evaluations with follow-up at 3 months.
Core methods
The study involved clinical, biochemical, cerebrospinal fluid, electrodiagnostic, antibody, and radiologic assessments. Follow-up at 3 months was conducted to monitor the patient's recovery and neurological status.
Main results
CSF showed albuminocytologic dissociation (protein 283 mg/dL, normal cell count). Anti-GM1 IgG antibodies were detected, which are classically associated with axonal variants but were identified in a demyelinating phenotype. Nerve conduction studies revealed prominent conduction block, prolonged distal latencies, slowed conduction velocities, and temporal dispersion in median and peroneal nerves, consistent with severe acquired demyelinating polyneuropathy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that this case demonstrates that PCEC antirabies vaccination can be associated with severe demyelinating GBS with atypical anti-GM1 IgG seropositivity, which is a novel finding as anti-GM1 IgG is typically linked to axonal variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that post-vaccination neurological surveillance protocols need strengthening as rabies vaccination expands globally. Early electrodiagnostic evaluation and plasmapheresis initiation are critical for managing such cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single-patient observational report, which limits the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is strong due to the detailed clinical and diagnostic assessments provided.

来自巴西的未分类蝙蝠相关α冠状病毒中具有重塑受体结合界面的发散刺突蛋白

Divergent spike protein with a remodeled receptor-binding interface in an unclassified bat-associated alphacoronavirus from Brazil
作者： Rodrigo Lopes Sanz Duro, Raquel de Oliveira Vaz, Junior Olímpio Martins, Bruna Stefanie Silvério, Larissa Leão Férrer de Sousa, Clara Lacerda de Athayde, Louise Maranhão, Emiliano Esterci Ramalho, Nelda Deisy Boa, Mariana Dias Guilardi
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 蝙蝠拥有已知最丰富的α冠状病毒(alphaCoV)多样性,但这种多样性在新热带地区的样本仍然很少。表征不同的蝙蝠相关 alphaCoV 是了解冠状病毒 (CoV) 进化、刺突蛋白多样性以及 宿主 适应的分子基础的核心。我们对巴西蝙蝠和美洲虎样本的病毒组特征进行了表征,鉴定了之前未描述的与蝙蝠相关的 alphaCoV,并对一种未分类的 alphaCoV 的 Spike 蛋白进行了建模,并为其恢复了完整的 Spike 基因。方法 首先使用泛冠状病毒 PCR 检测对个体样本进行冠状病毒筛查,以及甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 和 狂犬病病毒 (RABV)。随后使用 Sanger 方法对 CoV 扩增子进行测序。然后汇集样本,并对蝙蝠组织(Molossus molossus 和 Uroderma bilobatum)和美洲虎(Panthera onca)的全血样本进行宏转录组测序。使用最大似然和贝叶斯系统发育推断、核苷酸相似性分析和重组筛选来组装、手动策划和分析病毒重叠群。使用 AlphaFold3 对完整的 Spike 蛋白进行建模,并通过结构叠加、静电表面绘图以及预测与人类受体血管紧张素转换酶 2 (ACE2) 和氨肽酶 N (APN) 的复合物,与实验确定的 alphaCoV Spike 结构进行比较。所有推论均基于序列和结构分析;未进行病毒分离、受体结合测定或细胞进入实验。结果 在 M. bilobatum 蝙蝠中检测到 AlphaCoV 序列,从中回收并单独分析了 13 个部分 alphaCoV 重叠群。系统发育分析将大多数这些序列置于不同的蝙蝠相关 alphaCoV 进化枝内,这些进化枝无法归属于任何公认的 ICTV 物种或亚属;相反,来自 U. bilobatum 的一段来自胃肠道的短序列聚集在与公认的 Amalacovirus 亚属相对应的进化枝内。回收的 Spike 基因与所有已表征的 alphaCoV 都高度不同,没有近期重组的证据。尽管存在这种差异,预测的刺突结构保留了整体受体结合结构域(RBD)折叠,同时表现出受体结合基序和表面静电特性的广泛重塑。AlphaFold3 预测了与 ACE2 和 APN 空间相容的复合物,但预测的界面是由与实验确定的人类 alphaCoV 复合物不同的残基集和几何形状形成的。据报道,从美洲虎全血中回收的非 CoV 序列与来源不确定的分类学匹配;它们没有建立感染,并且血液中没有冠状病毒序列并不能证明这些动物没有被感染。结论 这些发现扩展了巴西蝙蝠相关 αCoV 的已知多样性,并鉴定了无法归属于任何公认的 Alpha 冠状病毒属亚属的序列。尽管受体结合界面进行了广泛的重塑,但 RBD 折叠的保守性与 alphaCoV 的受体结合表面可以多样化而底层结构支架得以保留的模型是一致的。由于结构分析完全是计算性的,因此它们生成用于实验测试的假设,而不是人类受体使用的证据。总的来说,这些发现为 alphaCoV 进化 提供了新的见解,并为研究如何在保留底层结构支架的同时对 Spike 受体结合界面进行广泛的重塑提供了一个框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在表征巴西蝙蝠和美洲虎的病毒组特征,以识别以前未描述的与蝙蝠相关的α冠状病毒,并了解宿主适应冠状病毒进化的分子基础。
设计与对象
该研究涉及筛选来自巴西蝙蝠和美洲虎的样本,识别 alphaCoV,并对一种未分类的 alphaCoV 的 Spike 蛋白进行建模,并恢复了完整的 Spike 基因。
核心方法
使用泛冠状病毒 PCR 检测对单个样本进行冠状病毒筛查,并筛选甲型流感病毒和 狂犬病病毒。使用桑格方法对 CoV 扩增子进行测序,然后汇集样本,对蝙蝠组织和美洲虎全血样本进行宏转录组测序。
主要结果
在 Molossus molossus 蝙蝠中检测到了 AlphaCoV 序列,回收并分析了 13 个部分 alphaCoV 重叠群。来自 Uroderma bilobatum 的一个序列聚集在 Amalacovirus 亚属内,而其他序列则被放置在与蝙蝠相关的不同 alphaCoV 进化枝中,不能归属于任何公认的 ICTV 物种或亚属。
解释与创新
这些发现扩展了巴西已知的与蝙蝠相关的 alphaCoV 的多样性,并鉴定了无法归属于任何公认的 Alphacoronavirus 亚属的序列,凸显了新的 alphaCoV 谱系的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 alphaCoV 进化 的新见解,并为研究如何在保留底层结构支架的同时对 Spike 受体结合界面进行广泛重塑提供了一个框架。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到计算结构分析使用的限制,计算结构分析产生的假设而不是人类受体使用的实验证据。由于缺乏实验验证,证据强度中等。

Divergent spike protein with a remodeled receptor-binding interface in an unclassified bat-associated alphacoronavirus from Brazil

Authors: Rodrigo Lopes Sanz Duro, Raquel de Oliveira Vaz, Junior Olímpio Martins, Bruna Stefanie Silvério, Larissa Leão Férrer de Sousa, Clara Lacerda de Athayde, Louise Maranhão, Emiliano Esterci Ramalho, Nelda Deisy Boa, Mariana Dias Guilardi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Bats harbor the greatest known diversity of alphacoronaviruses (alphaCoVs), yet this diversity remains poorly sampled in the Neotropics. Characterizing divergent bat-associated alphaCoVs is central to understanding coronavirus (CoV) evolution, Spike protein diversification, and the molecular basis of host adaptation. We characterized the virome profiles of samples from Brazilian bats and jaguars, identified previously undescribed bat-associated alphaCoVs, and modeled the Spike protein of one unclassified alphaCoV for which a complete Spike gene was recovered. Methods Individual samples were first screened for CoVs using a pan-CoVs PCR assay, and for influenza A virus (IAV) and rabies virus (RABV). CoV amplicons were subsequently sequenced using the Sanger method. Samples were then pooled, and metatranscriptomic sequencing was performed on bat tissues ( Molossus molossus and Uroderma bilobatum ) and whole-blood samples from jaguar ( Panthera onca ). Viral contigs were assembled, manually curated, and analyzed using maximum-likelihood and Bayesian phylogenetic inference, nucleotide similarity profiling, and recombination screening. The complete Spike protein was modeled with AlphaFold3 and compared with experimentally determined alphaCoV Spike structures by structural superposition, electrostatic surface mapping, and prediction of complexes with the human receptors angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 (ACE2) and aminopeptidase N (APN). All inferences are based on sequence and structural analysis; no virus isolation, receptor-binding assay or cell-entry experiment was performed. Results AlphaCoV sequences were detected in M. bilobatum bats, from which 13 partial alphaCoV contigs were recovered and analyzed individually. Phylogenetic analyses placed most of these sequences within divergent bat-associated alphaCoV clades that could not be assigned to any recognized ICTV species or subgenus; one short gastrointestinal tract-derived sequence from U. bilobatum instead clustered within the clade corresponding to the recognized subgenus Amalacovirus. The recovered Spike gene was highly divergent from all characterized alphaCoVs, with no evidence of recent recombination. Despite this divergence, the predicted Spike structure retained the overall receptor-binding domain (RBD) fold, while exhibiting extensive remodeling of the receptor-binding motif and of surface electrostatic properties. AlphaFold3 predicted sterically compatible complexes with both ACE2 and APN, but the predicted interfaces were formed by residue sets and geometries distinct from those of experimentally determined human alphaCoV complexes. Non-CoV sequences recovered from jaguar whole blood were reported as taxonomic matches of uncertain provenance; they do not establish infection, and the absence of CoV sequences in blood does not establish that these animals were uninfected. Conclusions These findings extend the known diversity of bat-associated alphaCoVs in Brazil and identify sequences that could not be assigned to any recognized subgenus of the genus Alphacoronavirus. The conservation of the RBD fold, despite extensive remodeling of the receptor-binding interface, is consistent with a model in which the receptor-binding surface of alphaCoVs can diversify while the underlying structural scaffold is retained. Because the structural analyses are entirely computational, they generate hypotheses for experimental testing rather than evidence of human receptor usage. Overall, these findings provide new insights into alphaCoV evolution and offer a framework for investigating how extensive remodeling of the Spike receptor-binding interface can occur while preserving the underlying structural scaffold.

Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the virome profiles of Brazilian bats and jaguars to identify previously undescribed bat-associated alphacoronaviruses and understand the molecular basis of host adaptation for coronavirus evolution.
Design and population
The study involved screening samples from Brazilian bats and jaguars, identifying alphaCoVs, and modeling the Spike protein of one unclassified alphaCoV with a complete Spike gene recovered.
Core methods
Individual samples were screened for coronaviruses using a pan-CoVs PCR assay, and for influenza A virus and rabies virus. CoV amplicons were sequenced using the Sanger method, and samples were then pooled for metatranscriptomic sequencing of bat tissues and jaguar whole-blood samples.
Main results
AlphaCoV sequences were detected in Molossus molossus bats, with 13 partial alphaCoV contigs recovered and analyzed. One sequence from Uroderma bilobatum clustered within the Amalacovirus subgenus, while the others were placed in divergent bat-associated alphaCoV clades not assignable to any recognized ICTV species or subgenus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings extend the known diversity of bat-associated alphaCoVs in Brazil and identify sequences that could not be assigned to any recognized subgenus of Alphacoronavirus, highlighting the potential for new alphaCoV lineages.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide new insights into alphaCoV evolution and offer a framework for investigating how extensive remodeling of the Spike receptor-binding interface can occur while preserving the underlying structural scaffold.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of computational structural analysis, which generates hypotheses rather than experimental evidence of human receptor usage. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of experimental validation.

哥斯达黎加首次检测到犬瘟热病毒在斑纹松鼠(Sciurus variegatoides)和豪猪(Coendou mexicanus)中的感染

First detection of canine distemper virus infection in varie¬gated squirrels (Sciurus variegatoides) and porcupines (Coendou mexicanus) in Costa Rica
作者： B. León, C. Wiederkehr, R. Cruz, M. Cordero, A. Chinchilla, T. Solorzano-Scott, L. Brenes, F. Aguilar-Vargas
摘要中文翻译犬瘟热病毒(CDV)是一种多宿主病原体,能够感染多种家养和野生动物,然而,关于其在松鼠和豪猪中的感染报告仍然很少。作为哥斯达黎加野生动物监测项目的一部分,对六只表现出神经系统症状的野生哺乳动物(四只Sciurus variegatoides和两只Coendou mexicanus)进行了评估以排除某种病原体。所有脑组织样本通过直接免疫荧光和RT-PCR检测均未发现该病原体。对豪猪的结膜液和鼻分泌物进行的快速犬瘟热抗原检测呈阳性,随后这些豪猪被安乐死。五只动物因严重的自溶作用而无法进行组织病理学评估;然而,一只松鼠表现出严重的中性粒细胞性肺炎伴有坏死和出血,肺间质中有中度的淋巴浆细胞浸润,支气管上皮细胞中有胞浆内包涵体,中脑区域有局灶性中性粒细胞性浸润。这些发现与CDV感染伴随严重的系统性细菌感染一致。通过RT-PCR和测序确认了其中两只松鼠和两只豪猪感染了CDV。系统发育分析显示,哥斯达黎加的序列分为两个不同的簇:一个簇包含松鼠,另一个簇包含豪猪。所有四个序列都与美国2012–2013年检测到的犬瘟热病毒株有共同祖先,并且与阿根廷2005年检测到的犬瘟热病毒株有较远的亲缘关系。这些病例是全球少数记录的CDV在松鼠和豪猪中的感染案例之一,也是哥斯达黎加首次报告,强调了继续进行分子监测以明确病毒谱系动态和传播途径的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
犬瘟热病毒(CDV)是一种多宿主病原体,能够感染多种家养和野生动物,然而,关于其在松鼠和豪猪中的感染报告仍然很少。
设计与对象
作为哥斯达黎加野生动物监测项目的一部分,对六只表现出神经系统症状的野生哺乳动物(四只Sciurus variegatoides和两只Coendou mexicanus)进行了评估以排除某种病原体。
核心方法
所有脑组织样本通过直接免疫荧光和RT-PCR检测均未发现该病原体。通过RT-PCR和测序确认了其中两只松鼠和两只豪猪感染了CDV。
主要结果
对豪猪的结膜液和鼻分泌物进行的快速犬瘟热抗原检测呈阳性,随后这些豪猪被安乐死。五只动物因严重的自溶作用而无法进行组织病理学评估;然而,一只松鼠表现出严重的中性粒细胞性肺炎伴有坏死和出血,肺间质中有中度的淋巴浆细胞浸润,支气管上皮细胞中有胞浆内包涵体,中脑区域有局灶性中性粒细胞性浸润。
解释与创新
这些发现与CDV感染伴随严重的系统性细菌感染一致。这些病例是全球少数记录的CDV在松鼠和豪猪中的感染案例之一,也是哥斯达黎加首次报告,强调了继续进行分子监测以明确病毒谱系动态和传播途径的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现突显了分子监测在理解CDV谱系动态和传播途径中的重要性,这可以为疾病预防和控制的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告研究的局限性,但这些发现得到了一种动物的分子确认和组织病理学分析的支持,表明结果具有中等程度的可信度。

First detection of canine distemper virus infection in varie¬gated squirrels (Sciurus variegatoides) and porcupines (Coendou mexicanus) in Costa Rica

Authors: B. León, C. Wiederkehr, R. Cruz, M. Cordero, A. Chinchilla, T. Solorzano-Scott, L. Brenes, F. Aguilar-Vargas
Original abstract (English)
Canine distemper virus (CDV) is a multi-host pathogen capable of infecting a wide range of do-mestic and wild mammals, however, reports of infection in squirrels and porcupines remain scarce. As part of the wildlife rabies surveillance programme in Costa Rica, six wild mammals (four Sciurus variegatoides and two Coendou mexicanus) with neurological signs were evaluated to rule out rabies. All brain samples tested negative for rabies by direct immunofluorescence and RT-PCR. Rapid distemper antigen tests of ocular and nasal secretions were positive in both porcupines, which were subsequently euthanised. Advanced autolysis prevented histopathological evaluation in five of the animals; however, one squirrel showed severe neutrophilic pneumonia with necrosis and haemorrhage, a moderate lymphoplasmacytic infiltrate in the pulmonary interstitium, intracytoplasmic inclusion bodies in bronchial epithelium, and a focal neutrophilic infiltrate in the midbrain. The findings were consistent with CDV infection accompanied by severe systemic bacterial infection. CDV was confirmed in two of the four squirrels and in the two porcupines by RT-PCR and sequencing. Phylogenetic analysis revealed that the Costa Rican sequences grouped into two distinct clusters: one containing both squirrels and another with both porcupines. All four sequences shared a common ancestor with CDV strains detected in domestic dogs in the United States (2012–2013), and more distantly with a dog strain from Argentina (2005). These cases represent one of the few documented occurrences of CDV in squirrels and porcupines worldwide and the first reported in Costa Rica, underscoring the need for continued molecular surveillance to clarify lineage dynamics and spillover pathways.

Question and background
Canine distemper virus (CDV) is a multi-host pathogen capable of infecting a wide range of domestic and wild mammals, however, reports of infection in squirrels and porcupines remain scarce.
Design and population
As part of the wildlife rabies surveillance programme in Costa Rica, six wild mammals (four Sciurus variegatoides and two Coendou mexicanus) with neurological signs were evaluated to rule out rabies.
Core methods
All brain samples tested negative for rabies by direct immunofluorescence and RT-PCR. CDV was confirmed in two of the four squirrels and in the two porcupines by RT-PCR and sequencing.
Main results

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of molecular surveillance in understanding CDV lineage dynamics and spillover pathways, which can inform public health strategies for disease prevention and control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study, but the findings are supported by molecular confirmation and histopathological analysis of one animal, suggesting moderate confidence in the results.

用于时空风险分析的疾病不可知数据模型和可插入模块接口:基于兽医 监测 数据评估的开放参考实现

A Disease-Agnostic Data Model and Pluggable Module Interface for Spatio-Temporal Risk Analytics: An Open Reference Implementation Evaluated on Veterinary Surveillance Data
作者： Ablaikhan S. Kadyrov, Дина Сатыбалдина, Нурлан Наркенович Ташатов, Tolegen Aidynov, D. Satybaldina, N. Tashatov
摘要中文翻译动物健康时空风险分析 监测 依赖于既定方法——最大熵 (MaxEnt) 适宜性建模、时空扫描统计、主成分分析 (PCA) 和贝叶斯 患病率 估计——通常作为孤立的、特定于疾病的工作流程运行。本文提出了一个与疾病无关的事件模式和一个类型化的插件接口,以及一个开源的 Python 参考实现。完整的注册表摄取和建模工作流程使用分别包含 4183 和 363 条地理参考记录的原始炭疽和 狂犬病 注册表进行了测试。然而,PCA 和贝叶斯组件使用了从早期出版物中重建的聚合,因此这些测试评估的是接口一致性和数值再现,而不是独立验证。使用相同的输入,扫描组件再现了主要的 SaTScan 簇,产生的 Jaccard 指数为 0.91 和 1.00,尽管它与 13 个重要炭疽簇中的 8 个匹配。空间块交叉验证产生的炭疽病接收者操作特征曲线 (AUC) 值下的面积值为 0.74–0.77,而 elapid MaxEnt 实现的值为 0.67–0.70;确切的数字取决于折叠分配和正则化。大约两个数量级的运行时差距也仅适用于那些特定的实现、功能集和数据集。添加第五种疾病需要一个 37 行源加载器,并且无需对 1034 行共享代码进行编辑。这些结果表明,该系统可以在一台工作站上运行,但数据库支持的部署、操作扩展和前瞻性预测尚未经过测试。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发用于时空风险分析的疾病不可知数据模型和可插入模块接口,解决动物健康 监测 依赖孤立的、特定于疾病的工作流程的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究使用原始 anthrax 和 狂犬病 注册表分别评估了开源 Python 参考实现,其中包含 4183 条和 363 条地理参考记录,以测试完整的注册表摄取和建模工作流程。
核心方法
该系统使用原始炭疽和 狂犬病 注册表进行了测试,PCA 和贝叶斯组件使用从早期出版物重建的聚合来评估界面一致性和数值再现。
主要结果
扫描组件再现了 Jaccard 指数为 0.91 和 1.00 的主要 SaTScan 簇,尽管它仅与 13 个重要炭疽簇中的 8 个匹配。空间块交叉验证得出的炭疽 AUC 值为 0.74-0.77,而 elapid MaxEnt 实现的 AUC 值为 0.67-0.70。
解释与创新
结果证明了系统在单个工作站上运行的能力,突出了其模块化设计和集成到现有 监测 框架中的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,该系统可以提高动物健康 监测 时空风险分析的效率和灵活性,支持更好的疾病监测和应对策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有测试数据库支持的部署、操作扩展或前瞻性预测,并且运行时差距仅适用于特定的实现、功能集和数据集。

A Disease-Agnostic Data Model and Pluggable Module Interface for Spatio-Temporal Risk Analytics: An Open Reference Implementation Evaluated on Veterinary Surveillance Data

Authors: Ablaikhan S. Kadyrov, Дина Сатыбалдина, Нурлан Наркенович Ташатов, Tolegen Aidynov, D. Satybaldina, N. Tashatov
Original abstract (English)
Spatio-temporal risk analytics for animal-health surveillance relies on established methods—maximum entropy (MaxEnt) suitability modeling, space–time scan statistics, principal component analysis (PCA), and Bayesian prevalence estimation—usually run as isolated, disease-specific workflows. This paper presents a disease-agnostic event schema and a typed plug-in interface, with an open-source Python reference implementation. The full registry-ingestion and modeling workflow was tested with raw anthrax and rabies registries containing 4183 and 363 georeferenced records, respectively. The PCA and Bayesian components, however, used aggregates reconstructed from earlier publications, so those tests assessed interface conformance and numerical reproduction rather than independent validation. With the same inputs, the scan component reproduced the primary SaTScan clusters, yielding Jaccard indices of 0.91 and 1.00, although it matched 8 of 13 significant anthrax clusters overall. Spatial block cross-validation produced produced area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) values values of 0.74–0.77 for anthrax, compared with 0.67–0.70 for the elapid MaxEnt implementation; the exact figures depended on fold assignment and regularization. The roughly two-orders-of-magnitude runtime gap also applies only to those particular implementations, feature sets, and datasets. Adding a fifth disease required a single 37-line source loader, with no edits to the 1034 lines of shared code. These results show that the system can run on one workstation, but database-backed deployment, operational scaling, and prospective forecasting were not tested.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a disease-agnostic data model and pluggable module interface for spatio-temporal risk analytics, addressing the challenge of relying on isolated, disease-specific workflows for animal-health surveillance.
Design and population
The study evaluated an open-source Python reference implementation using raw anthrax and rabies registries with 4183 and 363 georeferenced records, respectively, to test the full registry-ingestion and modeling workflow.
Core methods
The system was tested with raw anthrax and rabies registries, and the PCA and Bayesian components used aggregates reconstructed from earlier publications to assess interface conformance and numerical reproduction.
Main results
The scan component reproduced the primary SaTScan clusters with Jaccard indices of 0.91 and 1.00, although it matched only 8 of 13 significant anthrax clusters. Spatial block cross-validation yielded AUC values of 0.74–0.77 for anthrax, compared with 0.67–0.70 for the elapid MaxEnt implementation.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate the system's ability to run on a single workstation, highlighting its modular design and potential for integration into existing surveillance frameworks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the system can improve the efficiency and flexibility of spatio-temporal risk analytics for animal-health surveillance, supporting better disease monitoring and response strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not test database-backed deployment, operational scaling, or prospective forecasting, and the runtime gap applies only to specific implementations, feature sets, and datasets.
