犬脂肪间充质干细胞制剂的微生物安全评估显示没有可检测到的污染

Microbiological safety assessment of canine adipose derived mesenchymal stem cell preparations reveal no detectable contamination
作者： Marina Prišlin Šimac, V. Kunić, D. Brnić, Daria Jurković Žilić, Relja Beck, Sanja Duvnjak, M. Zadravec, Vjekoslava Kostanić
摘要中文翻译犬脂肪源性间充质干细胞/基质细胞(cAD-MSC)的实验应用已证明治疗对许多以前无法治疗或难以治疗的疾病具有优异的治疗潜力。然而,用于临床应用的cAD-MSC的生产需要体外分离和细胞扩增,从而引入微生物污染的风险。研究表明,储存的间充质干细胞可能被细菌、真菌或病毒污染,导致细胞转化、细胞死亡或持续感染。这最终会损害治疗效果并可能促进治疗诱导的病原体 传播。根据欧洲药品管理局指南,要求将无菌检测作为生产过程的一个组成部分,本研究旨在评估克罗地亚兽医研究所 cAD-MSC 库的无菌性。为了检测临床上最重要的犬病原体,从 32 个 cAD-MSC 制剂中提取核酸,并使用标准分子 PCR 方法进行分析。病原体筛查小组包括狂犬病病毒(狂犬病毒)、犬疱疹病毒(Varicellovirus canidalpha1)、细小病毒(Carnivore protoparvovirus 1)、冠状病毒(Alphacoronavirus 1)、犬瘟热病毒(Morbillivirus canis)、轮状病毒A、钩端螺旋体属、犬布鲁氏菌、伯氏疏螺旋体、无形体spp、埃利希体属、支原体属(嗜血性)和犬新孢子虫。没有检测到上述病原体的测试基因片段,表明cAD-MSC制剂不存在临床上显着的病原体。在细胞分离和扩增过程中严格实施无菌控制对于确保犬类患者的安全细胞治疗至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估犬脂肪源性间充质干细胞 (cAD-MSC) 制剂的微生物安全性,因为其治疗应用需要确保它们不受污染,以免影响疗效或给犬类患者带来风险。
设计与对象
该研究在克罗地亚兽医研究所进行,使用分子 PCR 方法测试了 32 种 cAD-MSC 制剂是否存在临床上重要的病原体,包括 狂犬病病毒。
核心方法
从 32 个 cAD-MSC 制剂中提取核酸,并使用标准分子 PCR 方法进行分析,以检测特定病原体(包括 狂犬病病毒)的存在。
主要结果
在 32 个 cAD-MSC 制剂中未检测到 狂犬病病毒 或任何其他测试病原体的基因片段,表明它们没有污染。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,cAD-MSC 制剂满足无菌要求,这是对其在犬类患者治疗中的安全性的新确认。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为确保兽医学中基于细胞的疗法的安全性提供了关键信息,特别是 狂犬病病毒,它是犬类群体中一个重大的公共卫生问题。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于测试一组特定的病原体,并且测试样本中没有污染并不能保证完全安全,尽管考虑到严格的测试方案,证据是强有力的。

Microbiological safety assessment of canine adipose derived mesenchymal stem cell preparations reveal no detectable contamination

Authors: Marina Prišlin Šimac, V. Kunić, D. Brnić, Daria Jurković Žilić, Relja Beck, Sanja Duvnjak, M. Zadravec, Vjekoslava Kostanić
Original abstract (English)
The experimental application of canine adipose-derived mesenchymal stem/stromal cells (cAD-MSC) has demonstrated excellent therapeutic potential in the treatment of a number of previously untreatable or difficult-to-treat diseases. However, the production of cAD-MSC for clinical application necessitates in vitro isolation and cell expansion, introducing the risk of microbiological contamination. Studies indicate that stored MSCs may be contaminated with bacteria, fungi, or viruses, resulting in cell transformation, cell death, or persistent infection. This ultimately compromises therapeutic efficacy and potentially facilitates therapy-induced pathogen transmission. In accordance with the European Medicines Agency guidelines mandating sterility testing as an integral component of the manufacturing process, this study aimed to assess the sterility of the cAD-MSC bank at the Croatian Veterinary Institute. To test for the most clinically significant canine pathogens, nucleic acids were extracted from 32 cAD-MSC preparations and analyzed using standard molecular PCR methods. The pathogen screening panel included rabies virus (Lyssavirus), canine herpesvirus (Varicellovirus canidalpha1), parvovirus (Carnivore protoparvovirus 1), coronavirus (Alphacoronavirus 1), distemper virus (Morbillivirus canis), Rotavirus A, Leptospira spp, Brucella canis, Borrelia burgdorferi sensu lato, Anaplasma spp, Ehrlichia spp, Mycoplasma spp (hemotropic), and Neospora caninum. No tested gene fragments of the aforementioned pathogens were detected, indicating that the cAD-MSC preparations were free from the presence of clinically significant pathogens. Rigorous implementation of sterility control during the cell isolation and expansion process is essential to ensure safe cell-based therapy in canine patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the microbiological safety of canine adipose-derived mesenchymal stem cell (cAD-MSC) preparations, as their therapeutic application requires ensuring they are free from contamination that could compromise efficacy or pose risks to canine patients.
Design and population
The study was conducted at the Croatian Veterinary Institute, where 32 cAD-MSC preparations were tested for the presence of clinically significant pathogens, including rabies virus, using molecular PCR methods.
Core methods
Nucleic acids were extracted from the 32 cAD-MSC preparations and analyzed using standard molecular PCR methods to detect the presence of the specified pathogens, including rabies virus.
Main results
No gene fragments of rabies virus or any other tested pathogens were detected in the 32 cAD-MSC preparations, indicating they were free from contamination.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the cAD-MSC preparations met sterility requirements, which is a novel confirmation of their safety for therapeutic use in canine patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical information for ensuring the safety of cell-based therapies in veterinary medicine, particularly for rabies virus, which is a significant public health concern in canine populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to testing a specific set of pathogens, and the absence of contamination in the tested samples does not guarantee complete safety, though the evidence is strong given the rigorous testing protocol.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

狂犬病病毒 基因组学的最新进展,特别强调免疫原性糖蛋白胞外域:结构、进化 和干预意义

Recent Advances in Rabies Virus Genomics with Special Emphasis on Immunogenic Glycoprotein Ectodomain: Structure, Evolution, and Intervention Implications
作者： Richard Vijee Kennady Mervin, Mani Saminathan, Padma Kumar Aswin, Shubham Deshmukh, Rushikesh Suresh Harne, Paramasivam Raja, Obli Rajendran Vinodh Kumar, Vinay Ganeshrao Joshi, Sonal Saxena, Sudha Rani Kripal
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 仍然是一种致命的脑炎,每年夺去约 59,000 人的生命,负担集中在亚洲和非洲的低收入和中等收入国家。预防几乎完全依赖于 疫苗接种 和 暴露后预防 (PEP) 方案,这些方案可引发针对病毒糖蛋白 (G) 的病毒中和抗体。本综述综合了 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 基因组学和结构病毒学的最新进展,特别强调 G 胞外域、唯一的表面暴露病毒成分和保护性免疫的主要免疫学靶点。这篇综述研究了 RABV 进化 跨越系统发育上不同的狂犬病病毒谱系、G 基因内编码的毒力和神经侵袭性的分子决定因素,以及通过当代冷冻电子显微镜和晶体学对胞外域的结构表征。高分辨率结构研究已经解析了中和抗体参与的融合前三聚体的四级组织,表明这些表位的构象稳定性是 疫苗 诱导免疫的重要决定因素,2026 年报道的九种抗体复合物的冷冻电子显微镜将抗原图谱扩展到了 6 个位点,其中两个是新定义的,在狂犬病毒中异常保守,并被最广泛的交叉反应抗体识别尚未描述。然后,应用文献围绕该蛋白质的三种转化用途进行组织:其在基于胞外域的 血清学 和抗原检测中的诊断应用,其在替代血清源免疫球蛋白的单克隆抗体混合物中的治疗应用,以及其在灭活、亚基、病毒样颗粒、载体和核酸平台上的疫苗应用。因此,胞外域结构支撑了下一代 疫苗、改进的暴露后治疗和诊断,并由此实现到 2030 年狗介导的 狂犬病 人类零死亡的全球目标。

范围与问题
狂犬病 仍然是一种致命的脑炎,每年夺去约 59,000 人的生命,负担集中在亚洲和非洲的低收入和中等收入国家。
证据基础与方法
预防几乎完全依赖于 疫苗接种 和 暴露后预防 (PEP) 方案,这些方案可引发针对病毒糖蛋白 (G) 的病毒中和抗体。
共识与结论
本综述综合了 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 基因组学和结构病毒学的最新进展,特别强调 G 胞外域、唯一的表面暴露病毒成分和保护性免疫的主要免疫学靶点。
争议与缺口
这篇综述研究了 RABV 进化 跨越系统发育上不同的狂犬病病毒谱系、G 基因内编码的毒力和神经侵袭性的分子决定因素,以及通过当代冷冻电子显微镜和晶体学对胞外域的结构表征。
科研与实践启示
高分辨率结构研究已经解析了中和抗体参与的融合前三聚体的四级组织,表明这些表位的构象稳定性是 疫苗 诱导免疫的重要决定因素,2026 年报道的九种抗体复合物的冷冻电子显微镜将抗原图谱扩展到了 6 个位点,其中两个是新定义的,在狂犬病毒中异常保守,并被最广泛的交叉反应抗体识别尚未描述。

Recent Advances in Rabies Virus Genomics with Special Emphasis on Immunogenic Glycoprotein Ectodomain: Structure, Evolution, and Intervention Implications

Authors: Richard Vijee Kennady Mervin, Mani Saminathan, Padma Kumar Aswin, Shubham Deshmukh, Rushikesh Suresh Harne, Paramasivam Raja, Obli Rajendran Vinodh Kumar, Vinay Ganeshrao Joshi, Sonal Saxena, Sudha Rani Kripal
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains an invariably fatal encephalitis claiming approximately 59,000 human lives annually, with the burden concentrated in low- and middle-income countries of Asia and Africa. Prevention depends almost exclusively upon vaccination and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) regimens that elicit virus-neutralizing antibodies directed against the viral glycoprotein (G). This review synthesizes recent advances in rabies virus (RABV) genomics and structural virology with particular emphasis upon the G ectodomain, the sole surface-exposed viral component and the primary immunological target of protective immunity. This review examines the RABV evolution across phylogenetically distinct lyssavirus lineages, the molecular determinants of virulence and neuroinvasiveness encoded within the G gene, and structural characterization of the ectodomain by contemporary cryo-electron microscopy and crystallography. High-resolution structural studies have resolved the quaternary organization of the prefusion trimer engaged by neutralizing antibodies, indicating that the conformational stability of these epitopes is an important determinant of vaccine-induced immunity, and cryo-electron microscopy of nine antibody complexes reported in 2026 has extended the antigenic map to six sites, two of them newly defined, unusually well conserved across lyssaviruses and recognized by the most broadly cross-reactive antibodies yet described. The applied literature is then organized around the three translational uses of the protein: its diagnostic application in ectodomain-based serology and antigen detection, its therapeutic application in monoclonal antibody cocktails replacing serum-derived immunoglobulin, and its vaccinal application across inactivated, subunit, virus-like particle, vectored, and nucleic acid platforms. Ectodomain architecture therefore underpins next-generation vaccines, improved post-exposure therapeutics and diagnostics, and with them the global target of zero human deaths from dog-mediated rabies by 2030.

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
This review examines the RABV evolution across phylogenetically distinct lyssavirus lineages, the molecular determinants of virulence and neuroinvasiveness encoded within the G gene, and structural characterization of the ectodomain by contemporary cryo-electron microscopy and crystallography.
Research and practice implications
High-resolution structural studies have resolved the quaternary organization of the prefusion trimer engaged by neutralizing antibodies, indicating that the conformational stability of these epitopes is an important determinant of vaccine-induced immunity, and cryo-electron microscopy of nine antibody complexes reported in 2026 has extended the antigenic map to six sites, two of them newly defined, unusually well conserved across lyssaviruses and recognized by the most broadly cross-reactive antibodies yet described.

对土耳其 狂犬病 暴露后预防实践的系统回顾:14 年回顾性评估(2010-2024 年)

A systematic review of prophylaxis practices after rabies exposure in Türkiye: a 14-year retrospective evaluation (2010–2024)
作者： Semsi Nur Karabela, Muhammet Salih Tarhan, Ruveyda Alacahan-Durer, Kursad Nuri Baydili, Kadriye Kart-Yasar, Şemsi Nur Karabela, Kadriye Kart Yaşar, Ş. Karabela
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 狂犬病 是一种致命的病毒性人畜共患疾病。尽管土耳其制定了综合人类、家畜和野生动物 疫苗接种 计划以及 暴露后预防 (PEP) 服务,但人类 狂犬病 病例每年仍然发生。目的 本综述系统地研究了土耳其疑似 狂犬病 暴露后 暴露后预防 (PEP) 的现状,评估了 狂犬病 控制实践和文献中的预防数据。方法 根据 PRISMA 2020 指南,使用 2010 年至 2024 年间在土耳其发表的 43 项关于 狂犬病 预防的观察性研究的数据进行了描述性证据综合,这些数据检索自 PubMed、Web of Science、TR Dizin 和本地存储库等数据库。结果 所有纳入的研究均为回顾性研究,报告了 111,677 份报告;在有可用预防数据的 110,094 人中,92.5% 接受了 PEP。与流浪动物接触的占67.8%,与自养但未接种疫苗的动物接触的占18.3%。4 剂 疫苗 方案是最常见的,36.2% 的病例使用了 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG)。值得注意的是,81.5% 的人在 24 小时内寻求 PEP。3.84% 的病例报告了副作用,包括发烧、局部肿胀和红斑。结论 虽然土耳其的 狂犬病 管控措施取得了实质性进展,但 PEP 管理的地区差异仍然明显。观察到的预防完成情况的差异、延迟就诊(> 24 小时)和不一致的 RIG 管理凸显了持续的公共卫生挑战。由于纳入的研究没有直接评估这些模式的原因,因此指南遵守情况、公众意识和区域服务组织等因素值得进一步调查。持续的专业培训、公共卫生推广和流浪动物种群管理可能有助于优化 狂犬病 预防策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
This review focuses on 狂犬病病毒 and 狂犬病 disease.核心问题是在土耳其疑似 狂犬病 暴露后对 暴露后预防 (PEP) 实践进行评估,检查 狂犬病 控制实践和文献中的预防数据。
证据基础与方法
该综述遵循 PRISMA 2020 指南,并使用 2010 年至 2024 年间发表的土耳其 狂犬病 预防的 43 项观察性研究的数据进行了描述性证据综合。这些研究是从 PubMed、Web of Science、TR Dizin 和本地存储库等数据库中检索的。
共识与结论
所有纳入的研究均为回顾性研究,且92.5%的暴露后预防数据可获得者接受了暴露后预防(PEP)。接触流浪动物占病例的67.8%,而接触已拥有但未接种疫苗的动物占18.3%。4剂疫苗方案是最常见的方案,免疫球蛋白(RIG)在36.2%的病例中被使用。值得注意的是,81.5%的个体在24小时内寻求了PEP,且有3.84%的病例报告了不良反应。
争议与缺口
纳入的研究并未直接评估预防完成情况变化、延迟就诊(> 24 小时)和 RIG 给药不一致的原因。这些模式可能受到指南遵守情况、公众意识和区域服务组织等因素的影响,需要进一步调查。
科研与实践启示
持续的专业培训、公共卫生推广和流浪动物种群管理可能有助于优化 狂犬病 预防策略。观察到的预防完成情况的差异、延迟就诊和 RIG 管理不一致凸显了持续的公共卫生挑战。

A systematic review of prophylaxis practices after rabies exposure in Türkiye: a 14-year retrospective evaluation (2010–2024)

Authors: Semsi Nur Karabela, Muhammet Salih Tarhan, Ruveyda Alacahan-Durer, Kursad Nuri Baydili, Kadriye Kart-Yasar, Şemsi Nur Karabela, Kadriye Kart Yaşar, Ş. Karabela
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Rabies is a fatal viral zoonotic disease. Despite Türkiye’s integrated human, domestic animal, and wildlife vaccination programs alongside post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) services, human rabies cases continue to occur annually. Aim This review systematically examines the current state of Post-Exposure Prophylaxis (PEP) following suspected rabies exposure in Türkiye, evaluating rabies control practices and prophylaxis data from the literature. Method Following PRISMA 2020 guidelines, a descriptive evidence synthesis was conducted using data from 43 observational studies on rabies prophylaxis in Türkiye published between 2010 and 2024, retrieved from databases including PubMed, Web of Science, TR Dizin, and local repositories. Results All included studies were retrospective and reported 111,677 presentations; among the 110,094 individuals with available prophylaxis data, 92.5% received PEP. Contact with stray animals accounted for 67.8%, while contact with owned but unvaccinated animals was 18.3%. The 4-dose vaccine regimen was the most common, and rabies immunoglobulin (RIG) was administered in 36.2% of cases. Notably, 81.5% of individuals sought PEP within 24 h. Side effects, including fever, local swelling, and erythema, were reported in 3.84% of cases. Conclusion Although rabies control measures in Türkiye have achieved substantial progress, regional disparities in PEP administration remain evident. Observed variations in prophylaxis completion, delayed presentation (> 24 h), and inconsistent RIG administration highlight ongoing public health challenges. As the included studies did not directly assess the causes of these patterns, factors such as guideline adherence, public awareness, and regional service organization warrant further investigation. Continuous professional training, public health outreach, and stray animal population management may help optimize rabies prophylaxis strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease. The central question is the evaluation of post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) practices following suspected rabies exposure in Türkiye, examining rabies control practices and prophylaxis data from the literature.
Evidence base and method
The review followed PRISMA 2020 guidelines and conducted a descriptive evidence synthesis using data from 43 observational studies on rabies prophylaxis in Türkiye published between 2010 and 2024. These studies were retrieved from databases including PubMed, Web of Science, TR Dizin, and local repositories.
Consensus and conclusions
All included studies were retrospective, and 92.5% of individuals with available prophylaxis data received PEP. Contact with stray animals accounted for 67.8% of cases, while contact with owned but unvaccinated animals was 18.3%. The 4-dose vaccine regimen was the most common, and rabies immunoglobulin (RIG) was administered in 36.2% of cases. Notably, 81.5% of individuals sought PEP within 24 h, and side effects were reported in 3.84% of cases.
Controversies and gaps
The included studies did not directly assess the causes of variations in prophylaxis completion, delayed presentation (> 24 h), and inconsistent RIG administration. These patterns may be influenced by factors such as guideline adherence, public awareness, and regional service organization, which require further investigation.
Research and practice implications
Continuous professional training, public health outreach, and stray animal population management may help optimize rabies prophylaxis strategies. The observed variations in prophylaxis completion, delayed presentation, and inconsistent RIG administration highlight ongoing public health challenges.

描述印度动物福利组织中自由放养犬的收容所接纳情况、停留时间和结果

Characterizing shelter intake, length of stay, and outcomes among free-ranging dogs in animal welfare organizations in India
作者： Rubina Mondal, Alexandra Protopopova, Anindita Bhadra
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:全球南方国家承受着全球散养狗种群不受管理和研究不足的经济和社会负担。然而,兽医、住房科学和行为研究领域主要由北半球国家的研究主导,并且迎合了片面的观点。在这项探索性研究中,我们通过分析超过 24,000 条记录的不同数据,调查了印度动物救援组织接纳的自由放养狗的摄入量和结果模式,试图弥合这一差距。我们确定了 2018 年至 2022 年间印度四个地理位置和机构不同的动物收容所的入院原因、临床负担和动物存活率的关键趋势。结果 - 在研究期间,收容所的平均收容量每年增长约 35%。无论气候或城市化的地区差异如何,身体创伤都成为所有地点入院的主要驱动因素。虽然大多数庇护所的生命结果通常超过非生命结果,但非生命结果(死亡和安乐死)的发生率很高(40-60%),远高于报告的全球北方基准。生存与临床表现密切相关:神经系统病例,最有可能与地方性 狂犬病 和犬瘟热有关,预后最差,中位生存时间仅为一天。全身、胃肠道和肌肉骨骼疾病对非生命结果也具有显着更高的风险。总体中位住院时间为 8 天,但住院时间差异很大,其中肿瘤病例需要最长的护理。结论-研究结果表明,印度收容所并不是作为下游安全网的重新安置中心,因为动物福利危机源于缺乏上游种群管理、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和伤害预防基础设施。此外,该研究指出缺乏标准化数据收集是循证政策的根本障碍,强调迫切需要共享数据标准,以改善整个印度动物救援部门的动物福利和公共卫生结果。最终,这项研究旨在为评估南半球国家和其他面临类似问题的国家的收容所科学和犬类福利提供基础证据基础和概念验证。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究的重点是描述印度动物福利组织中自由放养狗的收容所接纳情况、停留时间和结果。核心问题是了解这些庇护所的入院模式、存活率以及影响结果的因素,重点是上游人口管理、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和伤害预防基础设施的作用。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一项探索性研究,分析了 2018 年至 2022 年间印度四个地理和机构不同的动物收容所的 24,000 多个记录的不同数据。研究设计包括审查入院原因、临床负担和存活率,但没有明确提及荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
该研究发现,无论气候或城市化的地区差异如何,身体创伤都是所有地点入院的主要驱动因素。它还报告说,非生命结果(死亡和安乐死)的发生率很高(40-60%),远高于报告的全球北方基准。此外,生存率与临床表现密切相关,神经系统病例可能与 狂犬病 和犬瘟热有关,预后最差,中位生存时间仅为一天。
争议与缺口
该研究强调,缺乏标准化数据收集是循证政策的根本障碍,这是一个关键的证据差距。它还指出,调查结果将印度收容所描述为动物福利危机的下游安全网,这些危机源于缺乏上游种群管理、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和伤害预防基础设施。
科研与实践启示
该研究指出,迫切需要共享数据标准,以改善印度动物救援部门的动物福利和公共卫生成果。它还旨在为评估南半球和其他面临类似问题的国家的收容所科学和犬类福利提供基础证据基础和概念验证。

Characterizing shelter intake, length of stay, and outcomes among free-ranging dogs in animal welfare organizations in India

Authors: Rubina Mondal, Alexandra Protopopova, Anindita Bhadra
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background- Countries in Global South carry the economic and social burden of unmanaged and understudied populations of free-ranging dogs globally. However, veterinary, shelter science and behaviour research fields are dominated by research from Global North countries and cater to a partial perspective. In this exploratory study, we try to bridge the gap of this divide by investigating the intake and outcome patterns of admitted free-ranging dogs in animal rescue organizations in India by analysing diverse data from over 24,000 records. We identify critical trends in admission causes, clinical burdens, and animal survival rates across from four geographically and institutionally distinct animal shelters in India between 2018 and 2022. Results- Average shelter intake volume grew by approximately 35% per year over the study period. Physical trauma emerged as the primary driver of admissions across all sites, regardless of regional differences in climate or urbanisation. While live outcomes generally exceeded non-live outcomes in most shelters, non-live outcomes (death and euthanasia) occurred at high rates (40–60%), substantially higher than reported Global North benchmarks. Survival was strongly associated with clinical presentation: neurological cases, most plausibly linked to endemic rabies and distemper, carried the poorest prognosis, with a median survival time of only one day. Systemic, gastrointestinal, and musculoskeletal conditions also carried significantly higher hazards for non-live outcomes. The overall median length of stay was 8 days, though hospitalization duration varied significantly, with oncological cases requiring the longest care. Conclusion- The findings characterise Indian shelters not as rehoming centres as a downstream safety net, for animal welfare crises originating from a lack of upstream population management, vaccination coverage, and injury prevention infrastructure. Furthermore, the study identifies the absence of standardised data collection as a fundamental barrier to evidence-based policy, highlighting an urgent need for shared data standards to improve animal welfare and public health outcomes across the Indian animal rescue sector. Ultimately, this study aims to provide a foundational evidence base and proof-of-concept for evaluating shelter science and canine welfare in Global South and other countries facing similar issues.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on characterizing shelter intake, length of stay, and outcomes among free-ranging dogs in animal welfare organizations in India. The central question is to understand the patterns of admissions, survival rates, and the factors influencing outcomes in these shelters, with an emphasis on the role of upstream population management, vaccination coverage, and injury prevention infrastructure.
Evidence base and method
The review is an exploratory study analyzing diverse data from over 24,000 records across four geographically and institutionally distinct animal shelters in India between 2018 and 2022. The study design involves examining admission causes, clinical burdens, and survival rates, with no explicit mention of meta-analysis or consensus processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The study identifies that physical trauma is the primary driver of admissions across all sites, regardless of regional differences in climate or urbanisation. It also reports that non-live outcomes (death and euthanasia) occur at high rates (40–60%), substantially higher than reported Global North benchmarks. Additionally, survival is strongly associated with clinical presentation, with neurological cases, likely linked to rabies and distemper, having the poorest prognosis, with a median survival time of only one day.
Controversies and gaps
The study highlights the absence of standardised data collection as a fundamental barrier to evidence-based policy, which is a critical evidence gap. It also notes that the findings characterise Indian shelters not as rehoming centres but as a downstream safety net for animal welfare crises originating from a lack of upstream population management, vaccination coverage, and injury prevention infrastructure.
Research and practice implications
The study identifies the urgent need for shared data standards to improve animal welfare and public health outcomes across the Indian animal rescue sector. It also aims to provide a foundational evidence base and proof-of-concept for evaluating shelter science and canine welfare in Global South and other countries facing similar issues.

国家免疫咨询委员会 (NACI) 声明摘要:使用 狂犬病 疫苗 进行暴露前预防 (PrEP) 的更新指南

Summary of the National Advisory Committee on Immunization (NACI) Statement: Updated guidance for use of rabies vaccine for pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP)
作者： April M. Killikelly, Bryna Warshawky, Miranda O'Driscoll
摘要中文翻译背景:狂犬病 是一种罕见但几乎普遍致命的疾病。虽然 治疗 一旦出现症状就无效,但可以通过及时的 暴露后预防 (PEP) 来预防感染。建议有 狂犬病 暴露风险的人进行暴露前预防 (PrEP)。虽然 PrEP 通常简化了 PEP,但它并不能取代 PEP 的需要。国家免疫咨询委员会(NACI) 在审查最新证据后更新了其 PrEP 指南。目的:总结 NACI 关于 狂犬病 PrEP 的更新建议,包括风险群体、疫苗接种 时间表、给药途径、血清学测试、产品互换性和针对先前接种过疫苗的个体的 PEP 指南。方法:NACI 使用证据到推荐框架,包括按剂量数量(一剂、两剂或三剂)和途径对免疫原性进行系统审查给药方案(单部位肌肉注射 [IM] 与两部位皮内注射 [ID])。这些建议由 NACI 根据现有证据进行审查和批准。结果:NACI 将 PrEP 风险类别重新定义为极高、高和中等风险。对于大多数组,建议采用三剂方案(包括两剂加加强剂/第三剂)。对于风险持续不到三年的中度风险组中非免疫功能低下的个体,采用两剂方案建议使用单位点 IM 和双位点 ID 途径。血清学检测建议由风险组提供,并且不建议仅因为 PrEP 是通过双位点 ID 途径进行常规 血清学。疫苗 产品和给药途径在必要时被认为是可以互换的。强调 PEP 的重要性,包括对于那些以前接受过 PrEP 的人,并为那些先前接受过一项或多项 狂犬病 的人提供指导疫苗 剂量。结论:更新的 NACI 指南减少了某些群体的剂量,并在保持保护的同时增加了给药的灵活性。NACI 将继续监测证据并根据需要更新建议。完整的 NACI 指南可在题为“使用 狂犬病 暴露前预防 (PrEP) 的更新指南”的 NACI 声明中找到,并且也在加拿大的 狂犬病 疫苗 章节中进行了总结。免疫指南。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病。核心问题是总结更新的 NACI 关于 狂犬病 暴露前预防 (PrEP) 的指南,包括风险群体、狂犬病 时间表、给药途径、血清学检测、产品互换性以及针对先前接种疫苗的个体的 PEP 指南。
证据基础与方法
NACI 使用了从证据到推荐的框架,包括通过剂量数量(一剂、两剂或三剂)和给药途径(单部位肌肉注射 [IM] 与两部位皮内注射 [ID])对免疫原性进行系统评价。这些建议由 NACI 根据现有证据进行审查和批准。
共识与结论
NACI 将 PrEP 风险类别重新定义为极高、高和中等风险。对于大多数群体,建议采用三剂方案(包括两剂加加强剂/第三剂)。对于风险持续不到三年的中度风险组中非免疫功能低下的个体,建议采用两剂方案。单站点 IM 和双站点 ID 路由均可接受。疫苗 产品和给药途径在必要时被认为是可以互换的。强调了 PEP 的重要性,包括对于那些之前接受过 PrEP 的人,并为那些之前接受过一剂或多剂 狂犬病 疫苗 剂量的人提供了指导。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据不报告争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
所提供的证据未报告研究、监测、诊断、治疗、疫苗接种、生态、风险评估或政策的具体优先事项。

Summary of the National Advisory Committee on Immunization (NACI) Statement: Updated guidance for use of rabies vaccine for pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP)

Authors: April M. Killikelly, Bryna Warshawky, Miranda O'Driscoll
Original abstract (English)
Background: Rabies is a rare but almost universally fatal disease.While treatment is ineffective once symptoms develop, infection can be prevented through timely post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP).Pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) is recommended for people at risk of rabies exposure.While PrEP generally simplifies PEP, it does not replace the need for PEP.The National Advisory Committee on Immunization (NACI) updated its PrEP guidance following a review of recent evidence.Objective: To summarize NACI's updated recommendations on rabies PrEP, including risk groups, vaccination schedules, routes of administration, serologic testing, product interchangeability and PEP guidance for previously vaccinated individuals.Methods: NACI used an evidence-to-recommendation framework, including a systematic review of immunogenicity by number of doses (one, two or three) and route of administration (one-site intramuscular [IM] vs two-site intradermal [ID]).These recommendations were reviewed and approved by NACI based on the available evidence.Results: NACI re-defined PrEP risk categories into very high, high and moderate risk.For most groups, a three-dose schedule (which includes two doses plus a booster/third dose) is recommended.For non-immunocompromised individuals in the moderate risk group with risk lasting less than three years, a two-dose schedule is recommended.Both one-site IM and twosite ID routes are acceptable.Serologic testing recommendations are provided by risk group, and routine serology solely because PrEP was administered by the two-site ID route is not recommended.Vaccine products and routes of administration are considered interchangeable when necessary.The importance of PEP, including for those who have previously received PrEP, is emphasized, and guidance provided for those who have received one or more prior rabies vaccine doses.Conclusion: Updated NACI guidance reduces doses for some groups and increases flexibility in administration while maintaining protection.NACI will continue to monitor evidence and update recommendations as needed.The full NACI guidance is available in the NACI statement entitled Updated Guidance for Use of Rabies Pre-Exposure Prophylaxis (PrEP) and is also summarized in the Rabies vaccines chapter of the Canadian Immunization Guide.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease. The central question is to summarize the updated NACI guidance on rabies pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP), including risk groups, vaccination schedules, routes of administration, serologic testing, product interchangeability, and PEP guidance for previously vaccinated individuals.
Evidence base and method
NACI used an evidence-to-recommendation framework, including a systematic review of immunogenicity by number of doses (one, two or three) and route of administration (one-site intramuscular [IM] vs two-site intradermal [ID]). These recommendations were reviewed and approved by NACI based on the available evidence.
Consensus and conclusions
NACI re-defined PrEP risk categories into very high, high and moderate risk. For most groups, a three-dose schedule (which includes two doses plus a booster/third dose) is recommended. For non-immunocompromised individuals in the moderate risk group with risk lasting less than three years, a two-dose schedule is recommended. Both one-site IM and two-site ID routes are acceptable. Vaccine products and routes of administration are considered interchangeable when necessary. The importance of PEP, including for those who have previously received PrEP, is emphasized, and guidance is provided for those who have received one or more prior rabies vaccine doses.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not report specific priorities for research, surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, vaccination, ecology, risk assessment or policy.

不断变化的景观中的吸血蝙蝠 狂犬病:土地利用引起的种群连通性变化如何影响 狂犬病病毒 跨物种溢出 的风险

Vampire bat rabies in changing landscapes: how land use-induced changes in population connectivity affect the risk of rabies virus spillover

Vampire bat rabies in changing landscapes: how land use-induced changes in population connectivity affect the risk of rabies virus spillover

狂犬病 疫苗 犹豫是新罕布什尔州兽医和立法者反复出现的话题 - 来自美国的新闻

Rabies vaccine hesitancy a recurring topic for NH veterinarians, legislators - News From The States
新闻简报在新罕布什尔州,犹豫不决已成为兽医和立法者之间反复出现的问题。一项旨在增加宠物 疫苗 豁免的法案(众议院法案 1488)被搁置,但后来建议众议院环境和农业委员会于 2026 年 11 月重新审议。该法案于 2026 年 3 月送交临时研究,随着 2026 年立法会议结束,不再有资格通过。该委员会以 10 比 1 的投票结果决定在未来的立法中讨论这一主题,强调了对不良反应和滴度测试的伦理挑战的担忧。狂犬病 是一种可传染给人类的普遍致命疾病,仍然是一个重要的公共卫生问题,CDC 指出,美国人类接触 狂犬病 的人数异常高,全球统计数据显示,每年有超过 70,000 人死亡。兽医强调 疫苗 的重要性,尽管他们承认需要进一步研究和澄清其有效性和安全性。这场辩论反映了 疫苗 犹豫不决的更广泛趋势,以及平衡宠物健康与道德考虑的复杂性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
众议院环境与农业委员会建议重新考虑 2026 年 11 月上届会议中搁置的一项法案的内容。该法案于 2026 年 3 月送交临时研究,随着 2026 年会议结束,不再符合通过资格。该委员会投票建议在 2026 年 10 月的未来立法中解决该主题。
地点与对象
美国新罕布什尔州。该主题涉及宠物(特别是狗、猫和雪貂)的 狂犬病 疫苗 豁免,州法律要求这些宠物接种 狂犬病 疫苗。
事件
狂犬病 疫苗 犹豫已成为新罕布什尔州兽医和立法者反复讨论的话题,一项法案(众议院法案 1488)寻求增加宠物的 疫苗 豁免。该法案被搁置,后来建议众议院环境和农业委员会重新考虑,该委员会以 10 比 1 的投票决定在未来的立法中解决这个问题。
规模、影响与风险
狂犬病 是一种对宠物和人类致命的疾病,美国疾病控制和预防中心 (CDC) 报告称,今年美国记录的人类接触 狂犬病 的数量异常多。在全球范围内,每年约有 70,000 人死于 狂犬病,其中美国境外超过 99% 的病例是由狗咬伤造成的。一旦出现症状,这种疾病就被认为是无法治愈的,传播 的风险与野生动物和家畜有关。
应对、状态与不确定性
新罕布什尔州兽医协会和兽医们表达了对 狂犬病 疫苗 的支持,理由是它的安全性和救生性质。然而,委员会成员希望为 疫苗 指南未来可能发生的变化敞开大门。由于缺乏抗体水平的明确阈值以及 免疫应答 的动态性质,狂犬病 的滴度测试方案并不可靠。

Rabies vaccine hesitancy a recurring topic for NH veterinarians, legislators - News From The States

News Brief
In New Hampshire, rabies vaccine hesitancy has emerged as a recurring issue among veterinarians and legislators. A bill (House Bill 1488) aimed at increasing vaccine exemptions for pets was sidelined but later recommended for reconsideration by the House Environment and Agriculture Committee in November 2026. The bill was sent for interim study in March 2026 and was no longer eligible for passage as the 2026 legislative session closed. The committee voted 10-1 to address the topic in future legislation, highlighting concerns about adverse reactions and the ethical challenges of titer testing. Rabies, a universally fatal disease transmissible to humans, remains a critical public health issue, with the CDC noting an unusually high number of human exposures in the U.S. and global statistics showing over 70,000 annual deaths. Veterinarians emphasize the importance of the vaccine, though they acknowledge the need for further research and clarification on its effectiveness and safety. The debate reflects broader trends of vaccine hesitancy and the complexities of balancing pet health with ethical considerations.
Original source text (English)
Last month, the House Environment and Agriculture Committee recommended reconsidering the contents of a sidelined bill from last session: House Bill 1488 , which sought to increase rabies vaccine exemptions for New Hampshire pets. The bill was sent for interim study by the full New Hampshire House in March and, with the 2026 session closed and the biennium winding down, was no longer eligible for passage. Instead, the committee voted, 10-1, to recommend the topic be addressed in future legislation. That move came as a surprise to some. “Truthfully, we didn’t think it was going to go as far,” said Dr. Jane Barlow Roy, who chairs the legislative committee of the New Hampshire Veterinary Medical Association. That’s because there is broad consensus among veterinarians that the rabies vaccine — which protects against a disease both transmissible to humans and universally fatal — is safe and life-saving. Yet the bill was driven forward by pet owners concerned about the rare but non-zero occurrence of adverse reactions to the vaccine, reflecting the increasing hesitancy around both human and pet shots that is driving the resurgence of some infectious diseases in the U.S. Committee members said at an October work session they wanted to keep the door open to potential future changes to rabies vaccine guidance and ensure the topic is revisited if more information becomes available. But answering some of the questions raised by pet owners and legislators, veterinarians said, will require research that is complicated and ethically fraught. A lethal disease For household dogs, cats and ferrets, rabies is the only vaccine required by New Hampshire state law. The viral illness is transmitted when saliva from an infected animal touches broken skin or mucous membranes like those inside the mouth, nose and eyes. The virus moves slowly through nervous tissue, incubating for weeks or more before symptoms like fever, headache, confusion, vomiting, and, later, aggression, difficulty swallowing or paralysis begin to manifest. While post-exposure treatment can prevent the disease from progressing if it is administered soon enough, once symptoms appear, the disease is considered incurable. Wildlife are the most common transmitters of rabies in the U.S., where the prevalence of vaccines has reduced both the disease’s incidence in domestic animals and the rabies death rate in humans and pets. But globally, about 70,000 people die of rabies each year, and more than 99% of people who get the disease outside the U.S. are infected by a dog , according to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. In the U.S., there was an unusually high number of human exposures documented this year, according to the CDC. And recent research suggests that the disease is responsible for more human deaths nationwide than scientists once believed. Hesitancy Kelly Arsenault, a Sandown Realtor, is skeptical about some vaccines. But rabies, she said, is not one that she skips when she brings her small dogs to the vet. “I believe in the ability of the body to heal,” she said. “However, rabies is not one of them — and I know that — it is not one of those that the body is going to heal from.” Yet Arsenault, who has for years worked to help rehome and rescue small dogs, also has questions about the rabies vaccine. More than 150 dogs, most of them fluffy and weighing under 15 pounds, have passed through Arsenault’s care over the years, she said. To date, one that she is aware of has had a significant negative reaction to a vaccine, she said, becoming severely ill after injection. Treating the dog with allergy medication managed the reaction, she said, but it was unsettling. She wonders why it happened and whether the incident could have been prevented. Negative reactions to vaccines in pets are uncommon, but most often appear similar to what many people experience after a flu shot, like injection site soreness and mild discomfort, said Dr. Lisa Boyer, a Seacoast area veterinarian, in an online seminar this October. Less commonly, severe reactions can involve facial swelling, vomiting or difficulty breathing. These reactions are managed with veterinary care, said Barlow Roy. And in New Hampshire, as in some other states, a pathway exists in state law to allow owners of pets susceptible to reactions to seek an exemption from the requirement. “The process is there for those dogs and cats that truly should not be getting the rabies vaccine,” said Barlow Roy. That exemption must be approved by the New Hampshire state veterinarian; after it is granted, exempt animals must be kept away from other animals or muzzled. Owners also assume more liability for their unvaccinated animals. For these reasons and the lethal nature of the rabies virus, the veterinarians consider the vaccine more than worthwhile for almost all pets. Yet the online proliferation of information and misinformation about vaccines has raised more curiosity among pet owners, who are asking more questions and starting conversations the veterinarians said they welcomed. However, in some cases, they said, pet owners have also become more likely to push back against professionals’ recommendations. Titer testing HB 1488 sought to broaden the allowed cases for rabies vaccine exemptions in New Hampshire under one condition: A pet has received a series of tests attempting to demonstrate that their immune system has responded to the vaccine. Those tests, known as antibody titers, can measure the level of rabies-specific antibodies, or immune system proteins, in a pet’s blood. The pet’s own white blood cells produce antibodies after they are exposed to the inactivated virus molecules in a vaccine; then the antibodies remain in the pet’s blood, ready to raise the alarm if they ever encounter the rabies virus again, such as if the pet is exposed to rabies from a wild animal. The idea of a titer testing protocol appeals to Arsenault, she said, because if a test could demonstrate that her pet is protected against rabies, she could skip additional “booster” shots. But the problem, according to veterinarians, is that there is currently no test that can prove definitively whether or not a pet has enough antibodies to be protected against rabies. If a titer comes back showing rabies antibodies in a pet’s blood, that confirms that the animal has some degree of immunity against rabies from a previous vaccination, Barlow Roy said. But it takes a certain threshold amount of antibodies to actually combat the virus in the case of a real exposure. That makes the rabies titer test unhelpful for two reasons. One limitation, according to American Veterinary Medical Association President Dr. Jennifer Quammen, is that the test is a snapshot in time of a dynamic situation. The amount of antibodies in a pet’s blood changes as time goes on; this is part of the reason booster shots are called for. Second, scientists don’t have a reliable way of knowing what threshold amount of antibodies a pet’s blood must contain in order for them to be fully protected against the rabies virus. Therefore, in the face of a deadly disease like rabies, veterinarians recommend pets get boosters on schedule to ensure they are protected. An ethical dilemma The limitation of the rabies titer test doesn’t apply to some other diseases for which scientists have developed titer testing protocol. Pet owners may opt to use an antibody titer test to guide decisions about booster shots for some other illnesses, like canine distemper and parvovirus, veterinarians said. With rabies, the factor that has limited the development of a titer testing protocol is the disease’s lethality. Reaching a conclusion about what level of blood antibodies is enough to protect a dog requires testing on living animals. Dogs with different levels of antibodies in their blood, for example, would be intentionally exposed to the disease; some are expected to contract the illness. That result helps scientists determine how much antibody is actually protective. Animals that contract diseases for which treatments exist can be supported after that experiment with medical intervention, Barlow Roy said, making these studies less problematic for investigators. But an animal that begins showing signs of rabies is effectively facing a death sentence, making the protocol more fraught. “We’re not comparing apples to apples, you know? We’re in the same realm,” she said, describing the differences in rabies titer research versus that on other diseases. “It’s more challenging from an ethical point of view ... and that’s why we hesitate,” she said. Arsenault said she was conflicted: While she wants the results of the experiment, she also feels moral qualms about what it would necessitate. “That’s probably where this whole thing gets difficult,” she said. “Would I be willing to be part of that study and watch a dog die from rabies? No ... I don’t know if I could live with myself.” However, some advocates of reducing rabies vaccine frequency have pushed for that research to move ahead, and some studies of limited scope have been completed. That included a 2020 study funded by the Rabies Challenge Fund Charitable Trust that involved the euthanasia of at least 45 beagles, some of which were infected with rabies, raising objections from animal rights groups like People for the Ethical Treatment of Animals. But research has not yielded enough data to draw conclusions, veterinarians told legislators at hearings on HB 1488. Most rabies policy and vaccine guidance is based on a document called the “rabies compendium,” compiled by the National Association of State Public Health Veterinarians. The document is due for revision this year, said Dr. Nathan Harvey, New Hampshire assistant state veterinarian, at an October work session on the bill. Veterinarians expect that the compendium will include updated guidance on the vaccine, possibly — but not certainly — to include guidance around the use of titer testing. “They are looking at whether or not we can start making those correlations to (titer levels) being protective, and that information may come out in the compendium. But we can’t assume that information is going to be published, or not,” Barlow Roy said. The reality at present continues to be that titer tests cannot replace vaccine programs, for the sake of animal and human health, Quammen said. Body size Some pet owners have also raised concerns about particularly small animals, like the dogs that Arsenault rescues. Some negative reactions seem to occur with more frequency in small animals, Barlow Roy said, though she stressed that they are still uncommon. But while pet owners like Arsenault have asked whether a smaller “dose” of vaccine could be administered to smaller animals, that is misleading, Boyer said. Confusion can arise, she said, because many medications are dosed by body weight. But the way that vaccines work — by triggering an immune response — is not as sensitive to body size, meaning that the amount of a vaccine’s active ingredient that an animal must receive for the vaccine to work is not necessarily different for, say, a Pomeranian and a Lab. “If you cut the dose in half and assume you have an appropriate dose, you will not be fully vaccinated or fully protected,” she said. But there are other components besides dead viral molecules in a vaccine, and some formulations exist that deliver the same amount of active ingredient in a smaller volume, Boyer said. A matter of public health Veterinarians continue to recommend the rabies booster schedule that is widely accepted as the safest and best option for people and pets. South Acworth Republican Rep. Judy Aron, who chaired the House Committee on Environment and Agriculture last session but did not seek reelection this fall, said during the Oct. 6 work session that the topic could be revisited by the Legislature if more guidance is released in the revised compendium. Barlow Roy urged pet owners to discuss any concerns with their veterinarian, including by reporting any incidences of suspected vaccine side effects, which she said clinics routinely share with vaccine manufacturers. For Barlow Roy, the urgency of keeping pets protected from the virus is augmented by her compassion for the animals that visit her exam table — and the humans who love them. “If I ever, God forbid, have an animal in my care that has rabies and it is given to, you know, a child in the household, or another family member, a human, that we could have prevented, that would definitely crush me,” she said. “That’s always the thing in the back of my head: Rabies is 100% fatal. It results in death 100% of the time. I think that’s why we are so adamant about rabies and this whole titer discussion.”

Time
The House Environment and Agriculture Committee recommended reconsidering the contents of a sidelined bill from last session in November 2026. The bill was sent for interim study in March 2026 and was no longer eligible for passage as the 2026 session closed. The committee voted to recommend the topic be addressed in future legislation in October 2026.
Location and population
New Hampshire, United States. The topic concerns rabies vaccine exemptions for pets, specifically dogs, cats, and ferrets, which are required by state law to be vaccinated against rabies.
Event
Rabies vaccine hesitancy has become a recurring topic for New Hampshire veterinarians and legislators, with a bill (House Bill 1488) seeking to increase vaccine exemptions for pets. The bill was sidelined and later recommended for reconsideration by the House Environment and Agriculture Committee, which voted 10-1 to address the issue in future legislation.
Scale, impact and risk
Rabies is a lethal disease for pets and humans, with the CDC reporting that in the U.S., there was an unusually high number of human exposures documented this year. Globally, about 70,000 people die of rabies annually, with over 99% of cases outside the U.S. attributed to dog bites. The disease is considered incurable once symptoms appear, and the risk of transmission is associated with wildlife and domestic animals.
Response, status and uncertainty
The New Hampshire Veterinary Medical Association and veterinarians have expressed support for the rabies vaccine, citing its safety and life-saving nature. However, the committee members want to keep the door open for potential future changes to vaccine guidance. The titer testing protocol for rabies is not reliable due to the lack of a definitive threshold for antibody levels and the dynamic nature of immune response.

狂犬病未来的发展与面临的挑战(一)引言+流行病学

Original source text (Chinese)
前记 : 目前国际上关于狂犬病研究最权威、最全面的大型学术专著是 《 狂犬病的科学基础和管控( RABIES: SCIENTIFIC BASIS OF THE DISEASE AND ITS MANAGEMENT) 》,简称《 狂犬病 (RABIES) 》。该专著最新版( 第 5 版 )已于 2025年7月 面世。 本公众号已陆续将该专著第 4版(2020年版)的绝大部分内容翻译成中文。该专著最新版对旧版有较多更新和改进。本公众号将继续全文翻译该专著最新版的内容,将优先翻译新增加或更新较多章节的内容。 以下为该专著最新版的第 21章: ( Future developments and challenges ) 作者: Anthony R. Fooks 1 , Alan C. Jackson 2 1 Animal and Plant Health Agency (APHA), Weybridge, Surrey, United Kingdom ( 英国萨里郡韦布里奇 , 动物与植物卫生管理局 ) 2 Department of Clinical Neurosciences (Neurology), Cumming School of Medicine, University of Calgary, Calgary, AB, Canada ( 加拿大阿尔伯塔省卡尔加里 , 临床神经科学系,坎明医学院,卡尔加里大学 ) 本章目录: 21.1 引言 21.2 流行病学 21.3 免疫学 21.4 发病机制 21.5 动物狂犬病 21.6 动物狂犬病的控制 21.7 人狂犬病 21.8 人狂犬病的控制 21.9 人狂犬病的治疗 21.10 结论 参考文献 狂犬病是一种古老的疾病,至今仍是人类已知最古老的传染病之一 ,并作为 21 世纪最令人恐惧的病毒性人畜共患病之一持续存在。该病的病死率接近 100% ,威胁着全世界贫困人口的生存 (Bannazadeh Baghi and Rupprecht, 2021) 。 犬是狂犬病最重要的储存宿主,造成了超过 99% 的人狂犬病病例 。因此,减少犬介导的狂犬病将带来人狂犬病病例的相应下降。由于监测和报告系统不完善,每年实际发生的人类死亡被严重低估 (Franka and Wallace, 2018) 。在世界许多地区,由于卫生保健基础设施薄弱、教育体系乏力,狂犬病是一种被忽视的疾病,始终是一种与无知和贫困相伴的疾病 (Fooks, 2005) 。这种状况令人不满,尤其是 狂犬病完全可以通过接种疫苗来预防 。在许多狂犬病流行国家,狂犬病的管理被其他卫生优先事项所淹没。狂犬病的许多受害者是生活处境不利的儿童。在许多国家,由于没有实验室确认、往往只有狂犬病的临床诊断,人类的该病常被误诊 (Mallewa et al., 2007) 。这些挑战制约了可用于动物(特别是自由游荡犬)狂犬病控制的资金。狂犬病是 " 同一健康 " ( One Health )的最佳例证,集中体现了需要把兽医医学和人类医学这些彼此分离的群体与生态学家和环保主义者汇聚在一起,以制定成功的干预策略 (Nadal et al., 2022; Saleem and Jan, 2024; Tidman et al., 2022) (见第 20 章)。世界若干地区 已证明狂犬病的消除是可行的 ,并且存在在狂犬病流行国家消除由犬传播给人的 RABV 的愿景 (Abela-Ridder et al., 2016) 。尽管控制狂犬病的许多工具已经具备,我们的知识中仍存在众多 " 黑箱 " 。要降低狂犬病造成的人类死亡人数,需要就特定问题开展更多研究。 这最后一章将聚焦于未来的研究工作,以及那些尚未得到解答、仍需集中研究以推进我们对狂犬病认识的问题 。 RABV lyssavirus 是 狂犬病毒属( Lyssavirus )的原型病毒种,与该属另外 17 个成员一道,隶属于 单股负链病毒目( Mononegavirales ) 弹状病毒科( Rhabdoviridae ) (Walker et al., 2022) 。该属成员的数量正在扩大,反映出这类病毒广泛的多样性以及 RNA 病毒在宿主转换方面所拥有的进化优势(见第 2 章和第 3 章)。狂犬病毒属成员在持续变化的格局动态中不断演化,在亚洲和非洲国家尤为突出 (Fisher et al., 2018) ,而 RABV 继续占全球狂犬病负担的绝大部分,这需要我们理解病毒种、宿主与环境之间的相互关联(见第 4 章和第 5 章)。持续的被动与主动监测结合病毒基因测序,对于增进我们对 RABV 传播以及控制狂犬病所需干预策略的认识至关重要,在发达国家尤其如此(见第 5 章)。未来很可能会鉴定出更多的狂犬病毒属成员。更好地理解病毒的进化,以及病毒在翼手目(蝙蝠)宿主与陆生哺乳动物宿主之间的宿主转换,将有助于我们认识狂犬病毒属病毒未来是否可能浮现。 研发能够对新发现的任一狂犬病毒属成员提供保护性免疫的 泛狂犬病毒属疫苗 ( pan-lyssavirus vaccine )以及广谱抗病毒药物,是公共卫生干预的未来之需 。 (未完待续) 本公众号相关文章的链接 权威的大型学术专著《狂犬病(Rabies)》最新版已面世 2020-06-30 一部跨越二十三年的"狂犬病百科全书" 2026-10-03 大型学术专著《狂犬病》2025年最新版的更新 2026-10-19 当代最著名狂犬病专家为学术专著《狂犬病》写的序言 2026-10-21 狂犬病毒的分类学(一) 2026-10-25 狂犬病毒的分类学(二) 2026-10-27 狂犬病毒的分类学(三) 2026-10-29 狂犬病毒的分类学(四) 2026-10-31 狂犬病毒的分类学(五) 2026-11-02 狂犬病毒的分类学(六) 2026-11-04 狂犬病毒的分类学(七) 2026-11-06

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

提高认识并主动预防 狂犬病。- 越南.vn

Raise awareness and proactively prevent rabies. - Vietnam.vn
新闻简报Vietnam.vn 于 2026 年 11 月 11 日发表的文章强调了越南金瓯省持续存在的 狂犬病 风险。报告称,2026 年前 8 个月,全国共记录了 69 例与 狂犬病 相关的死亡,其中当地报告了 1 例人类死亡和 2 例动物 狂犬病 死亡。狂犬病 被描述为一旦出现症状几乎 100% 致命,但通过适当的伤口 治疗 和及时的 暴露后预防 是完全可以预防的。金瓯市卫生局正在通过社区外展、培训课程和媒体宣传活动积极提高人们的认识。金瓯市疾病预防控制中心主任 Nguyen Hoang Tuan Anh 强调预防和正确 治疗 的重要性。文章还指出,人们越来越多地在动物咬伤后寻求医疗建议和 疫苗接种,而不是使用未经证实的民间疗法。专家警告说,狂犬病 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁,99% 的人类病例中,狗是 传播 的主要来源。文章呼吁加强宠物管理、狂犬病和社区教育,以降低狂犬病的风险,实现狂犬病零死亡的目标。
查看新闻五要素
时间
文章发表于2026年11月11日。它引用了 2026 年前 8 个月记录的 狂犬病 死亡人数,并提到了炎热夏季期间 发病率 的增加。
地点与对象
文章重点介绍了越南金瓯省,特别提到了新安公社的南义村和丰协公社的 1A 号村。它还指的是越南的一般人口,并指出每年有数十人因狗和猫咬伤而死亡。
事件
文章报道了金瓯省人类 狂犬病 死亡事件和动物 狂犬病 死亡事件,以及当地卫生部门通过教育和培训提高认识和预防 狂犬病 所做的努力。它还强调了及时 暴露后预防 和 疫苗接种 的重要性。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年前 8 个月,全国共记录了 69 例因 狂犬病 死亡的病例。金瓯省报告了 1 人死亡和 2 只动物死亡。狂犬病 被描述为一旦出现症状几乎 100% 致命,症状出现后 死亡率 的死亡率接近 100%。该疾病被认为是社区中的持续风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
金瓯卫生局正在通过各种渠道加强沟通工作,包括为卫生合作者举办培训课程。文章强调了立即伤口 治疗 和 暴露后预防 的必要性,并提到人们越来越主动地寻求医疗建议和 疫苗接种。来源是一篇完整的文章,因此没有注明任何限制。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Raise awareness and proactively prevent rabies. - Vietnam.vn

News Brief
The article published on November 11, 2026, by Vietnam.vn highlights the ongoing risk of rabies in Ca Mau province, Vietnam. It reports that 69 rabies-related deaths were recorded nationwide in the first eight months of 2026, with one human death and two animal outbreaks reported in the locality. Rabies is described as almost 100% fatal once symptoms appear, but it is entirely preventable with proper wound treatment and timely post-exposure prophylaxis. The Ca Mau Health Department is actively raising awareness through community outreach, training courses, and media campaigns. Nguyen Hoang Tuan Anh, head of the CDC Ca Mau, emphasizes the importance of prevention and proper treatment. The article also notes that people are increasingly seeking medical advice and vaccination after animal bites, as opposed to using unproven folk remedies. Experts warn that rabies remains a significant public health threat, with dogs being the primary source of transmission for 99% of human cases. The piece calls for stricter pet management, vaccination, and community education to reduce the risk of rabies and achieve the goal of zero rabies deaths.
Original source text (English)
Raise awareness and proactively prevent rabies. Rabies is a particularly dangerous infectious disease caused by the rabies virus, transmitted primarily through the saliva of infected animals, most commonly through bites, scratches, or licking of damaged skin. Once clinical symptoms appear, the disease is almost 100% fatal. However, death from rabies is entirely preventable if people properly treat the wound and receive timely post-exposure prophylaxis. Find Pet Care Sở Y tế tỉnh Cà Mau • 11/11/2026 According to reports, in the first eight months of 2026, the whole country recorded 69 deaths due to rabies. In Ca Mau , so far, one death due to rabies has been recorded in a human in Nam Nghia hamlet, Tan An commune. Regarding animals, the locality has recorded two outbreaks in Trung Cang hamlet, Tran Phan commune and Hamlet 6, Khanh Lam commune. Deaths from rabies serve as a warning about the persistent risk in the community. In particular, complacency after being bitten or scratched by a dog or cat, or coming into contact with an animal suspected of having the disease, can cause people to miss the opportunity for effective preventative treatment. According to Dr. Nguyen Hoang Tuan Anh, Head of the Department of Infectious Disease Prevention and Control, CDC Ca Mau, rabies is particularly dangerous because once the disease has started, there is almost no possibility of cure. Therefore, prevention and proper treatment immediately after being bitten or scratched by an animal suspected of having the disease are crucial factors in protecting people's lives. Discover more Check Symptoms Find Treatments Get Health Tips Given the continued prevalence of rabies, the Ca Mau Health Department is intensifying its communication efforts through various means, from direct community outreach to information disseminated through newspapers, radio, social media, and integrated into disease prevention and control activities. The communication focuses on the dangers of rabies, prevention methods, treatment of dog and cat bites and scratches, and the importance of post-exposure rabies vaccination. Find Pet Care Notably, the Health Education and Communication Department of the Ca Mau CDC organized 40 training courses on rabies prevention and control for health collaborators. This activity contributed to improving the capacity for communication, counseling, and mobilizing people to proactively prevent the disease at the grassroots level. Mr. Tran Hoang Tien, a health collaborator in Hamlet 1A, Phong Hiep Commune, said: After participating in the training, he understood more clearly the dangers of rabies as well as how to educate and guide people on how to handle dog and cat bites and scratches. He continues to share this knowledge with the people in the local area while carrying out his duties. You may also like Lam Dong province strengthens inter-agency coordination in rabies prevention and control. On November 9th, the Central Institute of Hygiene and Epidemiology, in collaboration with the Lam Dong Provincial Department of Health, organized a training session on monitoring and implementing an inter-sectoral health-veterinary cooperation model for rabies surveillance based on individuals bitten by dogs or cats who receive vaccinations. The disease is almost incurable once symptoms appear. Experts warn that rabies has a nearly 100% mortality rate after symptoms appear, while timely wound treatment and vaccination can prevent the risk of contracting the disease. Stricter penalties for allowing pets to roam freely. Rabies, contracted from bites by pets such as dogs and cats, is considered a dangerous disease. In fact, dozens of deaths from rabies are recorded annually in Vietnam after people are bitten by infected dogs or cats. Yet, people continue to let their pets roam freely in public places. Especially during the hot summer months, when temperatures rise, the incidence of rabies in dogs and cats increases, making this disease even more dangerous. In reality, as awareness increases, people tend to be more proactive after being bitten or scratched by animals. Instead of self-monitoring at home or applying unproven folk remedies, many people go to medical facilities for advice and vaccination. Find Vaccines Ms. Huynh Thi Su, a resident of Hamlet 1A, Phong Hiep Commune, said that after being scratched by a cat, she proactively went to a medical facility for advice and rabies vaccination. This is one of the necessary actions to reduce the risk of contracting the disease after exposure. According to the World Health Organization (WHO), if bitten or scratched by a dog, cat, or animal suspected of having rabies, people should immediately treat the wound by thoroughly washing it with soap and plenty of water for at least 15 minutes, then go to a medical facility for risk assessment and post-exposure prophylaxis. Depending on the case, the exposed person may be prescribed rabies vaccine and anti-rabies serum or monoclonal antibodies. People should absolutely not apply leaves, ointments, irritants, or folk remedies to wounds on their own. Inappropriate treatments can increase the risk of infection and are not a substitute for preventive treatment for rabies. Besides proactively preventing the disease in humans, managing and vaccinating dogs and cats is also an important solution to control rabies from the source of infection. Every family needs to ensure their pets are fully vaccinated, manage their dogs and cats, limit letting them roam freely, and proactively notify authorities when they notice any unusual behavior in their animals. The WHO states that dogs are the source of transmission for 99% of human rabies cases; therefore, vaccinating dogs and managing dog populations are crucial measures to prevent rabies from spreading from the source. Find Pet Care You may also like Dr. Nguyen Hoang Tuan Anh emphasized that rabies is dangerous but completely preventable if people and communities proactively implement preventive measures. This includes combining management and vaccination of dogs and cats with raising public awareness and ensuring timely access to post-exposure prophylaxis. Preventing and controlling rabies is not only the responsibility of the health sector but also requires the coordination of local authorities, veterinary services, and the public. Each family proactively vaccinating their pets; each individual acting correctly and getting vaccinated promptly upon exposure will contribute to preventing rabies and protecting their own health, the health of their families, and the community. Taking proactive preventive measures starting today is the most practical way to minimize tragic deaths from rabies, aiming towards the goal of zero rabies deaths. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

独家|纽约市的 狂犬病 率是多年来最高的,而喂养浣熊的人是罪魁祸首 - 《纽约邮报》

Exclusive | NYC’s rabies rate is highest in years — and people who feed raccoons are to blame - New York Post
新闻简报纽约市浣熊 狂犬病 病例大幅增加,2026 年,布朗克斯、布鲁克林、皇后区和史坦顿岛的确诊病例达到 26 例,与去年全年相比增加了 30%。在萨福克县,有两人在他们的狗接触患有狂犬病的浣熊后接受了 狂犬病 治疗,今年报告了 32 例病例,其中 31 例涉及浣熊。纽约市卫生部门将这种传播归因于浣熊数量激增和易地事件(动物被转移到新地区)等因素。官员们正在将口服 疫苗 与鱼粉混合,秋季通过在公园和其他浣熊栖息地投放诱饵进行分发。虽然在没有 治疗 的情况下 狂犬病 是 100% 致命的,但该病毒很少以电影中看到的攻击性形式出现,通常会导致受感染的浣熊虚弱或迷失方向。该市正在采用非常规方法,例如在史坦顿岛使用直升机诱饵,以有效对抗 暴发。
查看新闻五要素
时间
跳至主要内容《纽约邮报》我们的品牌第六页名人新闻、娱乐和内幕消息,包括独家故事、照片和视频。
地点与对象
纽约市,特别是布朗克斯、布鲁克林、皇后区、史泰登岛和萨福克县,长岛和曼哈顿有浣熊种群。
事件
纽约市浣熊中的 狂犬病 病例激增,2026 年多个行政区确诊了 26 例,萨福克县有两人在狗接触狂犬病浣熊后接受了 狂犬病 治疗 治疗。
规模、影响与风险
2026年,狂犬病案件为26起,比去年全年增加30%,是2024年总数的12倍多。萨福克县今年报告了 32 起病例,其中 31 起涉及浣熊。注意到人类暴露的风险,因为如果没有 治疗,狂犬病 是 100% 致命的。
应对、状态与不确定性
纽约市卫生局正在调查 狂犬病 的传播,并列举了浣熊种群数量激增和易地事件等可能的原因。官员们正在使用秋季分发的口服 疫苗,通过在公园和其他热点地区投放诱饵来瞄准浣熊。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Exclusive | NYC’s rabies rate is highest in years — and people who feed raccoons are to blame - New York Post

News Brief
New York City has seen a significant rise in rabies cases among raccoons, with 26 confirmed cases in 2026 across the Bronx, Brooklyn, Queens, and Staten Island, marking a 30% increase compared to all of last year. In Suffolk County, two individuals received rabies treatment after their dogs were exposed to rabid raccoons, with 32 cases reported this year, 31 involving raccoons. The New York City Health Department attributes the spread to factors like a raccoon population boom and translocation events, where animals are moved to new areas. Officials are using oral vaccines mixed with fishmeal, distributed in autumn through baiting in parks and other raccoon habitats. While rabies is 100% fatal without treatment, the virus rarely manifests in the aggressive form seen in movies, typically causing weakness or disorientation in infected raccoons. The city is employing unconventional methods, such as helicopter baiting in Staten Island, to combat the outbreak effectively.
Original source text (English)

Lifestyle exclusive NYC’s rabies rate is highest in years — and people who feed raccoons are to blame By Ben Cost Published Nov. 10, 2026, 11:10 a.m. ET 2424 Comments See more of our coverage in your search results. Make New York Post a Preferred Source 1/1 Skip Ad Continue watchingafter the adVisit Advertiser websiteGO TO PAGE These trash pandas are trouble. New York has been invaded by a plague of rabid raccoons, with Gothamites reporting surging numbers of foaming-at-the-mouth animals across multiple boroughs, sparking fears of infection. According to city data, 2026 has already seen 26 cases spanning the Bronx, Brooklyn, Queens and Staten Island, with nearly half the cases at 12. Manhattan, meanwhile, had none (sorry, not sorry). Overall, that’s 30% more raccoon rabies cases than all of last year — and over 12 times the 2024 total. Advertisement While these surges are cyclical, with some years seeing as many as 50, the masked menaces have invaded boroughs where they were previously uncommon. 6 Emergency Service Unit officers use food as a tactic to lure a raccoon into a crate on 58th Street in Brooklyn. Michael Dalton Advertisement “Historically, we never really saw that many rabid raccoons in Queens or Brooklyn,” Sally Slavinski, director of the Zoonotic and Vector-Borne Disease Unit at the New York City Health Department, told the Post. “And then in 2022, and into 2023, we saw clustering of raccoons in Queens along the Nassau County border, and then there was a spread into Brooklyn and into Long Island.” Big Apple residents are feeling the effects. “I reported a sick raccoon in the park by 19th and Hoyt north to 311 that got picked up by park rangers on June 11,” said one Astoria-based Redditor, who said the Department of Health and Mental Hygiene confirmed it was rabid. On September 28, two people in Suffolk County had to receive rabies treatment after their dogs were exposed to raccoons that tested positive for rabies. They were among the 32 cases reported in the county so far this year (up 10% from last year), 31 of which involved raccoons. Advertisement Rabies day out It’s unclear what’s driving the spread of the disease, which primarily passes between raccoons through infected saliva, but Slavinski cited several causes. One is a raccoon boom that’s providing more “opportunities for transmission to occur.” While a population spike hasn’t been confirmed, Long Island notably boasts over 100 of the critters per square mile, compared to just 20–40 in rural areas, Newsday reported. Meanwhile, one longtime Queens resident on Reddit reported a “massive raccoon colony” in Astoria that emerged during the COVID-19 pandemic, although this cluster remains unverified. Advertisement 6 A pregnant raccoon rests near the basement door of A to Z Daycare in Brooklyn, New York. Paul Martinka Meanwhile, Staten Island residents — which boasts 26 rabies cases since 2022 (the second-highest behind Queens at 39) — claimed the critters are “constantly in my yard,” and “there are so many houses going up, they have nowhere to go.” Another possibility is that the virus is fanning out to new areas, exposing trash pandas with little immunity. Slavinski said this can occur naturally or through “translocation events” where people trap and move raccoons to new places. Advertisement In October, a dead raccoon found in an East Islip home tested positive for rabies, prompting Suffolk County to investigate how it got there in the first place, per a county report. Risk factors Contrary to movie depictions, Slavinski notes that raccoons rarely develop “the aggressive form of rabies” that causes animals to chase and bite humans, zombie-style. Instead, rabies-infected raccoons often appear weak or disoriented, and struggle to walk due to creeping paralysis. 6 New York City police officers remove a raccoon on the Upper East Side. Robert Miller Advertisement But it’s still best for people to keep their distance from all raccoons, since on occasion, the ring-tailed dumpster divers might try to attack when they first become sick. Westchester County resident Frank Maida, 81, told The Post about getting mauled in 2019 by a relentless raccoon that later tested positive. “I kept kicking him and he wouldn’t leave me alone,” recalled the senior while describing the infected critter, which reportedly bit multiple people before getting dispatched with a shovel. And even docile Ranger Ricks can transmit the virus to other mammals such as cats, which have a higher animal-to-human transmission rate than raccoons. 6 A raccoon searches for food in a trash can in Central Park in Manhattan. Anadolu via Getty Images Advertisement “When people find a sick cat, they’re more likely to pick it up, bring it to a veterinarian, and that leads to a number of people who potentially get exposed to that cat,” said Slavinski. She noted that cats are also more prone to “aggression” than raccoons — something anyone with a scratch-happy tabby at home already knows. Avoiding bites is paramount, as rabies is 100% fatal without treatment, per the World Health Organization. It starts with flu-like symptoms, followed by rapid neurological decline that includes hallucinations, thrashing, paralysis, and foaming at the mouth. Vax collector To nip outbreaks in the bud, NYC officials distribute mustard-like packets containing an oral vaccine mixed with fishmeal. Generally deployed in autumn, the critter candy is either scattered by hand or hidden inside specialized inoculation stations in public parks and other raccoon hotspots. Advertisement 6 NYPD officers attempt to capture a raccoon on the subway tracks in Brooklyn. Stephen Yang The trick, said Slavinski, is figuring out how to get the oral vaccine straight to raccoons instead of dogs or feral cats. That’s tricky in Manhattan and Brooklyn, but goes more smoothly in Staten Island, where officials can cover large tracts by helicopter, where a wildlife official can toss bait over a known raccoon habitat. Slavinski said the unconventional methods are proven public health savers. For instance, following the 2010 rabies outbreak in Central Park, Slavinski’s team embarked on a vaccination project that essentially “eliminated raccoon rabies from Manhattan.” Individual measures Despite precautions, Slavinski doesn’t see raccoon rabies going away “anytime soon.” Advertisement “As long as we have raccoons, it’s going to continue to circulate until we find a way to produce an amazing vaccine that allows us to vaccinate a really large proportion of that population,” she said. Still, much like Smoky Bear and his forest fires spiel, individual New Yorkers can do a lot to prevent rabies in the Big Apple. Individuals can do their part by reporting sick, potentially infected animals—including those that appear confused or are dragging their hind legs — to 311. 6 A raccoon searches for food in a trash can in Central Park. Anadolu via Getty Images Advertisement And if the animal is behaving aggressively or has actually bitten someone? Call 911 stat and ensure the affected human is receiving immediate medical attention. In an October vaccination announcement, the NYC Department of Health advised New Yorkers to keep garbage tightly sealed and refrain from leaving pet food outdoors, as this can attract raccoons to residential areas. 24 What do you think? Post a comment. And don’t, under any circumstances, interact with raccoons or feed them, even if you’re convinced you’ve encountered Jimothy himself. Along with rabies, raccoons can carry distemper, bacterial infections, raccoon roundworm, and other illnesses that both humans and pets can catch. And if you thought tainted lettuce was unbearable, a raccoon-borne illness can be infinitely worse. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

俄勒冈州立大学兽医学院将兽医专业人士聚集在一起参加年度秋季会议和世界 狂犬病 研讨会 - news.okstate.edu

OSU College of Veterinary Medicine brings veterinary professionals together for annual Fall Conference and World Rabies Symposium - news.okstate.edu
新闻简报俄克拉荷马州立大学兽医学院于 2026 年 11 月 5 日至 6 日在会议期间举办了年度秋季会议和勃林格殷格翰世界 狂犬病 研讨会。该活动在 Roger J. Panciera 教育中心和网上举行,兽医专业人士、研究人员和公共卫生官员聚集在一起,讨论 狂犬病 的预防、管理和“同一个健康”方法。主题包括 狂犬病 预防、狂犬病、协调暴露反应和国家 狂犬病 管理计划。One Health Commission 执行主任谢丽尔·斯特劳德 (Cheryl Stroud) 博士就人类、动物和环境健康领域的合作发表了主题演讲。Rodney Willoughby 博士介绍了人类 狂犬病 治疗,包括实验性密尔沃基协议。研讨会还包括在俄克拉荷马州动物疾病诊断实验室进行的实践培训。该活动强调了跨学科合作在解决 狂犬病 相关挑战方面的重要性。没有具体的暴发或确诊病例报告,重点是教育和预防。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该活动于 2026 年 11 月 5 日至 6 日举行,会议和研讨会与秋季会议同时举行。该消息发布日期为2026年11月13日。
地点与对象
该活动在俄克拉荷马州的 Roger J. Panciera 教育中心举行,并在网上提供。它吸引了来自俄克拉荷马州及其他地区的兽医专业人士、研究人员、行业专家和公共卫生官员。
事件
俄克拉荷马州立大学兽医学院主办了年度秋季会议和勃林格殷格翰世界 狂犬病 研讨会,重点讨论了 狂犬病 的预防、管理和“同一个健康”方法。俄勒冈州立大学兽医学院报道了这一事件。
规模、影响与风险
研讨会涵盖了 狂犬病 预防、治疗 和协调暴露反应,并在俄克拉荷马州动物疾病诊断实验室进行了实践培训。它包括对国家 狂犬病 管理计划、暴露后预防 方案和动物咬伤的讨论。证据中没有报告具体的病例数、死亡数或暴露人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
该活动是一次教育和合作研讨会,没有迹象表明存在 暴发 或确诊病例。应对状态的重点是预防、教育和专业人员之间的协调。来源是完整的原始报告,该活动被描述为一次会议和研讨会。

OSU College of Veterinary Medicine brings veterinary professionals together for annual Fall Conference and World Rabies Symposium - news.okstate.edu

News Brief
Oklahoma State University’s College of Veterinary Medicine hosted its annual Fall Conference and the Boehringer Ingelheim World Rabies Symposium on November 5-6, 2026, in conjunction with the conference. The event, held at the Roger J. Panciera Education Center and online, brought together veterinary professionals, researchers, and public health officials to discuss rabies prevention, management, and the One Health approach. Topics included rabies prevention, treatment, coordinated exposure responses, and the National Rabies Management Program. Dr. Cheryl Stroud, executive director of the One Health Commission, delivered a keynote on collaboration across human, animal, and environmental health. Dr. Rodney Willoughby presented on human rabies treatment, including the experimental Milwaukee Protocol. The symposium also featured hands-on training at the Oklahoma Animal Disease Diagnostic Laboratory. The event emphasized the importance of interdisciplinary cooperation in addressing rabies-related challenges. No specific outbreak or confirmed cases were reported, and the focus was on education and prevention.

Skip to main content OKState.edu Quicklinks / Search Close Search Search this site Choose what to search Search this site All of Oklahoma State University Headlines News and Media Open Main Menu Close Main Menu News News Archive Topics Colleges and Divisions In the MediaMagazinesExperts DirectorySubmit NewsContact Us News News Archive Topics Colleges and Divisions In the MediaMagazinesExperts DirectorySubmit NewsContact Us Search Search this site Choose what to search Search this site All of Oklahoma State University Home Articles OSU College of Veterinary Medicine brings veterinary professionals together for annual Fall Conference and World Rabies Symposium Friday, November 13, 2026 Media Contact: Kinsey Reed | Communications Coordinator | 405-744-6740 | kinsey.reed@okstate.edu Share Facebook Twitter Pinterest Key Takeaways Oklahoma State University’s College of Veterinary Medicine hosted its annual Fall Conference and the Boehringer Ingelheim World Rabies Symposium Nov. 5-6. The conference offered continuing education in person and online, covering companion animal, equine and food animal medicine, plus rabies prevention and management. One Health Commission Executive Director Dr. Cheryl Stroud highlighted collaboration across human, animal and environmental health during the Class of 1963 Distinguished Lecture. The rabies symposium covered prevention, treatment and coordinated exposure responses, with hands-on training at the Oklahoma Animal Disease Diagnostic Laboratory. The Oklahoma State University College of Veterinary Medicine welcomed veterinary professionals, researchers and industry experts Nov. 5-6 for its annual Fall Conference, held in conjunction with the Boehringer Ingelheim World Rabies Symposium. The two-day event brought together professionals from across the veterinary and public health fields for continuing education, collaboration and discussions on emerging issues impacting animal and human health. Offered in person at the Roger J. Panciera Education Center and virtually, the conference featured sessions covering companion animal, equine and food animal medicine, along with a dedicated track focused on rabies prevention and management. A highlight of this year's conference was the Class of 1963 Distinguished Lecture, delivered by Dr. Cheryl Stroud, executive director of the One Health Commission. Her keynote presentation, “Foundations of One Health: The Full Scope of the Concept and a Brief Overview of the Global Movement,” explored the importance of a collaborative approach to addressing health challenges that affect people, animals and the environment. A veterinarian with a Ph.D. in endocrine physiology, Stroud brings experience in veterinary practice, industry, academic teaching and research, and One Health advocacy. For 13 years, she has led the One Health Commission, a nonprofit organization dedicated to connecting stakeholders, building cross-disciplinary teams and educating communities about One Health issues around the world. The conference also featured the Boehringer Ingelheim World Rabies Symposium, which brought veterinary, wildlife and public health professionals together to discuss rabies prevention, disease management and the importance of coordinated responses to potential exposures. A featured speaker was Dr. Rodney Willoughby, professor of pediatrics and infectious disease specialist at the Medical College of Wisconsin. Willoughby presented “Curing Human Rabies,” sharing his expertise in human rabies treatment and the Milwaukee Protocol, an experimental treatment approach he developed in Milwaukee in 2004. His presentation explored the challenges of treating human rabies, current therapeutic approaches and emerging research aimed at improving patient outcomes. Additional symposium presentations addressed the National Rabies Management Program, pre- and post-exposure prophylaxis protocols, animal bites and current rabies vaccination issues. Attendees also had the opportunity to participate in a hands-on rabies wet lab at the Oklahoma Animal Disease Diagnostic Laboratory, where participants reviewed proper sampling techniques, field necropsy approaches and sample submission procedures. Dr. Rosslyn Biggs, director of Continuing Education at the OSU College of Veterinary Medicine, said the annual conference provides an important opportunity for veterinary professionals to stay informed and connected throughout their careers. “Fall Conference is one way we continue to support veterinary professionals throughout their careers. Each year, it gives veterinary professionals a chance to hear what’s changing in the profession, talk through cases with colleagues and bring practical ideas back to their teams,” Biggs said. “Offering the conference here at the CVM and online makes it easier for people to participate around the demands of practice.” Biggs emphasized that the rabies symposium demonstrated the importance of the One Health approach and the collaboration needed to protect both animal and human health. “Rabies shows what One Health looks like across veterinary medicine and beyond. When an animal encounters a potentially rabid animal, the veterinary team, diagnostic laboratory and public health officials may all have a role in determining whether people or other animals were exposed,” Biggs said. “The symposium brought veterinary, wildlife and public health professionals together to discuss how they can work together before an exposure occurs and how they can coordinate when one is suspected.” The OSU College of Veterinary Medicine extends its appreciation to Boehringer Ingelheim for sponsoring the World Rabies Symposium and to the speakers, sponsors, alumni and university colleagues who helped make this year's conference possible. “I'm grateful to the speakers, sponsors, alumni and OSU colleagues who made this year's conference possible, and to the veterinary professionals who made time to join us,” Biggs said. Through its annual Fall Conference, the OSU College of Veterinary Medicine continues its commitment to providing meaningful continuing education opportunities and supporting the veterinary professionals who serve communities across Oklahoma and beyond. Frequently Asked Questions When and where was OSU’s annual veterinary Fall Conference held? 路易斯安那州报告了自 2014 年以来首例狗 狂犬病 病例 - 来自美国的新闻

Louisiana reports its first rabies case in a dog since 2014 - News From The States
新闻简报路易斯安那州卫生官员于 2026 年 9 月 11 日证实,阿卡迪亚纳的一只狗被诊断出患有 狂犬病,这是该州自 2014 年以来首例犬类病例。这一罕见事件凸显了 狂犬病 的持续风险,一旦出现症状,这种疾病几乎总是对人类致命,但可以通过及时 治疗 进行预防。路易斯安那州卫生部指出,过去二十年来,狂犬病 在狗身上相对罕见,只有 5 例确诊病例。该州的病例发生在全国 狂犬病 感染病例上升之际,促使 CDC 发布关于动物接触增加和接触后 狂犬病 的健康建议。卫生官员强调在可能接触 狂犬病 后立即清洗伤口和就医的重要性,同时也提醒宠物主人注意 狂犬病 狂犬病 的法律要求。这种情况凸显了对病毒携带者保持警惕的必要性,例如蝙蝠和臭鼬,它们可以将 狂犬病 传播给家畜。
查看新闻五要素
时间
路易斯安那州报告了自 2014 年以来首例狗 狂犬病 病例,事件日期为 2026 年 9 月 11 日星期五。发布日期为 2026 年 11 月 12 日。
地点与对象
这起案件发生在路易斯安那州阿卡迪亚纳,涉及一只狗。受影响的人群包括当地社区和该地区的宠物。
事件
路易斯安那州阿卡迪亚纳的一只狗被诊断出患有 狂犬病,这是该州自 2014 年以来首例犬类病例。该州卫生部门官员证实了这一消息。
规模、影响与风险
过去 20 年来,狂犬病 在路易斯安那州的狗中相对罕见,只有 5 例确诊病例。这是该州自 2014 年以来的首例病例。CDC 注意到,全国范围内的 狂犬病 病例有所增加,动物接触和接触后 狂犬病 的报告也有所增加。在症状出现后,狂犬病 几乎总是对人类致命,但早期的 治疗 是可以预防的。
应对、状态与不确定性
路易斯安那州法律要求 3 个月以上的狗、猫和雪貂从有执照的兽医那里获得 狂犬病 疫苗。卫生官员敦促在可能接触 狂犬病 后立即清洗伤口并寻求医疗护理。CDC 针对 狂犬病 病例发布了健康咨询,强调了预防和早期干预的重要性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Louisiana reports its first rabies case in a dog since 2014 - News From The States

News Brief
Louisiana health officials confirmed on September 11, 2026, that a dog in Acadiana has been diagnosed with rabies, marking the first canine case in the state since 2014. This rare occurrence highlights the ongoing risk of rabies, which is nearly always fatal to humans once symptoms appear but can be prevented with prompt treatment. The Louisiana Department of Health noted that rabies has been relatively uncommon in dogs over the past two decades, with only five confirmed cases. The state's case comes amid a national rise in rabies infections, prompting the CDC to issue a health advisory on increased animal exposures and post-exposure vaccinations. Health officials emphasize the importance of immediate wound washing and seeking medical care after potential rabies exposure, while also reminding pet owners of the legal requirement for rabies vaccination. The situation underscores the need for vigilance against wildlife carriers of the virus, such as bats and skunks, which can transmit rabies to domestic animals.
