Bureau Healthcare Education Gov & Politics Immigration Environment Abortion Policy Economy Civil Rights Part of States Newsroom search Sort by Latest Relevant Search Search Stories From The States Is ranked choice voting the future of American elections? 28:30 https://play.prx.org/e?ge=prx_14251_c4000d2e-daf5-4f8c-8a38-25c51a242edf&uf=https%3A%2F%2Fpublicfeeds.net%2Ff%2F14251%2Fstoriesfromthestates.xml Part of States Newsroom News Louisiana reports its first rabies case in a dog since 2014 Share BlueskyXFacebookEmailCopy Link Trending Trending: Immigration Enforcement Data Centers Medicaid/SNAP Louisiana News Louisiana reports its first rabies case in a dog since 2014 Environment Healthcare Sep 11, 2026 | 5:25 pm ET By Elise Plunk Share BlueskyXFacebookEmailCopy Link Description A closeup photo of the reddened eyes of a large dog. (Photo by Sarah Ladd/Kentucky Lantern) A dog in Acadiana has been diagnosed with rabies, state health department officials confirmed Friday, marking the first canine case of the disease in Louisiana in more than 10 years. Rabies has been relatively rare in Louisiana dogs over the past 20 years, with only five confirmed cases in the state over that time period. The case that the Louisiana Department of Health confirmed Friday is the first in the state since 2014. While rare among dogs, wildlife such as bats and skunks carry the virus and can pass it on to household pets and domesticated animals. It’s spread through bites or scratches from an infected animal. The Louisiana infection comes as rabies cases across the country are on the rise. The U.S. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention issued a health advisory on Thursday for localities across the country after a spike in rabies cases over the summer, citing increased “reports of animal exposures and rabies post-exposure vaccinations.” Rabies is nearly always fatal to humans after symptoms start, but complications are preventable if the infected person is treated soon after being infected. Fewer than 10 people in the U.S. die from the virus every year, according to the CDC. Most contract the disease from bats, and three of every four Americans live in a community where rabies is present. The virus travels to the brain from an infected wound, with an incubation period of weeks to months before symptoms can begin. Early symptoms include fever, flu-like illness and an itching or prickling sensation at the location of the bite or scratch. Severe rabies symptoms include anxiety, hallucination and intense thirst paired with panic in the presence of liquids. Despite the virus causing near total fatality once it progresses to a late stage, there is a vaccine that prevents rabies from taking hold of both people and animals. People can take additional caution to prevent rabies by avoiding wildlife, vaccinating pets and seeking urgent medical care after being bitten by an animal that could be rabid. Louisiana law requires dogs, cats and ferrets over 3 months old to get a rabies vaccine from a licensed veterinarian. The state has reported an average of six rabies cases in animals each year since 2000, mainly in wildlife. If someone is bitten, scratched or otherwise exposed to an animal with suspected rabies, health officials urge them to immediately wash the wound with soap and water before seeking medical care. 得知 狂犬病 杀死了他们的组织捐献者后,患者争相取消手术 - 斯克里普斯新闻

Patients scramble to reverse surgeries after learning rabies killed their tissue donor - Scripps News
新闻简报斯克里普斯新闻最近的一篇新闻报道强调了一个令人担忧的案例,其中一名捐赠者未被发现的 狂犬病 感染导致了致命的器官移植和角膜受者的暴露。捐赠者詹姆斯·马丁 (James Martin) 住在爱达荷州潘汉德尔 (Idaho Panhandle),他于 2024 年 12 月被狂犬病臭鼬抓伤,但没有接受 狂犬病 检测。他的死亡最初归因于心脏病发作,他的器官和组织被捐赠给各个州的患者。角膜被用于凯撒医疗机构的手术中,接受者伊诺森西奥·米拉蒙特斯后来被告知存在风险。疾病预防控制中心指出,捐赠者的医疗记录包括臭鼬抓伤,但这一信息被忽视了。该报告对当前器官和组织移植筛查方案提出了关键问题,因为 狂犬病 测试由于其稀有性和诊断程序的复杂性而并非常规测试。此案例强调需要改进捐赠者筛选,以防止未来出现此类风险。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件发生在2025年2月使用捐献组织时,最初报道捐献者死亡是在2024年12月。新闻报道发表于2026年11月11日。
地点与对象
该活动涉及爱达荷州狭长地带的一名组织捐赠者,接受者是来自该捐赠者的器官和组织的各个州的患者。捐赠者的角膜用于凯撒医疗机构的手术中。
事件
捐赠者未被发现的 狂犬病 感染导致了致命的器官移植和角膜受者的暴露,引发了关于移植筛查的问题。斯克里普斯新闻报道了这一事件。
规模、影响与风险
捐献者詹姆斯·马丁(James Martin)的眼睛中的角膜组织可能受到感染,该组织被移植到伊诺森西奥·米拉蒙特斯(Inocencio Miramontes)体内。疾控中心证实,捐献者曾被患有狂犬病的臭鼬抓伤,在筛查过程中并未发现感染情况。通过组织移植产生 狂犬病 传播 的风险被强调为一个关键问题。
应对、状态与不确定性
疾病预防控制中心审查了这一事件,并指出捐赠者的医疗记录包括有关臭鼬抓伤的详细信息,但这些不足以确定 狂犬病 是死亡原因。由于其稀有性和复杂性,器官移植过程不包括 狂犬病 测试。这一情况仍未得到解决,人们对筛查方案的持续担忧。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Patients scramble to reverse surgeries after learning rabies killed their tissue donor - Scripps News

News Brief
A recent news report from Scripps News highlights a concerning case where a donor's undetected rabies infection led to a fatal organ transplant and exposed cornea recipients. The donor, James Martin, who lived in the Idaho Panhandle, was scratched by a rabid skunk in December 2024 but was not tested for rabies. His death was initially attributed to a heart attack, and his organs and tissues were donated to patients in various states. The cornea was used in a procedure at Kaiser Permanente, and the recipient, Inocencio Miramontes, was later informed of the risk. The CDC noted that the donor's medical records included the skunk scratch, but this information was overlooked. The report raises critical questions about the current screening protocols for organ and tissue transplants, as rabies testing is not routine due to its rarity and the complexity of diagnostic procedures. This case underscores the need for improved donor screening to prevent such risks in the future.
Original source text (English)

How deadly is rabies? Rabies is a fatal, viral neurological disease, associated with wild animals like bats, skunks, raccoons, and foxes. 1/1 Skip Ad Continue watchingafter the adVisit Advertiser websiteGO TO PAGE It may take weeks or months to incubate in the human body after a bite or a scratch, but a series of post-exposure vaccines can prevent the virus from developing. According to the CDC, more than 100,000 people receive these shots to prevent the disease each year, and fewer than 10 people die annually from rabies in the United States. However, once symptoms begin, the disease is nearly always fatal. RELATED STORY | Organ recipient dies after donor’s rabies infection went undetected And somehow, Holz's patient might have been exposed. The donor connection More than a month earlier, Holz had performed a successful cornea procedure at Kaiser Permanente on his patient, Inocencio Miramontes, using a tiny piece of tissue from a deceased donor. The tissue had been screened for safety and provided to him by an eye bank. Raquel Miramontes Inocencio Miramontes underwent eye surgery Holz said he didn’t know much about the tissue donor’s history beyond a few basic facts that the eye bank provided to him when it distributed the tissue. RELATED STORY | Boy dies from rabies after waking up with bat on his mouth “I knew that the donor had died of a heart attack, and I knew the donor’s age and the sex of the donor. I did not know much else,” he said. What no one knew at the time, however, was what actually killed donor. It wasn’t a heart attack. It was rabies. And the donor's potentially infected cornea tissue had been in Miramontes’ eye for weeks. Who was the donor? James Martin was a father of three grown children who loved animals and writing poems. He and his wife, Kim, lived in the Idaho Panhandle before he died of what was initially believed to be a heart attack in December 2024, and his organs and tissues were donated to people in various states. Family photo James Martin in a photo from 2012 More than a month before his death, however, Martin had been scratched by what is now believed to have been a rabid skunk. He was sitting on his front porch when the aggressive animal lunged at Martin’s rescue kitten. “He said he grabbed (the skunk) by the neck, and he literally, you know, pushed it up against the house,” his wife, Kim Martin, said. “He said he just gave it a few punches and everything, but in grabbing it, he said he got scratched.” Martin did not seek post-exposure prophylaxis shots as a precaution, and slowly, rabies silently took over his body. “He’s like, ‘I’m - I’ll be fine. It’s just a scratch. I’ll just put, you know, Neosporin on it, and I’ll be fine after I get it cleaned,” his wife said. Doctors now believe James Martin’s other extensive medical issues may have masked any rabies symptoms, leading a medical team to believe he had died of a heart attack. “In this case, hospital staff members who treated the donor were initially unaware of the skunk scratch and attributed his pre-admission signs and symptoms to chronic comorbidities,” the CDC said in a review of this incident. According to the CDC, details about Martin’s encounter with a skunk were documented on a standard donor risk assessment screening form before his organs and tissues were harvested and donated. But those details were not enough to stop the transplant, and Martin was not tested for the virus. “Some thought that, ‘Well, he just died of a heart attack,’ and so the other stuff was overlooked. But it can never be overlooked,” said Holz. Why organs and tissues are not tested for rabies According to the CDC, rabies deaths are extremely rare, and testing for the virus is complicated and time-consuming, unlike testing for more common illnesses like HIV and hepatitis. “Rabies is excluded from routine donor pathogen testing because of its rarity in humans in the United States and the complexity of diagnostic testing,” the CDC said in a review of this case. The demand for organs, meanwhile, is constant. According to David McCormick, a medical officer in the CDC office of Blood, Organ, and Other Tissue Safety, there are approximately 100,000 people on the waiting list for organs, and about 13 people die each day while they wait. “Given that there is such a need for organs and that timing in organ donation can be very critical, if we had to wait two to three days to test every organ donor for rabies...that could delay people receiving organs that they need, and with just how rare rabies is, it just wouldn’t be cost-effective and would likely result in organs not being used when they could have been used and could have saved people,” McCormick told Scripps News earlier this year. How the eye tissue was screened Lions World Vision Institute, the eye bank that reviewed the donor's medical records and provided the corneal tissue for transplant, told Scripps News the organization followed guidance from the Food and Drug Administration and standards set by the Eye Bank Association of America when screening the deceased donor’s tissue for a potential transplant. “The donor regulations at that time did not rule out (transplanting tissue due to animal) scratches, only bites,” said Jason Woody, the president and CEO of LWVI, in a written statement. RELATED STORY | Hundreds of Grand Teton National Park visitors may have been exposed to rabies At the time, Woody said tissue would have been rejected if a patient was suspected of having rabies or if the person had been bitten by an animal suspected to be infected with rabies within the past six months. Animal scratches, however, were not part of the guidelines for deferring tissue. “There was no indication at that time that this was a case of rabies,” said Kevin Corcoran, the president and CEO of EBAA. “They did note that he had been scratched by a skunk, but he had been seen by a number of physicians during the time that he was (in the hospital), and there was no expectation he had contracted rabies.” As a result, the donor’s corneal tissues were sent out for surgeries in Idaho, Missouri, New Mexico, and California, including the one that Miramontes had planned. The donor's kidney, meanwhile, was sent to a Michigan grandfather named Barney Kurowicki. Family photo Barney Kurowicki in July 2024 LifeCenter Northwest is the organ procurement organization that managed the kidney. “LifeCenter Northwest follows screening and testing protocols for donation as per (the Organ Procurement and Transplantation Network) policy, and we provide available donor information to our transplant partners in accordance with national policy requirements set by the OPTN,” a spokesperson told Scripps News earlier this year. How rabies was discovered More than a month after receiving the kidney transplant, Kurowicki started experiencing strange symptoms – including a fear of consuming water. At first, rabies didn’t seem like the logical culprit, but doctors contacted the CDC to be sure. At the time, only three cases of rabies transmission through an organ transplant had ever been documented in the history of U.S. transplant system, according to the CDC. But it was happening again. RELATED STORY | Woman stabs boyfriend's eye with rabies needle for eyeing other women By the time doctors and scientists confirmed that Kurowicki had contracted rabies from Martin’s donated kidney, it was too late to save his life. There was still time, however, to warn the people who received the donor’s corneal tissues. Suddenly, the CDC was on a quest to find Miramontes and three others who had already received or were scheduled for cornea graft surgery with the potentially infected tissue. Idaho patient learns about rabies threat Joseph Bird, 25, has suffered from a condition called keratoconus since his diagnosis as a teenager. His deformed cornea had progressively gotten worse over the years, distorting and diminishing the vision in his left eye. Joseph Bird Joseph Bird after eye surgery In 2025, he hoped some of his sight could be restored through a special new procedure in which his doctor in Idaho Falls would insert a piece of custom corneal tissue into his eye, provide by a deceased donor. “I was pretty excited,” he said. His doctor said it would take a few days before he would notice results, so Bird remained patient. “It wasn’t until about four days after the surgery when my doctor called me, saying that I should sit down,” Bird said. “He says, ‘The donor had rabies, and I should go to the emergency room to get the rabies (post-exposure prophylaxis) shots and that he’s already called them, and they’re all ready for me.” Although rabies is considered a “medical urgency” rather than an emergency, infectious disease experts urge exposed patients to seek vaccines immediately after being exposed because the virus is deadly once symptoms begin. “It’s not something that you get infected and two days later, you’re going to develop symptoms,” Rebecca Earnest, an epidemic intelligence service officer with the CDC, told Scripps News earlier this year. “However, because it's so fatal, we want people to get rabies (post-exposure prophylaxis shots) as soon as they find out they might have been exposed.” Although the tissue that had been implanted in Bird’s eye was not “live” tissue and had been sterilized through gamma irradiation, his surgery also would have to be reversed. “The FDA could not confirm that the gamma irradiation to the tissue would inactivate the virus,” said Dr. Bradley Gardner, Bird’s surgeon. “So, therefore we have to go full-court press.” “The following day, I would say, is when everything really hit the fan,” he said. Vaccine in or around the eye – a new approach “The concern came up if we need to also inject the human rabies immunoglobulin around the eye,” said Gardner. Essentially, because the eye is so close to the brain, would Bird and the other patients need antibodies injected directly in or around the eye to attack the virus if it were present? This was something that had never been done before, Gardner said. “It’s not like the first time that we’ve injected medications behind the eye and around the eye, but it’s never been done before with human rabies immunoglobulin,” he said. RELATED STORY | Vicious 'attack squirrel' sends multiple residents to the hospital in California Gardner said he participated in a series of urgent phone calls with every expert who might know anything about the potential risks and benefits. “I’m sitting here like, ‘I’m really going to be the first one to ever do this?’” he said. “So, that was a little intimidating, but having so many experts side with that protocol, we really felt that that was the best way to go.” In California, Dr. Holz was having a similar experience. “What they told me at the CDC is, ‘We’ve never done this at all, ever,’" he said. “We did have an advisory panel from the CDC who gave me some ideas about where to put it and how to put it, and I just followed their instructions. They had no experience with this, and I had no experience with this, so it was a bit of us both stumbling around in the dark trying to find the best outcome for the patient,” he said. The procedure was a success. The patients’ outcomes “I was just so nervous,” said Miramontes, who received the eye injection and a series of other post-exposure prophylaxis shots. Miramontes’ surgery was also reversed, and a new piece of tissue was implanted successfully. “I cannot sleep sometimes,” Miramontes said. “I feel like, stressed, but at the same time: stressed and nervous. I feel changed, but I cannot say how. It’s hard, especially when you get these surprises like that.” “The only thing I hated was the needle in the eye,” said Bird. Though both men are now safe from rabies, both say they continue to be affected by anxiety. Bird said he struggles with the idea of additional surgeries. “My honest opinion on all of it is the saying, ‘What doesn’t kill you makes you stronger,’” he said. “This one would be considered kind of half true. It didn’t kill me. I don’t think it made me stronger, though.” The other patients The CDC was able to contact the Missouri doctor before his patient’s eye surgery occurred. “We were able to switch to new tissue from another donor,” said Dr. Mujtaba Qazi. “The CDC did contact me, but I told them we never received nor used the potentially contaminated tissue.” Scripps News was unable to reach the New Mexico doctor and patient, but according to the CDC, that patient also received post-exposure prophylaxis shots to prevent the virus from developing. The CDC reported that all the donated eye tissues removed from the patients’ eyes were tested for rabies, and one tissue came back positive. “Every time we go forward now, this is something, obviously, we’re thinking about,” said Gardner, acknowledging that this set of circumstances was incredibly rare. “When I look back on my career and – going through all this – I'd say this is definitely one of the most memorable events of my career,” he said. Holz agreed. “This is a once-in-a-career thing if you ever experience this, ever,” said Holz. “And I expect I will never get a call like this again.” Changes in eye banking after the rabies transmission “The case was really almost a perfect storm of unexpected instances,” said Corcoran. “This was probably a one in 40 million – a one in one hundred million chance.” Following the incident, the Eye Bank Association of America immediately launched an investigation through its medical review subcommittee, Corcoran said. “Once we came to fully understand what had happened, we sent an alert out to all of our eye banks that brought them all the information we had on this and told them that our initial guidance was to change the way they screened for donors, so that if there was ever an incident where a donor had contact with an animal, and it was unclear what the status of that animal was, that donor would be deferred, so we would not recover their tissue. We would not transplant that tissue,” he said. The new policy, established in June 2025, is currently in effect today. Corcoran said there is a surplus of corneal tissue in the United States, unlike the massive demand for organs. “We are able to be more selective with tissue that we recover,” he said. “Whereas, on the organ side, because there are shortages, because there are many people that die every day because they can’t get organs that they need, there’s much more urgency for them.” Proposed changes in organ screening for rabies In April 2025, the Organ Procurement and Transplantation Network was urged by the Health Resources and Services Administration to develop better protocols to reduce the risk of donor-derived rabies transmission through organ transplants. A special Disease Transmission Advisory Committee, DTAC, proposed policy changes and new protocols that would enhance organ screening criteria and improve data collection, but no official votes have been made to adopt these policies and protocols. In a written statement, a spokesperson for HRSA told Scripps News that the issue was being prepared to be voted on by the OPTN board of directors. The next board meeting is November 19. Many organ procurement organizations have voluntarily adopted the practices, however, “including rabies testing when appropriate to help protect patient safety,” a spokesperson said. The event involves a tissue donor in the Idaho Panhandle, and the recipients were patients in various states who received organs and tissues from this donor. The donor's cornea was used in a procedure at Kaiser Permanente.
Event
A donor’s undetected rabies infection led to a fatal organ transplant and exposed cornea recipients, raising questions about transplant screening. This event was reported by Scripps News.
Scale, impact and risk
The donor, James Martin, had potentially infected cornea tissue in his eye, which was transplanted into Inocencio Miramontes. The CDC confirmed that the donor had been scratched by a rabid skunk, and the infection was not detected during the screening process. The risk of rabies transmission through tissue transplants was highlighted as a critical issue.

The CDC reviewed the incident and noted that the donor's medical records included details about the skunk scratch, but these were not sufficient to identify rabies as the cause of death. The organ transplant process did not include rabies testing due to its rarity and complexity. The situation remains unresolved, with ongoing concerns about screening protocols.

约翰斯顿和考文垂的浣熊 狂犬病 检测呈阳性 - East Greenwich News

Raccoons in Johnston and Coventry Test Positive for Rabies - East Greenwich News
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 10 日,罗德岛州卫生部 (RIDOH) 报告称,两只浣熊(一只来自约翰斯顿,另一只来自考文垂)狂犬病 检测呈阳性。这使得今年罗德岛州发现的患有狂犬病的动物总数达到 48 只,超过了 2021 年至 2025 年间平均每年 25 只的数量。这些动物大多数是浣熊、蝙蝠和臭鼬,还有其他病例涉及猫、郊狼和土拨鼠。RIDOH 敦促居民避免接触流浪动物或野生动物,并确保他们的宠物接种了 狂犬病 疫苗。建议那些可能接触过浣熊的人联系 RIDOH 进行评估并可能获得 治疗。如果在症状出现之前不进行治疗,狂犬病 是一种致命的疾病,必须立即启动 暴露后预防。该部门还建议保护垃圾桶,以防止吸引野生动物。

事件
据罗德岛州卫生部报告,约翰斯顿和考文垂的浣熊 狂犬病 检测呈阳性。
规模、影响与风险
今年罗德岛州已发现 48 种患有狂犬病的动物,其中大多数是浣熊、蝙蝠和臭鼬。强调如果在症状出现之前不进行治疗,狂犬病 传播 对人类的风险是致命的。
应对、状态与不确定性
RIDOH 正在向公众通报 狂犬病 病例,并建议那些可能与浣熊接触过的人致电进行评估。建议包括给宠物接种疫苗、避免接触流浪动物或野生动物以及保护垃圾桶。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Raccoons in Johnston and Coventry Test Positive for Rabies - East Greenwich News

News Brief
On November 10, 2026, the Rhode Island Department of Health (RIDOH) reported that two raccoons, one from Johnston and another from Coventry, tested positive for rabies. This brings the total number of rabid animals identified in Rhode Island this year to 48, surpassing the average annual number of 25 between 2021 and 2025. The majority of these animals were raccoons, bats, and skunks, with additional cases involving a cat, coyote, and woodchuck. RIDOH is urging residents to avoid contact with stray or wild animals and to ensure their pets are vaccinated against rabies. Those who may have had exposure to the raccoons are advised to contact RIDOH for assessment and possible treatment. Rabies is a fatal disease if not treated before symptoms appear, and post-exposure prophylaxis must be initiated promptly. The department also recommends securing garbage cans to prevent attracting wildlife.

Raccoons in Johnston and Coventry Test Positive for Rabies by egnews | Nov 10, 2026 From the Rhode Island Department of Health The state has identified 48 rabid animals so far this year The Rhode Island Department of Health (RIDOH) is alerting the public that a raccoon from the Green Earth Avenue area of Johnston tested positive for rabies late last week, along with a second raccoon from the Plainfield Pike area of Coventry that also tested positive for rabies late last week. Rhode Island continues to see a significantly elevated number of rabid animals in 2026. To date in 2026, 48 rabid animals have been identified in Rhode Island. This exceeds the average annual number (25) of rabid animals reported between 2021 and 2025. A full list of specimen collection dates and locations may be found on RIDOH’s Rabies Data Dashboard online under “Animal Rabies Mapping.” The majority of these animals were raccoons, bats, and skunks. Additionally, a rabid cat, coyote, and woodchuck have been found in Rhode Island this year. Rabies is a fatal disease if it is not treated before the onset of symptoms. Anyone who may have had direct contact with a raccoon in these areas should immediately call RIDOH’s Center for Acute Infectious Disease Epidemiology at 401-222-2577 (Monday to Friday, 8:30 a.m. to 4:30 p.m. or 401-276-8046 after hours) so a nurse can assess your exposure and determine if you should receive treatment. If you think your pet may have come in contact with one of these raccoons, call RIDOH, your local animal control officer, and your pet’s veterinarian. The rabies virus infects the central nervous system. If a person does not receive the appropriate medical care after a rabies exposure, the virus can cause disease in the brain, ultimately resulting in death. Rabies post-exposure prophylaxis must be started as soon as possible after exposure and before symptoms appear. All dogs, cats, and ferrets are required by state law to have current vaccination against rabies. Vaccinating pets prevents them from getting rabies and prevents people from becoming exposed to rabies through their pets. RIDOH recommends the following to prevent rabies: Make sure all dogs, cats, and ferrets are up to date on rabies vaccination. Avoid all contact with and do not feed stray or free-roaming domestic animals. Avoid all contact with and do not feed wild animals. Do not feed your animals outdoors, as this will attract other animals. This is especially dangerous when feeding large numbers of free-roaming cats. Protect your pets by always maintaining control; walk dogs on a leash or let them play in a fenced yard, and do not let pets wander unsupervised. Report all animal bites to your city/town’s animal control officer. Securely cover all garbage cans so wild animals cannot scavenge for food. For more information, visit health.ri.gov/rabies. Raccoons in Johnston and Coventry Test Positive for Rabies by egnews | Nov 10, 2026 From the Rhode Island Department of Health The state has identified 48 rabid animals so far this year The Rhode Island Department of Health (RIDOH) is alerting the public that a raccoon from the Green Earth Avenue area of Johnston tested positive for rabies late last week, along with a second raccoon from the Plainfield Pike area of Coventry that also tested positive for rabies late last week.

Event
Raccoons in Johnston and Coventry tested positive for rabies, reported by the Rhode Island Department of Health.

Original title (Chinese)
表面健康犬只的血清与脑脊液非靶向代谢组学分析:尼日利亚狂犬病DFA阳性犬的早期代谢改变与候选诊断特征 - 生物通

Original source text (Chinese)
表面健康犬只的血清与脑脊液非靶向代谢组学分析:尼日利亚狂犬病DFA阳性犬的早期代谢改变与候选诊断特征 《Metabolomics》:Untargeted serum and cerebrospinal fluid metabolomics of apparently healthy rabies virus-positive dogs in Nigeria 【字体: 大 中 小 】 时间:2026年11月15日 来源:Metabolomics 3.5 为了促进科学的传播,生物通采集了这篇文章 订阅BioHot点这里解锁原文链接推送! “畅读会员”点此登录查看原文链接 Original Article Open access 狂犬病仍是一种具有全球重大公共卫生意义的被忽视人兽共患疾病,其根本原因在于该病近乎普遍致死、可靠的生前(antemortem)诊断选择有限以及临床体征出现后缺乏有效治疗。然而,对表面健康但狂犬病病毒阳性的犬只所表现出的代谢改变目前仍缺乏表征。因此,本研究旨在鉴定直接免疫荧光试验(direct fluorescent antibody, DFA)阳性的表面临床健康犬与DFA阴性犬相比的差异丰度代谢物。研究方法:样本采集自231只表面临床健康犬的脑组织、血清及脑脊液(cerebrospinal fluid, CSF)。采用直接免疫荧光试验(DFA)检测脑组织中的狂犬病病毒抗原,并依据检测结果将犬只分为狂犬病阳性或阴性。使用经验证的非靶向代谢组学样本制备流程提取血清和CSF中的代谢物。代谢谱分析在优化的分析条件下采用全二维气相色谱-飞行时间质谱(comprehensive two-dimensional gas chromatography coupled with time-of-flight mass spectrometry, GC×GC-TOFMS)完成。研究结果:在231份脑组织样本中检出20份狂犬病病毒抗原阳性(8.7%)。相应的血清和CSF样本被归类为狂犬病(DFA)阳性,同时选取20份阴性样本用于比较。经质量控制后,血清中保留868种化合物,CSF中保留300种化合物。主成分分析(principal component analysis)和偏最小二乘判别分析(partial least squares discriminant analysis)均无法区分任一样本基质中的组间。经错误发现率(false discovery rate, FDR)校正后,4个血清特征保持显著:Analyte 634、甘油酸(glyceric acid)和Analyte 634在DFA阳性犬中丰度降低,而苯甲酸(benzoic acid)升高。Analyte 634在发现集中的曲线下面积(area under the curve, AUC)最高(AUC = 0.9575)。在CSF中,一个未注释特征(Analyte 369)经FDR校正后在DFA阳性犬中丰度降低。血清Analyte 634与CSF Analyte 369的保留时间和质谱相同,可能为同一化合物。血清中富集p值最低的通路主要由甘油酸单独映射,且没有CSF通路在FDR校正后保持显著。在名义(nominal)水平上,吲哚乳酸(indolelactic acid)在DFA阳性犬的两种基质中均升高。研究结论:经FDR校正后,4个血清特征和1个CSF特征在表面健康DFA阳性犬与DFA阴性犬之间表现出差异。这些均为发现阶段的候选特征,在提出任何诊断学主张之前,需进行结构确证、靶向定量以及在独立队列中的验证。 **一、研究背景与问题提出** 索取CAR-T 细胞免疫疗法最新资料 领 取 狂犬病是一种被忽视的人兽共患疾病,在非洲和亚洲,家犬约占人类狂犬病病例的95%,每年导致全球约60000例相关死亡。由于狂犬病病毒属(Lyssavirus)病原能够逃避免疫系统攻击,生前诊断十分困难,感染通常只能在临床体征出现后才被确诊,而此时疾病已近乎必然致死。暴露后预防(post-exposure prophylaxis, PEP)联合狂犬病免疫球蛋白(rabies immunoglobulin, RIG)和疫苗是推荐的干预措施,但在尼日利亚农村,其高昂成本与有限可及性常使受害者转而依赖传统疗法。区分狂犬病犬与正常犬的咬伤本可减少不必要的PEP使用,但病毒的病理生理学使这一区分变得复杂。代谢组学作为新兴的"组学"技术已在传染病临床诊断中展现出前景,脑脊液(cerebrospinal fluid, CSF)和血清生物流体分析在神经系统疾病诊断中价值突出。然而,狂犬病相关代谢组学研究有限,且多针对实验室动物或已死亡犬只;对表面健康但病毒阳性犬只的代谢改变仍缺乏表征。为此,研究人员利用尼日利亚犬肉消费的习俗,在屠宰前从无明显临床体征的犬只采集样本,以DFA试验检测脑组织抗原为基础,比较DFA阳性犬与阴性犬的血清和CSF代谢谱差异,旨在鉴定差异丰度代谢物,为生前诊断提供初步线索。 **二、关键技术方法(约250字)** 样本队列来源于尼日利亚北中部高原州(Plateau State)的Dawaki犬市场和东南埃努古州(Enugu State)的Orie-Orba犬市场,共231只拟屠宰的Basenji犬,按性别、采样地点和年龄类别匹配选取20只阳性犬与20只阴性犬。脑组织经DFA试验(FITC标记单抗)检测病毒抗原;血清与CSF采用经验证流程提取代谢物(3-苯基丁酸内标、蛋白沉淀、甲氧胺肟化和含1% TMCS的BSTFA衍生化);采用GC×GC-TOFMS分析,以NIST 20谱库(相似度≥80%)进行注释;数据经内标归一化、EigenMS批次效应校正、对数转换与Pareto标度,利用MetaboAnalyst 6.0完成PCA、PLS-DA、Mann-Whitney U检验、FDR校正、ROC分析及基于HMDB与犬(Canis lupus familiaris)KEGG通路的通路富集分析。 **三、研究结果** 研究人群特征:231只犬中20只(8.7%)脑组织DFA阳性,所有犬均为Basenji犬,排除了具有流涎、不协调、攻击性等临床体征的个体,直肠温度在正常生理范围内。 GC×GC-TOFMS分析结果:FAMES标准品重复进样显示分析期间仪器稳定性可接受,系统空白与提取空白显示仪器及试剂污染极小。 数据概览:血清数据集经清理后保留868种化合物(含415种未注释),CSF数据集保留300种化合物;两组缺失率无显著差异;PCA未显示任一组间分离,血清前两个主成分解释30.9%方差,CSF解释32.5%;PLS-DA同样未显示分离,交叉验证预测性能差(血清准确率0.65、Q2=0.06、置换检验p=0.991;CSF Q2=-0.13),提示组间仅存在细微代谢改变。 血清差异代谢物:火山图分析鉴定出60个名义差异化合物,FDR校正后4个显著,Analyte 634(FC=6.57)、甘油酸(FC=2.64)和Analyte 706(FC=6.43)在阳性犬中降低,苯甲酸(FC=0.31)升高;ROC分析显示四者发现集AUC均高于0.8425,其中Analyte 634最高(AUC=0.9575,95% CI:0.889–1.000)。 CSF差异代谢物:12个名义差异化合物中,仅Analyte 369(FC=7.15,raw p=1.1×10-5,FDR=0.003)经FDR校正后显著且丰度降低;其AUC为0.9240(95% CI:0.801–1.000)。 通路分析:血清有10条通路FDR<0.05,但均由1–2个化合物单独映射(甘油酸),相对介数中心性通路影响值至多为0.097;CSF无通路在FDR校正后保持显著。 **四、讨论与结论** 研究人员指出,全局代谢谱无显著分离可能反映表面健康犬只系统代谢扰动细微、自由放养且营养状况各异的犬只组内生物学变异较大,或二者兼有。甘油酸降低可能与饮食、果糖或丝氨酸代谢或营养状态差异有关;苯甲酸升高可能源于饮食暴露(含苯甲酸钠防腐剂的食物),或狂犬病诱导的甘氨酸结合途径改变导致清除受损;血清Analyte 634与CSF Analyte 369的保留时间(1487 s和2.180 s)及质谱碎片(m z 235基峰等)高度相似,可能为同一化合物。 研究结论(翻译):本研究提供了关于表面健康DFA阳性及阴性Basenji犬血清和CSF代谢组中早期代谢改变的新见解。尽管DFA阳性组与阴性组之间的多变量差异极小,但单变量分析提示两个基质中均存在潜在的代谢扰动。经FDR校正后,4个血清特征(Analyte 634、甘油酸和Analyte 706在DFA阳性犬中降低,苯甲酸升高)和1个CSF特征(Analyte 369降低)保持显著。血清Analyte 634与CSF Analyte 369可能为同一化合物。由于血清通路结果主要依赖甘油酸、且没有CSF通路在FDR校正后显著,数据未确立任何代谢通路发生改变。上述发现为发现阶段候选特征,在考虑诊断应用之前,需经结构确证、靶向定量以及在独立且理想情况下纵向采样的队列中验证。 **一、研究背景与问题提出**狂犬病是一种被忽视的人兽共患疾病,在非洲和亚洲,家犬约占人类狂犬病病例的95%,每年导致全球约60000例相关死亡。由于狂犬病病毒属(Lyssavirus)病原能够逃避免疫系统攻击,生前诊断十分困难,感染通常只能在临床体征出现后才被确诊,而此时疾病已近乎必然致死。暴露后预防(post-exposure prophylaxis, State)的Dawaki犬市场和东南埃努古州(Enugu State)的Orie-Orba犬市场,共231只拟屠宰的Basenji犬,按性别、采样地点和年龄类别匹配选取20只阳性犬与20只阴性犬。脑组织经DFA试验(FITC标记单抗)检测病毒抗原;血清与CSF采用经验证流程提取代谢物(3-苯基丁酸内标、蛋白沉淀、甲氧胺肟化和含1% TMCS的BSTFA衍生化);采用GC×GC-TOFMS分析,以NIST 20谱库(相似度≥80%)进行注释;数据经内标归一化、EigenMS批次效应校正、对数转换与Pareto标度,利用MetaboAnalyst 6.0完成PCA、PLS-DA、Mann-Whitney U检验、FDR校正、ROC分析及基于HMDB与犬(Canis lupus familiaris)KEGG通路的通路富集分析。**三、研究结果**研究人群特征:231只犬中20只(8.7%)脑组织DFA阳性,所有犬均为Basenji犬,排除了具有流涎、不协调、攻击性等临床体征的个体,直肠温度在正常生理范围内。GC×GC-TOFMS分析结果:FAMES标准品重复进样显示分析期间仪器稳定性可接受,系统空白与提取空白显示仪器及试剂污染极小。数据概览:血清数据集经清理后保留868种化合物(含415种未注释),CSF数据集保留300种化合物;两组缺失率无显著差异;PCA未显示任一组间分离,血清前两个主成分解释30.9%方差,CSF解释32.5%;PLS-DA同样未显示分离,交叉验证预测性能差(血清准确率0.65、Q=0.06、置换检验p=0.991;CSF Original title (Chinese)
最新狂犬病毒分类和简要说明(2026年更新·修订版)
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly identify the location or affected population. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the scale, impact, or risk in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly report the response status or unresolved information.
Original source text (Chinese)
前言: 本文系对笔者 2019 年 5 月 6 日同名旧文的全面 更新与 修订 (参见: 最新狂犬病毒分类和简要说明 https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/eaGPyryxOy1Fbll0rjOy_A ) 。 主要依据 国际病毒分类委员会( ICTV ) 现行分类框架、 ICTV 现行 MSL41 ( 2025–2026 分类学 Release )、《 Rabies 》第 5 版第 2 章《分类学》,以及各物种人病例的原始病例报告与分子分型文献。核查截至 2026 年 10 月。 一、狂犬病毒属( Lyssavirus )在病毒分类学中的位置 丽沙病毒属 隶属于: 单分子负链 RNA 病毒目 ( Mononegavirales ) → 弹状病毒科 ( Rhabdoviridae ) → 弹状病毒 α 亚科 ( Alpharhabdovirinae ) → 丽沙病毒属 ( Lyssavirus , 又译狂犬病毒属) 。 注 : “ Lyssavirus ”一词源自古希腊语 λύσσα ( lyssa ,意为疯狂、狂犬病)。 关键名词的定义与区分: 英文术语 推荐中文译法 含义 Rabies virus / RABV 狂犬病毒 特指 基因 1型 ,即经典狂犬病毒,引起全球绝大多数病例。 Lyssavirus 丽沙病毒属 整个属的统称(含 RABV+所有狂犬病相关病毒)。 rabies-related lyssaviruses 狂犬病相关丽沙病毒 丽沙病毒属中除 RABV以外的所有成员。 重要提示 : 在日常口语中 “ 狂犬病毒 ” 常被泛化指代整个属,但在涉及非 RABV 成员的分类、诊断与疫苗保护范围时,应当作严格区分: “ 狂犬病毒( RABV ) = 经典狂犬病毒(基因 1 型) ” , “ 丽沙病毒( Lyssavirus ) = 整个丽沙病毒属 ” 。这是两个不同层次的概念。 二、属级特征简述 丽沙病毒颗粒为子弹形(约 60–110 nm × 130–250 nm ),内含一条负链单股 RNA 基因组( ≈11.9–12.3 kb )。 · 基因组排列 : 3′N-P-M-G-L 5′ ,仅编码 5 个结构蛋白(核蛋白 N 、磷蛋白 P 、基质蛋白 M 、糖蛋白 G 、大聚合酶 L )。 · 致病机理 :病毒严格嗜神经,经咬伤 / 黏膜污染侵入 → 局部复制 → 逆行轴突运输至中枢神经系统( CNS ) → 急性进行性脑炎。 · 致死率 :出现症状后几乎 100% 致死(尚无可靠治愈手段)。 诊断与疫苗保护的关键事实: 1. 诊断 :所有已知丽沙病毒的 N 蛋白在抗原上有跨属的交叉反应,因此标准 直接荧光抗体( dFA ) 检测可检出全属成员。 2. 疫苗保护 :中和抗体主要针对 G 蛋白。 G 蛋白在 遗传谱系( phylogroup ) 之间保守度较低( <65% ),这解释了为什么现行 RABV 疫苗对部分非 RABV 成员的防护存在不确定性。 三、病毒物种划分标准( ICTV现行准则) ICTV 对丽沙病毒属内不同物种的划分阈值大体为: · N 基因全长核苷酸同一性 :< 78–80% 判定为不同物种( ≥80% 才归同一物种)。 · 串联编码区 : N+P+M+G+L 阈值约 80% 核苷酸( nt )同一性。 · 系统发育 :新病毒不应只是某已定种内的姐妹分支,而应位于多个已定种构成的更高阶簇的基部位置。 · 血清学与生态位 :病毒中和试验可区分,且宿主种类、地理分布有明显区别。 四、当前全属成员一览 ( 19个ICTV正式物种+分类待定病毒) 按 ICTV 现行 MSL41 ,丽沙病毒属共有 19 个正式物种 。下表沿用经典 系统发生群 框架,物种名采用 2021 年起强制的双名法(斜体)。 " 致人死亡记录 " 列已按本次文献校勘重填, 统计口径为 " 经病原学确证的人致死病例累计 " ,与 RABV 的年度模型估计不同量纲,不可横向相加。 1. 系统发生群I(与RABV抗原/遗传关系较近,现行疫苗总体有效) # ICTV 物种名 常用病毒名 缩写 主要宿主/分布 确证人致死病例 † 1 Lyssavirus rabies 狂犬病病毒(经典) RABV 全球(除澳、南极);犬/食肉目为主 见下方专条 ‡ 2 Lyssavirus australis 澳大利亚蝙蝠丽沙病毒 ABLV 澳大利亚狐蝠、食虫蝠 4 ( 1996 、 1998 、 2013 、 2025 ),均死亡 3 Lyssavirus duvenhage 杜文黑基丽沙病毒 DUVV 非洲(南非、肯尼亚等),裂颜蝠 3 ( 1970 南非、 2006 南非、 2008 肯尼亚暴露/ 科塔拉赫蒂蝙蝠丽沙病毒 KBLV 芬兰,勃兰特蝙蝠 0† 13 Lyssavirus phala 帕拉(法拉)蝙蝠丽沙病毒 PBLV 南非 Phalaborwa ,施利芬蝠 Nycticeinops schlieffeni , 2021 0† 2. 系统发生群 II(遗传关系较远,现行疫苗交叉保护弱) # ICTV 物种名 常用病毒名 缩写 主要宿主/分布 确证人致死病例 † 14 Lyssavirus lagos 拉各斯蝙蝠丽沙病毒 LBV 非洲,食果蝠 0† 15 Lyssavirus mokola 莫科拉丽沙病毒 MOKV 非洲,麝鼩等陆生小型哺乳动物 1 ( 1971 尼日利亚,脑组织分离 MOKV 的致死性脑炎) 16 Lyssavirus shimoni 希莫尼蝙蝠丽沙病毒 SHIBV 肯尼亚,叶鼻蝠 0† 3. 系统发生群 III (独立分支,内部交叉中和有限) # ICTV 物种名 常用病毒名 缩写 主要宿主/分布 确证人致死病例 † 17 Lyssavirus caucasicus 西高加索蝙蝠丽沙病毒 WCBV 高加索/东欧,普通长翼蝠 0† 18 Lyssavirus ikoma 伊科马丽沙病毒 IKOV 坦桑尼亚,非洲灵猫(疑为溢出感染) 0† 19 Lyssavirus lleida 莱里达蝙蝠丽沙病毒 LLEBV 西班牙/法国,长翼蝠 0† 4. 已报道、分类地位待定的病毒(非ICTV正式物种) 病毒 缩写 发现时间/地点/宿主 系统发生群 MSL41 状态 马特洛(马特卢)蝙蝠丽沙病毒 Matlo bat lyssavirus MBLV 2015–2016 ,南非,纳塔尔长指蝠 Miniopterus natalensis III ,与 WCBV 近缘 未定种 。 2023 年分类学评估认为其与 WCBV 抗原高度相似, "likely related to WCBV" ,可能只是 L. caucasicus 的一个谱系; CDC EID 亦指出 " 若不另立新种,则视为 WCBV 的独立谱系 " 台湾蝙蝠丽沙病毒 2 型 Taiwan bat lyssavirus 2 TWBLV2 中国台湾,绒山蝠 Nyctalus plancyi velutinus ; GenBank ON437589 I 未分类( ICTV 属报告列为 "Related, unclassified viruses" ) 迪瓦查蝙蝠丽沙病毒 Divača bat ly ssavirus DLBV / DBLV 斯洛文尼亚,长指蝠 Myotis capaccinii ; GenBank OQ428158 , Černe 等 2023 报道 I ,与 KBLV 最近( N 基因 87.20% ) 未分类 ● 0 † :截至 2026 年 10 月核查日,无可靠文献报告 " 人是病例主体+该病毒物种经病原学确证+结局为死亡 " 三者齐备的病例。动物宿主分离、无症状人群序列检出、血清学阳性均 不计入 。无报告不等于生物学上不可能。 ● ‡ RABV 专条 :不能填 " 累计致死病例数 " ,应写为 模型估计 :「据 WHO 2021 年全球健康估计( GHE ),狂犬病归因死亡约 44,203 人 / 年 ;历史模型曾给出约 55,000 ( WHO 2005 )与 59,000 ( Hampson et al., PLoS NTD 2015 ,犬源性地方性狂犬病)」。初版 "≈5.9 万例 / 年 " 只是 2015 年模型的中心估计,且带较宽不确定区间,不是死亡证明逐例统计,更不能写成 " 全属每年死亡 5.9 万例 " 。 74,000 / 14,000 / 40,111 等数字本次未取得可核的一手出处,不进入正式表。 ● 物种数口径 :文献报道过的病毒 ≠ ICTV 已认定物种。若后续 MSL 版本将 MBLV 、 TWBLV2 或 DLBV 正式定种,物种总数会再变动,请以最新 Release 为准。 五、几个需要纠正或强调的要点(相对 2019年旧文) 1. “18 种 → 19 种(正式种) ” 的变化 : 随着系统发育与全基因组数据的累积, ICTV 已将本属定型为 19 个正式物种。原先一些 “ 未定 ” 的候选株被提升为种,如 L. phala 。 2. 科的亚科重组 : 丽沙病毒属现归 弹状病毒 α 亚科 ( Alpharhabdovirinae ),而非像早期文献那样直接写在 Rhabdoviridae 下不分亚科。 3. 疫苗保护范围的 “ 硬边界 ” : 现行人用 / 兽用疫苗株全部源自 RABV (谱系 I ),可产生对谱系 I 全组的有效中和保护;但对谱系 II 和谱系 III 的交叉中和明显减弱。 4. 蝙蝠 = 全属的核心宿主库 : 19 个物种中,除 RABV (多宿主)和 MOKV (鼩鼱 / 陆生小型动物)外,其余至少 17 种的天然贮存宿主均为蝙蝠。 5. 中国存在哪几种狂犬病毒? o 中国大陆 :已确证流行的是 RABV (基因 1 型) ;与人的致死病例相关的非 RABV 成员仅有 IRKV (伊尔库特, 1 例,未经实验室诊断确认) 。 o 中国台湾 :记录有 TWBLV (台湾蝙蝠丽沙病毒)。 o 风险提示 :至今在中国大陆 和台湾都 未检测到谱系 II 或谱系 III 成员,因此对谱系 II/III 的过度恐慌缺乏流行病学基础。 六、相关名词对照表 术语 英 /拉丁 说明 丽沙病毒属 Lyssavirus 属级分类 狂犬病毒 Rabies virus / RABV 基因 1型,全世界99%+病例的病原 狂犬病相关丽沙病毒 rabies-related lyssaviruses 属内除 RABV外的成员 储存宿主 reservoir host 可长期维持病毒传播流行的物种(几乎全为蝙蝠或食肉目) 溢出感染 spillover infection 病毒跨种感染非贮存宿主,通常为 “死胡同”感染 遗传谱系 phylogroup 基于 N/G/L基因树和血清学的分组框架 七、 “恐狂”读者最应关注的结论 1. 蝙蝠确实是绝大多数丽沙病毒的自然贮存库 , 但 19 个正式物种里,真正造成大量人死亡的仍然只有 RABV ;其余非 RABV 成员要么极罕见、要么地理上基本不在我国。 2. 现行人 / 兽疫苗来自 RABV 株 → 对遗传谱系 I ( PG I ,含 IRKV/EBLV/ABLV 等毒种)保护有效。 3. 风险管理的重心:永远不是 “ 怕未知丽沙病毒 ” ,而是冲洗伤口 15 分钟 + 按规范完成 PEP (疫苗 ± 免疫球蛋白) 。 主要参考文献: 1. ICTV (国际病毒分类委员会) – Rhabdoviridae/ Lyssavirus genus profile (current release). 2. WHO Expert Consultation on Rabies, Third report, WHO Technical Report Series No. 1012, Geneva: WHO; 2018. 3. Troupin JL et al. The spread and evolution of rabies virus: conquering new frontiers. Nat Rev Microbiol. 2018;16:241–255. 免责: 本文属科普综述性质,不构成临床诊断或处置建议;确实发生暴露后请一律遵从当地 CDC/ 卫生部门规范的伤口处理+疫苗 ± 免疫球蛋白流程。 本公众号相关文章的链接 最新狂犬病毒分类和简要说明(2026年更新版) 026-06-23 权威的大型学术专著《狂犬病(Rabies)》最新版已面世 2020-06-30 一部跨越二十三年的"狂犬病百科全书" 2026-10-03 大型学术专著《狂犬病》2025年最新版的更新 2026-10-19 当代最著名狂犬病专家为学术专著《狂犬病》写的序言 2026-10-21 狂犬病毒的分类学(一) 2026-10-25 狂犬病毒的分类学(二) 2026-10-27 狂犬病毒的分类学(三) 2026-10-29 狂犬病毒的分类学(四) 2026-10-31 狂犬病毒的分类学(五) 2026-11-02 狂犬病毒的分类学(六) 2026-11-04 狂犬病毒的分类学(七) 2026-11-06

狂犬病的诊断

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

近日,武汉市金银潭医院重症医学科连续收治两例狂犬病患者。一名男子手指被未接种疫苗的小奶狗抓伤,未冲洗伤口,后续出现怕风怕水症状确诊;另一名男子被幼犬舔到手上伤口,仅简单酒精消毒,未做暴露处置,发病后入院。目前两人病情加重,转入ICU救治。 医生介绍,狂犬病发病致死率接近100%,很多人误以为幼犬不带毒,实则小奶狗也可能携带病毒。只要皮肤屏障破损,就属于II级暴露,需立刻冲洗伤口并接种狂犬疫苗。 提醒 ...

Original title (Chinese)
一男子被小奶狗抓伤,另一男子被狗舔到伤口,2人确诊狂犬病住进ICU!医生:不要以为幼犬不带毒

近日,武汉市金银潭医院重症医学科连续收治两例狂犬病患者。一名男子手指被未接种疫苗的小奶狗抓伤,未冲洗伤口,后续出现怕风怕水症状确诊;另一名男子被幼犬舔到手上伤口,仅简单酒精消毒,未做暴露处置,发病后入院。目前两人病情加重,转入ICU救治。 医生介绍,狂犬病发病致死率接近100%,很多人误以为幼犬不带毒,实则小奶狗也可能携带病毒。只要皮肤屏障破损,就属于II级暴露,需立刻冲洗伤口并接种狂犬疫苗。 提醒 ...
